# TravelAgencyWeb / Hearth Core — Complete System Master Audit

> Read-only architecture audit of the entire codebase. Generated by reading the actual source (65 backend route modules, 97 Prisma models, 106 frontend pages, all libs/services/middleware). No code was modified.
> Legend: `✓` included · `✗` locked · `🔒` hidden/opt-in · `⭐` premium · `-1` = unlimited · `0` = none.

---

# 1. SYSTEM OVERVIEW

| Aspect | Detail |
|---|---|
| **System Name** | **TravelAgencyWeb** (public brand, `travelagencyweb.com`) / **Hearth Core** (internal app codename). Workbook/JWT creator string: `TravelAgencyWeb`. |
| **Product type** | Multi-tenant B2B SaaS ERP/CRM for travel agencies (Bangladesh-first: BDT `৳`, English + Bengali bilingual UI). |
| **Architecture** | Monorepo: React SPA front-end + Express REST API back-end + PostgreSQL. Row-level multi-tenancy. One VPS hosts many tenant apps under PM2; the app also white-labels tenant public websites via host/subdomain/custom-domain resolution. |
| **Frontend** | React 18 + Vite 5 + TypeScript; TailwindCSS + shadcn/ui (Radix) components; React Router v6; react-i18next (`en`/`bn`); Recharts for charts; custom `fetch` API client (`src/lib/api.ts`) — no data-fetching library. State via React Context (Auth, Website) + hooks. 106 pages (70 tenant-app, 23 super-admin, 7 marketing, 6 tenant-site). 14 pages lazy-loaded. |
| **Backend** | Node.js + Express 4; Prisma 5 ORM. ~65 route modules mounted under `/api/*`. 16 service modules, 7 middleware, 14 lib modules. Layered: routes → middleware (auth/RBAC/plan gates) → prisma. Transactional integrity via `prisma.$transaction` (wallet, agent ledger, payments). |
| **Database** | PostgreSQL 16, **97 models** (`backend/prisma/schema.prisma`). Every business model is tenant-scoped (`tenantId` + cascade delete). Financial denormalization pattern (`totalAmount/paidAmount/dueAmount`) across Booking/Invoice/VendorBill/HajjPilgrim. Most business dates stored as `String`; system timestamps as `DateTime`. |
| **Authentication** | JWT (7-day, `{userId,tenantId,role}`), bcrypt password hashing. Signup requires **WhatsApp OTP** verification; **email verification** (24h token); password reset (1h token). **TOTP 2FA** (speakeasy/Google Authenticator) for super-admin. Account approval workflow (`pending`/`active`/`rejected`). Separate **PlatformStaff** auth for the vendor's internal team. |
| **Multi-Tenant** | Tenant is the aggregate root (owns 60+ child collections). Isolation enforced server-side: every query filtered by `req.tenantId` (from JWT, re-read fresh each request). Feature-gating flags live on Tenant (`enabledModules`, `enabledServiceTypes`, `enabledSubcategories`, `subscriptionPlan`). Custom domains via `TenantDomain` (DNS/SSL/verification state). |
| **Subscription** | 4 active plans — **Basic / Pro / Business / Enterprise(Unlimited)** (+ deprecated `free` → Basic). Server-side enforcement: app-level 402 gate for expired/suspended tenants, per-resource count limits (`checkPlanLimit`), feature-flag gates (`requireFeature`), WhatsApp monthly quota. Billing monthly/yearly (yearly = 10× monthly). Manual + bKash + SSLCommerz payment. |
| **Storage** | File uploads via `multer` (`middleware/upload.js`); package/booking/client documents stored as URLs. Per-plan storage quota advertised (500 MB → unlimited) but **not** server-enforced. |
| **API** | REST, JSON, `/api/*`. Auth via `Authorization: Bearer <jwt>`. RBAC via `requirePermission(module, action)` / `requireRole(...)`. Rate-limited auth. Public sub-API for marketing/portal/website; separate `/api/admin/*` super-admin API; `/api/portal/*` customer portal; `/api/cron/*` for scheduled jobs. |
| **Deployment** | VPS (Linux) + **PM2** process manager (`scripts/vps-pm2-deploy.sh`), nginx reverse proxy + static serving, `prisma migrate deploy` on release. Multiple independent tenant/product apps co-hosted (each its own PM2 process + Postgres DB). Health check at `/api/health`. |
| **Cross-cutting** | Audit logging (`AuditLog`), notification engine (in-app + email + SMS + WhatsApp + Telegram), i18n bilingual, dark-mode-ready CSS (no toggle), soft plan-gating (locked "Upgrade" rows in sidebar), Excel/CSV export. |

# 2. MASTER ORGANOGRAM (top level)

```
TravelAgencyWeb (Multi-Tenant Travel Agency SaaS)
│
├── PUBLIC / MARKETING SITE (unauthenticated)
│   ├── Landing (Home, Features, Pricing, Demo, FAQ, Privacy, Terms, Contact-Us)
│   ├── Auth (Login, Register [WhatsApp-OTP], Forgot/Reset Password, Verify Email)
│   └── Tenant Public Website  /site/*  (Home, About, Packages, Pricing, Blog, Contact)
│
├── TENANT APP (authenticated agency workspace)
│   ├── 1. Overview  → Dashboard
│   ├── 2. CRM       → Workspace hub · Clients · Corporate Travel · Agents · Vendors
│   │                  (+ deep: Leads · Follow-ups · Complaints · Campaigns · CRM Analytics · CRM Settings · Support)
│   ├── 3. Sales & Bookings → Quotations · Bookings · Visa Stock · Service Catalog · Ticket Transactions · Flight Reminders
│   ├── 4. Tour & Group Travel → Group Tours · MICE Events · Travel Approvals · Visa Tracker
│   ├── 5. Operations → Documents · Tasks · Service Operations · Hajj/Umrah Ops · BD Ops (Study/Manpower)
│   │                   (+ deep: Inventory [hotel/transport contracts] · Recruitment [jobs])
│   ├── 6. Finance & Accounts → Invoices · Payments · Expenses · Commissions · Accounts Ledger · Reports
│   │                   (+ deep: Financial Statements · Sales Analytics · Finance Reminders · Tax settings)
│   ├── 7. HR & Payroll → Team · HRM (attendance/leave) · Roles · Activity Log · Payroll
│   ├── 8. Marketing & Loyalty → Loyalty · Referrals · (Campaigns)
│   ├── 9. Website & CMS → Website Home · Theme Builder · Blog · Publish/Domain · SEO
│   └── 10. Administration → Notifications · Settings · Organization · Subscription · User Guide
│
├── CUSTOMER PORTAL  /portal/*  (client self-service: bookings, invoices, documents)
│
└── PLATFORM ADMIN  /admin/*  (super-admin, vendor-side)
    ├── Overview (Admin Dashboard)
    ├── Agencies (Tenants) · Tenant Details · Pending Signups
    ├── Plans · Subscriptions · Payment Requests · Coupons · Feature Control
    ├── Roles & Permissions · Platform Staff · Two-Factor
    ├── Master Data · Domains
    ├── Communications (SMS/WhatsApp Templates + Logs)
    ├── Audit Log · Reports
    └── Landing Page CMS · Settings
```

# 4. SUB-MODULES (expanded)

### Dashboard
Role-aware KPIs (Active Leads, Quotations Sent, Confirmed Bookings, Upcoming Departures, Open Tickets, Passports Expiring) + finance-only widgets (Sales This Month, Overdue Invoices, Vendor Dues, Total Revenue, Recent Payments) · Plan usage bars · Onboarding checklist · Inquiry follow-up widget · Recent bookings · Top destinations · Bookings by type · Quick actions hub · client-side fallback recompute.

### Clients (Customers)
Add/List/Profile · KYC (passport, NID, nationality, DOB) · **Wallet ledger** (credit/debit, running balance, atomic) · Family members · Documents · Bookings / Invoices / Payments history · Tags & custom fields · Credit limit · Corporate summary · Birthday reminders · Passport-expiry alerts.

### Agents / Sub-Agents
Add/List/Profile · Commission profile (rate, linked user) · **Agent ledger** (deposit/payment, atomic running balance) · Per-booking commission (accrue + mark paid) · Agent performance report.

### Vendors / Suppliers
Add/List/Detail · Categories · Vendor bills (payables: total/paid/due/status) · Bill payments (+ synced expense transaction) · Vendor notes/log · Payable report.

### Leads / CRM
Lead capture · Pipeline status (new→contacted→qualified→quoted→won/lost) · Scoring · Assignment · Next follow-up · Activities/timeline · Convert to client/quotation · Sources · Complaints (ratings) · Campaigns (bulk SMS/WhatsApp/Email, gated) · CRM Analytics (funnel, source ROI, exec performance) · CRM Settings.

### Quotations
Builder (line items + itinerary) · Versioning (snapshots) · Discount/tax/grand-total · Valid-until · Terms · Print view (public) · Convert to Booking (carries profit/amounts).

### Bookings
Core sales order (amount/cost/profit/paid/due) · Types & service details (JSON) · Segments (per-service cost/price) · Travelers (passport) · Checklist · Timeline · Documents · Ops status · Follow-up (inquiry) · Agent commission · Invoices · Auto-links to quotation/package (snapshots).

### Visa Stock
Inventory of pre-bought visa quotas · buy/sell price → profit · status (available→processing→sold→completed) · sell (buyer) · stats (stock value, sold profit).

### Air Ticketing
Ticket transactions · Refunds · Voids · Reissues (each records financial adjustment) · BSP uploads/records · Flight departure reminders (auto + manual).

### Hajj / Umrah
Packages (hotels, inclusions, capacity, pricing) · Groups (leader, flight/transport schedule) · Pilgrims (KYC, mahram, rooming, visa status, departure status, payment) · Pilgrim payments/installments.

### Group Tours / MICE
Group tours + bookings (pax/seats) · MICE events + line items (venue/catering costs) · Travel policy + approval requests (corporate).

### Finance & Accounts
Invoices (tax breakdown, installments, refunds, audit trail) · Payments (allocation, methods, proof) · Expenses (approval, vendor/account link) · Accounts (cash/bank balances) · Transactions (general ledger) · Tax Rules · Commissions · Reports (6 CSV-exportable) · Financial Statements (P&L, Balance Sheet, Cash Flow, Tax) · Sales Analytics · Finance Reminders (overdue invoices/installments + send).

### HR & Payroll
Team invite/roles · Staff profiles · Attendance (daily) · Leave requests (approval) · Roles & permission matrix (view/assign) · Activity log · Salary structures · Payroll runs → payslips.

### Website & CMS
Theme builder (colors/layout) · Pages · Blog posts (SEO meta) · SEO settings · Publish + custom domain (DNS/SSL verification) · Public tenant site render.

### Administration
Notifications center · Settings (company, users, modules, service types, taxes, comms, integrations, domains) · Organization profile · Subscription/billing + renewal · User guide.

### Platform Admin (super-admin)
Tenant management · Plan management · Subscription approval workflow · Payment request review · Coupons · Feature control (per-tenant overrides) · Platform staff ACL · Master data · Domains · 2FA · Audit log · Admin reports · Landing CMS · Pending signups · Demo/contact submissions.

# 5. PAGE LIST (every route, component, guard, sidebar/hidden)

# Frontend Pages & Routes

All routing is defined in `src/App.tsx` (single `<Routes>` block, lines 184–322). There is one router (`BrowserRouter`); the same `AppContent` is reused if already inside a router context (`App`, lines 334–342).

## Route Guards / Access Wrappers

Guards are helper components defined at the top of `src/App.tsx` (lines 134–140):

| Wrapper | Definition | Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `<P>` | `<ProtectedRoute>` | Requires logged-in `user`; else redirects to `/login`. Wraps children in `<SubscriptionGate>`. (`src/components/ProtectedRoute.tsx`) |
| `<PM module=…>` | `<ProtectedRoute><PermissionRoute module=…>` | Protected **and** RBAC module check. If `!canAccess(module)` redirects to `/dashboard`. (`src/components/PermissionRoute.tsx`) |
| `<A>` | `<AdminRoute>` | Requires `role === "super_admin"` (via `mapLegacyRole`); no user → `/login`, non-admin → `/dashboard`. (`src/components/AdminRoute.tsx`) |
| (none) | public | No auth check; renders directly. |

Additional in-route gates: `<HajjModuleGate>`, `<BdModuleGate>` (opt-in module gating), `<SubscriptionGate>` (inside every `ProtectedRoute`). Some `PM` routes also add `<Suspense>` for lazy-loaded pages.

**Sidebar source of truth:** `src/config/navigation.ts` (`getNavigationGroups`) lists all tenant-app sidebar URLs; `src/components/AdminSidebar.tsx` lists super-admin sidebar URLs. A route is marked "Sidebar" below only if its URL appears in one of those two files; everything else is Hidden/deep-link (reached via buttons, row clicks, redirects, or direct URL).

| `/referrals` | `Referrals` | `<PM module="clients">` + Suspense | **Sidebar** (Marketing & Loyalty → Referrals, minPlan enterprise) | **lazy** | |
| `/sales-analytics` | `SalesAnalytics` | `<PM module="reports">` + Suspense | Hidden | **lazy** | deep-link |
| `/group-tours` | `GroupTours` | `<PM module="bookings">` + Suspense | **Sidebar** (Tour & Group Travel → Group Tours, minPlan basic, service-type gated) | **lazy** | |
| `/mice` | `MiceEvents` | `<PM module="bookings">` + Suspense | **Sidebar** (Tour & Group Travel → MICE, minPlan basic, service-type gated) | **lazy** | |
| `/travel-approvals` | `TravelApprovals` | `<PM module="bookings">` + Suspense | **Sidebar** (Tour & Group Travel → Travel Approvals, minPlan basic, service-type gated) | **lazy** | |
| `/visa-tracker` | `VisaTracker` | `<PM module="bookings">` + Suspense | **Sidebar** (Tour & Group Travel → Visa Tracker, minPlan basic, service-type gated) | **lazy** | |
| `/inventory` | `Inventory` | `<PM module="bookings">` + Suspense | Hidden | **lazy** | deep-link (not in nav config) |
| `/recruitment` | `Recruitment` | `<PM module="team">` + Suspense | Hidden | **lazy** | deep-link |
| `/ticket-transactions` | `TicketTransactions` | `<PM module="bookings">` + Suspense | **Sidebar** (Sales & Bookings → Ticket Transactions, service-type `air_ticket`) | **lazy** | |
| `/flight-reminders` | `FlightReminders` | `<PM module="bookings">` + Suspense | **Sidebar** (Sales & Bookings → Flight Reminders, service-type `air_ticket`) | **lazy** | |
| `/user-guide` | `UserGuide` | `<P>` | **Sidebar** (Administration → User Guide) | eager | |

## Super-Admin Routes (`/admin/*`)

All guarded by `<A>` (`AdminRoute`, requires `super_admin`). Sidebar source: `src/components/AdminSidebar.tsx`. All are sidebar-linked **except** `/admin/tenants/:tenantId` (deep-link detail). All eager-loaded.

| URL Path | Component | Guard | Sidebar? | Load |

| `/admin` | `admin/AdminDashboard` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Overview) | eager |
| `/admin/landing-cms` | `admin/AdminLandingCMS` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Landing Page CMS) | eager |
| `/admin/tenants` | `admin/AdminTenants` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Agencies) | eager |
| `/admin/tenants/:tenantId` | `admin/AdminTenantDetails` | `<A>` | Hidden (detail) | eager |
| `/admin/master-data` | `admin/AdminMasterData` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Master Data) | eager |
| `/admin/pending-users` | `admin/AdminPendingUsers` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Pending Signups) | eager |
| `/admin/domains` | `admin/AdminDomains` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Domains) | eager |
| `/admin/payments` | `admin/AdminPayments` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Payment Requests) | eager |
| `/admin/plans` | `admin/AdminPlans` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Manage Plans) | eager |
| `/admin/subscriptions` | `admin/AdminSubscriptions` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Subscriptions) | eager |
| `/admin/features` | `admin/AdminFeatures` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Feature Control) | eager |
| `/admin/roles` | `admin/AdminRoles` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Roles & Permissions) | eager |
| `/admin/sms-templates` | `admin/AdminSmsTemplates` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (SMS Templates) | eager |
| `/admin/whatsapp-templates` | `admin/AdminWhatsAppTemplates` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (WhatsApp Templates) | eager |
| `/admin/coupons` | `admin/AdminCoupons` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Coupons) | eager |
| `/admin/sms-logs` | `admin/AdminSmsLogs` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (SMS Logs) | eager |
| `/admin/whatsapp-logs` | `admin/AdminWhatsAppLogs` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (WhatsApp Logs) | eager |
| `/admin/audit-log` | `admin/AdminAuditLog` | `<A>` | **Sidebar** (Audit Log) | eager |

| URL Path | Component | Guard | Sidebar? | Load |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `*` | `NotFound` | public | Hidden | eager |

## Notes & Observations

- **Lazy-loaded pages (14 total)**, all wrapped in `<Suspense>` with a `Skeleton` fallback: `SettingsTax`, `FinancialStatements`, `Payroll`, `WebsiteSeo`, `Loyalty`, `Referrals`, `SalesAnalytics`, `GroupTours`, `MiceEvents`, `TravelApprovals`, `VisaTracker`, `Inventory`, `Recruitment`, `TicketTransactions`, `FlightReminders` (App.tsx 114–128). All other page imports are eager (static `import`).
- **`/quotations/:id/print` is intentionally unguarded** (no `<P>`) — a bare print view, unlike every other quotation route.
- **Component reuse across paths:** `Invoices` serves both `/invoices` and `/payments`; `Accounts` serves `/accounts` and `/expenses`; `Agents` serves `/agents` and `/commissions`; `WebsitePublishGuide` serves `/website/publish` and `/website/domains`.
- **`src/pages/Subscriptions.tsx`** is not imported in `App.tsx` directly — it is rendered indirectly via `SubscriptionRoute` at `/subscription` (`src/components/SubscriptionRoute.tsx`).
- **Sidebar-only URLs that don't map 1:1 to a route path:** nav item `service-catalog` points to `/packages/all` (matches the `/packages/:preset` route); `website-home` etc. under Website & CMS carry `requiredFeature: "hasWebsiteTemplates"` + `minPlan: "pro"`.
- **Deep-link-only app pages (exist as routes but absent from `navigation.ts`):** `/complaints`, `/campaigns`, `/crm-analytics`, `/crm-settings`, `/leads`, `/follow-ups`, `/support`, `/finance/reminders`, `/settings/billing`, `/settings/tax`, `/financial-statements`, `/sales-analytics`, `/inventory`, `/recruitment`, plus all `:id`/detail/print/receipt/callback routes.
- **Legacy redirect aliases (9):** `/travel-packages`, `/packages-and-services`, `/services`, `/app-packages`, `/packages` (→ `/packages/all`); `/hajj-umrah/operations`, `/legacy/hajj-operations` (→ `/hajj-umrah`); `/website/theme-builder` (→ `/website/builder`).
- **Host-aware public routing:** `/`, `/about`, `/packages`, `/blog`, `/contact` behave differently based on hostname resolution (`resolveHostname`, `getReservedSubdomain` in `src/lib/domainResolver`) — on a tenant public host they render the tenant website; on `app.` subdomain `/` redirects to `/login`; on the marketing root `/` renders `Index`.


---

# 6. DATABASE TABLES (97 models)

_All models multi-tenant (tenantId + cascade) unless noted. Part 1 + Part 2 below cover all 97 tables._

## Database Schema — Part 1

All models are multi-tenant (tenant-scoped via `tenantId` with cascade delete) unless noted. IDs are `String @id @default(uuid())` unless noted. Field type notation: `?`=optional, `PK`=primary key, `U`=unique, `@d`=default, `[]`=array.

| Model | Key fields | Relations | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Tenant** | `id` PK; `name`; `slug?` U; `ownerId?`; `phone?`, `whatsapp?`, `address?`, `city?`, `country?`, `website?`, `notes?`; `subscriptionPlan` @d"free"; `subscriptionStatus` @d"active"; `subscriptionExpiry?`; `enableHajjUmrahModule` bool @d true; `enableBdOperationsModule` bool @d false; `enabledServiceTypes[]`, `enabledSubcategories[]`, `enabledModules[]`; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | Root aggregate; owns 60+ child collections (users, clients, agents, vendors, leads, bookings, invoices, payments, quotations, accounts, transactions, expenses, hajj*, staff*, subscriptions, domains, travelPackages, loyalty*, referral*, ticket*, bsp*, etc.) | The workspace/agency account — top-level multi-tenancy boundary holding subscription state and feature-gating flags |
| **TenantDomain** | `id` PK; `tenantId` FK; `domain` U; `wwwRedirect` @d"www-to-root"; `status` @d"pending"; `sslStatus` @d"none"; `verificationStatus` @d"unverified"; `verificationToken`; `isPrimary` bool @d false; `lastDnsCheck?`; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade) | Custom domain mapping for a tenant's public website, with DNS/SSL/verification state |
| **User** | `id` PK; `name`; `email` U; `password`; `role` @d"sales_agent"; `status` @d"pending"; `phone?`, `whatsapp?`; `resetToken?`, `resetTokenExpiry?`; `emailVerified` bool; `emailVerifyToken?`, `emailVerifyExpiry?`; `whatsappVerified` bool; `approvedAt?`, `approvedBy?`, `rejectionReason?`; `lastLoginAt?`; `totpSecret?`, `totpEnabled` bool; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade); optional 1:1 AgentCommissionProfile; optional 1:1 StaffProfile | Staff/agency login account with role, approval workflow, email/WhatsApp verification and TOTP 2FA |
| **WhatsappOtp** | `id` PK; `phone` (idx); `code`; `purpose` @d"signup"; `expiresAt`; `attempts` int @d0; `sendCount` int @d1; `verified` bool @d false; `createdAt` | none (standalone) | One-time-password records for WhatsApp phone verification / signup |
| **StaffProfile** | `id` PK; `userId` U FK; `tenantId` FK (idx); `jobTitle?`, `department?`, `joinDate?`, `emergencyContact?`, `emergencyPhone?`, `notes?`; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | 1:1 User (cascade); belongs to Tenant; optional 1:1 SalaryStructure; has many PayslipEntry | HR profile extending a User with employment details |
| **StaffAttendance** | `id` PK; `userId`; `tenantId` FK; `date` (str); `status` @d"present"; `checkIn?`, `checkOut?`; `notes?`; `markedBy?`; `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; U(`tenantId`,`userId`,`date`); idx(`tenantId`,`date`) | belongs to Tenant (cascade) | Daily attendance record per staff member (one row per user per day) |
| **StaffLeaveRequest** | `id` PK; `userId`; `tenantId` FK; `leaveType` @d"annual"; `startDate`, `endDate` (str); `reason` @d""; `status` @d"pending"; `reviewedBy?`, `reviewedAt?`, `reviewNotes?`; `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; idx(`tenantId`,`status`) | belongs to Tenant (cascade) | Staff leave/time-off request with approval workflow |
| **Client** | `id` PK; `name`; `phone` @d""; `email` @d""; `alternatePhone?`, `address?`, `dateOfBirth?`, `passportNumber?`, `passportExpiry?`, `nidNumber?`, `nationality?`, `emergencyContact?`, `emergencyPhone?`, `notes?`, `companyName?`; `clientType` @d"individual"; `tags[]`; `walletBalance` float @d0; `creditLimit?`; `contractRef?`, `contractExpiry?`; `customFields?` Json; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; idx(`tenantId`,`createdAt`), idx(`tenantId`,`clientType`) | belongs to Tenant (cascade); has many ClientFamilyMember, WalletTransaction, Booking, Invoice, Lead, Quotation, ClientDocument, SupportTicket, MiceEvent, VisaApplication; optional 1:1 LoyaltyAccount | Customer/traveler record with KYC (passport/NID), wallet balance, credit terms and custom fields |
| **ClientDocument** | `id` PK; `clientId` FK; `name`; `type`; `url`; `uploadedAt` | belongs to Client (cascade) | Uploaded document/attachment for a client (passport scan, etc.) |
| **Agent** | `id` PK; `name`; `phone` @d""; `email` @d""; `address?`; `balance` float @d0; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade); has many Booking, AgentTransaction; optional 1:1 AgentCommissionProfile | Sub-agent / B2B partner with a running account balance |
| **AgentTransaction** | `id` PK; `agentId` FK (idx); `tenantId` FK; `type` @d"deposit"; `amount` float; `balance` float @d0; `method?`, `note?`, `createdBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to Agent (cascade), Tenant (cascade) | Ledger entry (deposit/adjustment) against an agent's balance |
| **VisaStock** | `id` PK; `tenantId` FK (idx w/ status); `visaType`; `country?`, `duration?`, `sponsorId?`, `visaId?`, `occupation?`; `buyingPrice` float @d0; `sellingPrice` float @d0; `profit` float @d0; `status` @d"available"; `buyerName?`, `agentId?`, `notes?`, `createdBy?`; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade) | Inventory of pre-purchased visa quotas (buy/sell price, profit, availability) — BD operations |
| **AgentCommissionProfile** | `agentId` PK/FK; `commissionRate` float @d0; `status` @d"active"; `userId?` U FK; `updatedAt` | 1:1 Agent (cascade); optional 1:1 User (SetNull) | Commission rate configuration linking an agent (and optionally a login user) |
| **BookingAgentCommission** | `bookingId` PK/FK; `agentCommissionAmount?` float; `agentCommissionStatus?` @d"pending" | 1:1 Booking (cascade) | Per-booking agent commission amount and payout status |
| **Vendor** | `id` PK; `name`; `phone` @d""; `email` @d""; `category` @d"other"; `contactPerson?`, `address?`, `serviceAreas?`, `website?`, `bankDetails?`, `notes?`; `status` @d"active"; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade); has many VendorBill, VendorNote, Expense | Supplier/service provider (hotels, airlines, transport, etc.) |
| **VendorBill** | `id` PK; `vendorId` FK; `bookingId?`, `segmentId?`; `description`; `totalAmount`/`paidAmount`/`dueAmount` float @d0; `status` @d"unpaid"; `dueDate?`, `invoiceRef?`, `notes?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to Vendor (cascade), Tenant (cascade); has many VendorBillPayment | Payable/bill owed to a vendor, optionally tied to a booking/segment |
| **VendorBillPayment** | `id` PK; `billId` FK; `amount` float; `method`; `reference?`; `date` (str); `notes?`, `paidBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to VendorBill (cascade) | Payment made against a vendor bill |
| **VendorNote** | `id` PK; `vendorId` FK; `type` @d"note"; `content`; `createdBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to Vendor (cascade) | Free-text note/log entry against a vendor |
| **Lead** | `id` PK; `name`; `phone` @d""; `email` @d""; `status` @d"new"; `source?`, `destination?`, `travelDateFrom?`, `travelDateTo?`; `travelerCount?` int; `budget?` float; `score` int @d0; `assignedTo?`, `nextFollowUp?`, `notes?`; `tags[]`; `clientId?` FK; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; idx(`tenantId`,`status`), (`tenantId`,`assignedTo`), (`tenantId`,`createdAt`) | belongs to Tenant (cascade), optional Client; has many LeadActivity, Quotation | Sales lead / inquiry with pipeline status, scoring and follow-up |
| **LeadActivity** | `id` PK; `leadId` FK; `type`; `content`; `oldStatus?`, `newStatus?`, `createdBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to Lead (cascade) | Activity/timeline entry (note, call, status change) on a lead |
| **Task** | `id` PK; `title`; `description` @d""; `status` @d"todo"; `priority` @d"medium"; `dueDate?`, `assignedTo?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade) | Generic to-do/task item assignable to a user |
| **SupportTicket** | `id` PK; `ticketNumber`; `subject`; `description` @d""; `status` @d"open"; `priority` @d"medium"; `category` @d"general"; `source` @d"internal"; `clientId?` FK, `bookingId?` FK; `assignedTo?`, `createdBy?`, `resolutionNotes?`, `resolvedAt?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; U(`tenantId`,`ticketNumber`); idx(`tenantId`,`status`) | belongs to Tenant (cascade), optional Client (SetNull), optional Booking (SetNull) | Customer support ticket linked to a client/booking |
| **WebsitePost** | `id` PK; `tenantId` FK; `slug`; `title`; `excerpt` @d""; `body` @d""; `coverImage?`; `status` @d"draft"; `publishedAt?`, `authorName?`, `metaTitle?`, `metaDescription?`, `ogImage?`; `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; U(`tenantId`,`slug`); idx(`tenantId`,`status`) | belongs to Tenant (cascade) | Blog/CMS post for the tenant's public website (with SEO meta) |
| **Booking** | `id` PK; `type` @d"package"; `title?`; `clientId` FK; `agentId?` FK; `quotationId?`, `packageId?` FK; `serviceType?`; `packageTitleSnapshot?`, `packageCodeSnapshot?`; `destination?`, `travelDateFrom?`, `travelDateTo?`; `travelerCount?` int; `amount`/`cost`/`profit`/`paidAmount`/`dueAmount` float @d0; `paymentStatus` @d"unpaid"; `status` @d"pending"; `assignedTo?`, `supplierName?`, `supplierRef?`, `internalNotes?`; `serviceDetails?` Json; `opsStatus?` @d"pending"; `followUpDate?`, `followUpNote?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; 6 idxs (status, clientId, createdAt, paymentStatus, agentId, followUpDate) | belongs to Tenant (cascade), Client, optional Agent, optional TravelPackage; has many BookingSegment, BookingTraveler, BookingChecklistItem, BookingTimelineEvent, BookingDocument, Invoice, SupportTicket, GroupTourBooking, VisaApplication, TicketRefund/Void/Reissue; optional 1:1 BookingAgentCommission | Core sales order — a sold trip/service with financials, ops status and full sub-record graph |
| **BookingSegment** | `id` PK; `bookingId` FK; `type`; `description`; `supplier?`, `supplierRef?`, `startDate?`, `endDate?`, `details?`; `cost` float @d0; `sellingPrice` float @d0; `status` @d"pending" | belongs to Booking (cascade) | Individual service line within a booking (flight/hotel/transfer) with its own cost/price |
| **BookingTraveler** | `id` PK; `bookingId` FK; `name`; `passportNumber?`, `passportExpiry?`, `nationality?`, `dateOfBirth?`, `phone?`, `email?`, `notes?` | belongs to Booking (cascade) | Passenger/traveler on a booking (passport details) |
| **BookingChecklistItem** | `id` PK; `bookingId` FK; `label`; `done` bool @d false; `doneAt?`, `doneBy?` | belongs to Booking (cascade) | Operational checklist task item for a booking |
| **BookingTimelineEvent** | `id` PK; `bookingId` FK; `type`; `content`; `oldStatus?`, `newStatus?`, `createdBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to Booking (cascade) | Audit/timeline log entry for a booking (status changes, notes) |
| **BookingDocument** | `id` PK; `bookingId` FK; `name`; `type`; `url`; `uploadedAt`; `uploadedBy?` | belongs to Booking (cascade) | Uploaded document/attachment for a booking |
| **Invoice** | `id` PK; `invoiceNumber?`; `bookingId` FK; `clientId?` FK, `clientName?`, `bookingTitle?`; `totalAmount`/`paidAmount`/`dueAmount`/`refundedAmount` float @d0; `status` @d"unpaid"; `dueDate?`, `issuedDate?`, `notes?`, `cancelReason?`; `taxRuleId?`; `taxRate`/`taxAmount`/`subTotal` float @d0; `createdBy?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; idx(status, clientId, createdAt) | belongs to Tenant (cascade), Booking, optional Client; has many Payment, InvoiceRefund, InvoiceAuditEvent, InvoiceInstallment | Invoice against a booking with tax breakdown and payment/refund tracking |
| **InvoiceInstallment** | `id` PK; `invoiceId` FK; `label`; `amount` float @d0; `dueDate?`; `paidAmount` float @d0; `status` @d"pending"; `sortOrder` int @d0; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to Invoice (cascade), Tenant (cascade) | Scheduled installment/payment-plan line on an invoice |
| **Payment** | `id` PK; `invoiceId` FK; `bookingId`; `amount` float; `method`; `transactionRef?`, `proofUrl?`; `date` (str); `notes?`, `receivedBy?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`; idx(`tenantId`,`createdAt`), (`tenantId`,`invoiceId`) | belongs to Invoice (cascade), Tenant (cascade) | Payment received against an invoice |
| **InvoiceRefund** | `id` PK; `invoiceId` FK; `amount` float; `reason`; `method?`, `processedBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to Invoice (cascade) | Refund issued against an invoice |
| **InvoiceAuditEvent** | `id` PK; `invoiceId` FK; `type`; `content`; `oldStatus?`, `newStatus?`; `amount?` float; `createdBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to Invoice (cascade) | Audit-trail entry for invoice changes (status, amounts) |
| **Quotation** | `id` PK; `title`; `clientId?` FK, `leadId?` FK, `packageId?` FK; `serviceType?`, `packageTitleSnapshot?`, `packageCodeSnapshot?`; `destination`; `travelDateFrom?`, `travelDateTo?`; `travelerCount` int @d1; `status` @d"draft"; `version` int @d1; `items` Json @d"[]", `itinerary` Json @d"[]"; `totalCost`/`totalSelling`/`totalProfit`/`discountAmount`/`taxAmount`/`grandTotal` float @d0; `validUntil?`, `notes?`, `termsAndConditions?`, `createdBy?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade), optional Client, Lead, TravelPackage; has many QuotationVersion | Price quote/proposal (line items + itinerary) that can convert to a booking |
| **QuotationVersion** | `id` PK; `quotationId` FK; `versionNumber` int; `snapshot` (str); `changeNote?`, `changedBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to Quotation (cascade) | Immutable historical snapshot of a quotation revision |
| **Account** | `id` PK; `name`; `type`; `balance` float @d0; `accountNumber?`, `bankName?`, `notes?`; `status` @d"active"; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade); has many Transaction, Expense | Financial account/ledger (cash/bank) holding a running balance |
| **Transaction** | `id` PK; `accountId?` FK; `type`; `category`; `description`; `amount` float; `referenceId?`, `referenceType?`, `clientId?`, `bookingId?`, `vendorId?`, `invoiceId?`, `paymentMethod?`; `status` @d"completed"; `date` (str); `tenantId` FK; `createdBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade), optional Account | General-ledger money movement (income/expense/transfer) with polymorphic reference links |
| **Expense** | `id` PK; `category`; `description`; `amount` float; `date` (str); `paymentMethod`; `reference?`, `notes?`, `attachmentUrl?`; `vendorId?` FK, `accountId?` FK; `approvedBy?`; `status` @d"pending"; `tenantId` FK; `createdBy?`; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade), optional Vendor, optional Account | Business expense with approval status and vendor/account links |
| **TravelPackage** | `id` PK; `tenantId` FK; `serviceType` @d"tour_domestic"; `code`; `title`; `slug`; `summary?`, `destination?`, `country?`; `durationDays` int @d1, `durationNights` int @d0; `basePrice` float @d0; `currency` @d"BDT"; `status` @d"draft"; `isFeatured` bool @d false; `visaRequired?` bool; `cancellationPolicy?`; `seasonalPricing?` Json; `heroImage?`, `metaTitle?`, `metaDescription?`, `ogImage?`, `createdBy?`; `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; U(`tenantId`,`code`), U(`tenantId`,`slug`) | belongs to Tenant (cascade); has many TravelPackageDay, TravelPackageInclusion, TravelPackagePricing, TravelPackageMedia, Quotation, Booking | Reusable tour/package product template (itinerary, pricing tiers, media, SEO) |
| **TravelPackageDay** | `id` PK; `packageId` FK; `dayNumber` int; `title`; `description?`, `overnightLocation?` | belongs to TravelPackage (cascade) | Single day of a package itinerary |
| **TravelPackageInclusion** | `id` PK; `packageId` FK; `type` @d"included"; `label`; `sortOrder` int @d0 | belongs to TravelPackage (cascade) | Inclusion/exclusion line item for a package |
| **TravelPackagePricing** | `id` PK; `packageId` FK; `label`; `travelerMin` int @d1, `travelerMax?` int; `price` float @d0; `currency` @d"BDT" | belongs to TravelPackage (cascade) | Tiered pricing band (by group size) for a package |
| **TravelPackageMedia** | `id` PK; `packageId` FK; `url`; `altText?`; `sortOrder` int @d0 | belongs to TravelPackage (cascade) | Image/media asset attached to a package |
| **HajjPackage** | `id` PK; `name`; `type`; `status` @d"upcoming"; `duration` @d""; `makkahNights`/`madinahNights` int @d0; `makkahHotel?`, `madinahHotel?`; `hotelClass` @d"economy"; `flightInfo?`; `visaIncluded`/`transportIncluded`/`mealsIncluded`/`ziyaratIncluded` bool @d false; `packagePrice`/`costPrice`/`profit` float @d0; `capacity`/`enrolled` int @d0; `departureDate?`, `returnDate?`, `highlights?`, `notes?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to Tenant (cascade); has many HajjGroup, HajjPilgrim | Hajj/Umrah package product (hotels, inclusions, capacity, pricing) |
| **HajjGroup** | `id` PK; `packageId` FK; `name`; `leader`; `leaderPhone?`; `departureDate`, `returnDate` (str); `flightDetails?`, `transportSchedule?`, `notes?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt` | belongs to HajjPackage (cascade), Tenant (cascade); has many HajjPilgrim | A travel group/batch within a Hajj package (leader, flight/transport schedule) |
| **HajjPilgrim** | `id` PK; `packageId` FK, `groupId` FK; `clientId?`; `name`; `phone` @d""; `email?`, `dateOfBirth?`, `gender?`; `passportNumber` @d"", `passportExpiry?`, `nidNumber?`, `nationality?`; `mahramName?`, `mahramRelation?`, `mahramPilgrimId?`; `roomType?`, `roomNumber?`, `roomPartners?`; `status` @d"registered"; `visaStatus` @d"not_started"; `departureStatus` @d"not_departed"; `totalAmount`/`paidAmount`/`dueAmount` float @d0; `paymentStatus` @d"unpaid"; `emergencyContact?`, `emergencyPhone?`, `medicalNotes?`, `notes?`; `tenantId` FK; `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | belongs to HajjPackage (cascade), HajjGroup (cascade), Tenant (cascade); has many HajjPilgrimPayment | Individual pilgrim enrolled in a Hajj package/group (KYC, mahram, rooming, visa & payment status) |
| **HajjPilgrimPayment** | `id` PK; `pilgrimId` FK; `amount` float; `method`; `reference?`; `date` (str); `note?`, `installmentLabel?`, `receivedBy?`; `createdAt` | belongs to HajjPilgrim (cascade) | Payment/installment received from a pilgrim |

**Notes:**
- Nearly all date fields on booking/hajj/staff/expense/payment models are stored as `String` (not `DateTime`) — e.g. `travelDateFrom`, `date`, `dueDate`, `joinDate`, `departureDate`. `DateTime` is used for system timestamps (`createdAt`/`updatedAt`) and a subset of workflow dates (`resolvedAt`, `approvedAt`, `followUpDate`, `publishedAt`, `lastLoginAt`).
- `Booking` is the central hub — it fans out to 17 relations and carries denormalized package snapshots (`packageTitleSnapshot`, `packageCodeSnapshot`) so historical bookings survive package edits.
- Financial denormalization pattern (`totalAmount`/`paidAmount`/`dueAmount`) is repeated across `Booking`, `Invoice`, `VendorBill`, `HajjPilgrim`.
- `AgentCommissionProfile` and `BookingAgentCommission` use a non-uuid PK (`agentId` / `bookingId` respectively) enforcing a strict 1:1 with their parent.
- Source file: `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/prisma/schema.prisma` (models span lines 10–1011 for this assignment's set).

### Cross-cutting notes
- **Multi-tenancy:** Nearly every model carries `tenantId`; most declare an explicit `tenant Tenant @relation(... onDelete: Cascade)`. Platform-global models with **no** `tenantId` are `PaymentMethodConfig`, `DemoRequest`, `ContactSubmission`, `PlatformNotification`, `MasterReference`, `SubscriptionCoupon`, and `PlatformStaff`. `AuditLog` and `ContactSubmission` keep tenant context as denormalized scalars only.
- **Nullable `tenantId` (per-tenant OR global rows):** `SmsTemplate`, `SmsLog`, `WhatsAppTemplate`, `WhatsAppLog` (`tenantId?`).
- **Communications logging trio:** `SmsLog`, `WhatsAppLog`, and `NotificationDelivery` are parallel delivery ledgers; `SmsTemplate`/`WhatsAppTemplate` supply the content; `SmsSettings` + `NotificationAutomation` govern whether/how each event fires.
- **Air-ticket after-sales trio:** `TicketRefund`, `TicketVoid`, `TicketReissue` share a common shape (PNR, ticket number, airline, fee columns, status lifecycle) and all optionally link back to a `Booking` via `SetNull`.
- **BSP reconciliation:** `BspUpload` (batch) → `BspRecord[]` (lines), matched against internal bookings by PNR/booking id.
- **Phase-3 modules** (explicitly demarcated by comment headers in the schema): MICE (`MiceEvent`, `MiceEventItem`), Corporate Travel Policy/Approval (`TravelPolicy`, `TravelApprovalRequest`), Visa Tracker (`VisaApplication`), Hotel/Transport Inventory (`HotelContract`, `TransportContract`), Recruitment/Manpower (`JobPosting`, `JobApplication`).
- **Date typing inconsistency:** Several models store dates as `String` (e.g. `Subscription.startDate/endDate`, `SalaryStructure.effectiveFrom`, `GroupTour.departureDate`, ticket `ticketDate/voidDeadline/originalDate`) while newer Phase-3 models use `DateTime?` — worth noting for date handling/reporting.


---

# 7 & 8. USER ROLES + PERMISSION MATRIX

# User Roles & Permission Matrix

## 1. Roles

The system defines **two distinct role systems**: **tenant roles** (stored on the `User.role` field) and a separate **platform staff** system (stored in the `PlatformStaff` table with a `permissions` array).

### 1.1 Tenant Application Roles (`AppRole`)

Defined in `src/lib/permissions.ts` (lines 5–11) and mirrored in `backend/src/middleware/auth.js`.

| Role ID | Label | Bengali Label | `isTenantRole` | Scope / Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `super_admin` | Super Admin | সুপার অ্যাডমিন | `false` (platform-only) | Full platform access. Manages all tenants, plans, system settings. **Bypasses all permission checks.** |
| `tenant_owner` | Tenant Owner | মালিক | `true` | Full access within their tenant. Manages team, subscription, all modules. **No** `admin_panel` access. |
| `manager` | Manager | ম্যানেজার | `true` | Day-to-day operations. Most modules except settings/billing edit. |
| `sales_agent` | Sales Agent | সেলস এজেন্ট | `true` | Clients, leads, bookings. Limited view of other modules. **Default role.** |
| `accountant` | Accountant | হিসাবরক্ষক | `true` | Invoices, payments, accounts, financial reports. |
| `operations` | Operations Staff | অপারেশন্স | `true` | Bookings, Hajj/Umrah operations, vendors, tasks. |

**Role metadata** (labels, colors, descriptions): `ROLE_METADATA` array — `src/lib/permissions.ts` lines 278–327. Colors are Tailwind badge classes (super_admin=red, tenant_owner=purple, manager=blue, sales_agent=green, accountant=amber, operations=cyan).

### 1.2 Legacy Roles (backend compatibility)

`src/lib/permissions.ts` lines 13–26 — `mapLegacyRole()` maps old role strings onto new `AppRole`s:

| Legacy Role | Maps To |
|---|---|
| `owner` | `tenant_owner` |
| `admin` | `tenant_owner` |
| `member` | `sales_agent` |
| (any other) | passed through as-is |

Backend also treats `owner` and `admin` as owner-equivalents when protecting the tenant owner from role changes (`backend/src/routes/tenants.js` line 188).

### 1.3 Persistence & Defaults

- `User.role` is a plain **`String @default("sales_agent")`** in `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` line 111 — **no DB enum**, so any string is technically storable; validation is enforced in application code (see §4).
- New tenant registration assigns the first user `role: "tenant_owner"` (`backend/src/routes/auth.js` line 282).
- The JWT carries `{ userId, tenantId, role }` (auth.js lines 61, 324); `authenticate` re-reads the role fresh from DB on every request (`backend/src/middleware/auth.js` lines 14–17, 38).

### 1.4 Platform Staff (separate system)

`backend/src/routes/adminPlatformStaff.js` — super-admin's internal team, stored in the `PlatformStaff` table (not tenant Users). Login issues a JWT with `role: "platform_staff"` and a `permissions` array. Available permission keys (`PERMISSION_OPTIONS`, lines 14–24):

| Key | Label |
|---|---|
| `agencies` | Agencies — view & manage |
| `payments` | Payments — view & approve |
| `subscriptions` | Subscriptions — activate & extend |
| `reports` | Reports & Analytics |
| `audit_log` | Audit Log |
| `whatsapp` | WhatsApp Logs & Send |
| `sms` | SMS Logs |
| `domains` | Domains |
| `master_data` | Master Data |

Managing platform staff requires `super_admin` (`router.use(requireSuperAdmin)`, line 54).

## 3. Actions

| Action | Label |

## 4. Default Permission Matrix

Source of truth (frontend): `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS` in `src/lib/permissions.ts` lines 68–200. Enforced backend copy: `ROLE_PERMISSIONS` in `backend/src/middleware/auth.js` lines 65–146 (the two are intended to mirror each other; `super_admin` is not in the map — it short-circuits to allow-all).

**Legend for cells:** `Full` = all 6 actions (view, create, edit, delete, approve, export). Otherwise the allowed action initials are listed. `—` = no access (NONE). `super_admin` = Full on every module including `admin_panel`.

Rows = modules; columns = the 5 tenant-configurable roles (super_admin omitted — always Full everywhere).

| Module | tenant_owner | manager | sales_agent | accountant | operations |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **dashboard** | Full | view, export | view | view, export | view |
| **clients** | Full | Full | view, create, edit | view | view |
| **agents** | Full | Full | view | view | view |
| **vendors** | Full | Full | view | view | view, create, edit |
| **leads** | Full | Full | view, create, edit, delete | — | — |
| **tasks** | Full | Full | view, create, edit | view | view, create, edit |
| **quotations** | Full | Full | view, create, edit, export | view, export | view |
| **packages** | Full | Full | view, create, edit | view | view, create, edit |
| **bookings** | Full | Full | view, create, edit | view | view, create, edit, export |
| **invoices** | Full | view, create, edit, approve, export | view | view, create, edit, approve, export | view |
| **accounts** | Full | view, create, edit, export | — | view, create, edit, export | — |
| **reports** | Full | view, export | — | view, export | view |
| **hajj_umrah** | Full | Full | view, create | view, export | view, create, edit, export |
| **subscription** | Full | view | — | view | — |
| **team** | Full | view | — | — | — |
| **organization** | Full | view | — | — | — |
| **settings** | Full | — | — | — | — |
| **website** | Full | view, edit | — | — | — |
| **admin_panel** | — | — | — | — | — |

**Notable per-role summaries:**
- **tenant_owner** — `Full` on every module **except** `admin_panel` (NONE). It is the only tenant role with any access to `settings` (Full).
- **manager** — Full CRM/operations; on finance it can approve invoices but **cannot delete** invoices/accounts and gets only view+export on reports; view-only on subscription/team/organization; view+edit on website; **no** settings/accounts-delete/admin_panel.
- **sales_agent** — CRM-focused; the only role besides owner/manager that can `delete` leads; no accounts/reports/subscription/team/organization/settings/website access.
- **accountant** — Finance-focused: Full-minus-delete on invoices & accounts (view, create, edit, approve, export — no delete), view+export reports & Hajj/Umrah; view-only clients/agents/vendors/tasks/packages/bookings/quotations(+export)/subscription; **no** leads.
- **operations** — Operations-focused: create/edit on vendors, tasks, packages; bookings gets view+create+edit+export; Hajj/Umrah view+create+edit+export; view-only elsewhere; **no** leads/accounts/subscription/team/settings.

### Backend vs frontend matrix parity note
The backend `ROLE_PERMISSIONS` (auth.js) does **not** list modules where a role has NONE (e.g. `sales_agent` has no `accounts`/`reports` keys), which is equivalent to denial in `requirePermission`. The two matrices are consistent with the frontend `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS`. The backend omits `admin_panel` entirely from the map (guarded separately by `requireSuperAdmin`).

---

## 5. Permission Resolution & Middleware

### 5.1 Backend (`backend/src/middleware/auth.js`)

| Function | Purpose | Lines |
|---|---|---|
| `authenticate` | Verifies JWT, loads `{id, tenantId, role, status}`, blocks `pending`/`rejected`/non-`active` accounts (returns codes `PENDING_APPROVAL`, `REJECTED`, `INACTIVE`), sets `req.userId`/`req.tenantId`/`req.userRole`. | 8–43 |
| `requireSuperAdmin` | 403 unless `req.userRole === "super_admin"`. | 45–48 |
| `requireRole(...roles)` | `super_admin` always passes; else `req.userRole` must be in the allow-list; 403 otherwise. | 52–61 |
| `requirePermission(module, action)` | `super_admin` bypass; looks up `ROLE_PERMISSIONS[role][module]`, checks `action` is included; 403 `"Forbidden: {role} cannot {action} {module}"`. | 149–158 |
| `checkPlanLimit(resource)` | Subscription-limit gate (not a role check) — counts tenant rows vs plan limit; fails closed (503) on error. | 165–201 |
| `requireFeature(flag)` | Subscription-feature gate; `super_admin` bypass; 403 `FEATURE_NOT_IN_PLAN` otherwise. | 207–227 |

**Resolution order in `requirePermission`:** super_admin → allow; else exact lookup in the static `ROLE_PERMISSIONS` map; missing module OR action not in array → 403. There is **no per-user or per-tenant override applied on the backend** — the backend map is static.

### 5.2 Frontend (`src/lib/permissions.ts` + `src/hooks/usePermissions.ts`)

Helper functions (permissions.ts lines 208–266):

| Helper | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `hasPermission(role, module, action, overrides?)` | `super_admin` → always `true`; then checks `overrides[role][module][action]` if defined; else `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS[role][module][action] ?? false`. |
| `canAccessModule(role, module, overrides?)` | `super_admin` → true; else true if the module has **any** truthy action (from overrides or defaults). |
| `getModulePermissions(role, module, overrides?)` | `super_admin` → `ALL_ACTIONS`; else overrides-or-default action map (or `{}`). |
| `getAccessibleModules(role, overrides?)` | Filters all 19 modules via `canAccessModule`. |

`usePermissions()` hook (`src/hooks/usePermissions.ts`):
- Reads `user.role` from `AuthContext`, maps legacy roles via `mapLegacyRole`, defaults to `"sales_agent"` when no user.
- Exposes: `role`, `can(module, action)`, `canAccess(module)`, `modulePerms(module)`, `accessibleModules`, boolean flags `isSuperAdmin`/`isTenantOwner`/`isManager`/`isSalesAgent`/`isAccountant`/`isOperations`, and convenience gates `canManageTeam` (super_admin | tenant_owner), `canManageSubscription` (super_admin | tenant_owner), `canAccessAdmin` (super_admin only).
- **Note:** the hook calls the helpers **without** passing an `overrides` argument, so in practice the frontend always uses `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS`.

---

## 6. Role Management UI (`src/pages/RoleManagement.tsx`)

- Page title "Role Management", guarded by `canManageTeam` for edit actions.
- **Role summary cards** — one per tenant role (from `getTenantRoles()`, i.e. the 5 non-super_admin roles), showing live member counts; click opens a permissions dialog.
- **Two tabs:**
  1. **Team Members** — table of members (name, email, role badge, status, joined date). Search by name/email, filter by role. If `canManageTeam`: per-row "View permissions" (Eye) and "Change role" (Pencil) actions.
  2. **Permission Matrix** — modules × tenant roles grid; cells render `Full` badge, action initials, or `—`. Excludes `admin_panel` (`.filter(m => m.id !== "admin_panel")`).
- **Change Role dialog** — select among the 5 tenant roles; calls `tenantApi.updateMember(id, { role })`.
- **View Permissions dialog** — full module × action grid (✓/✗) for the selected role, reading straight from `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS`. Also excludes `admin_panel`.
- The UI only reads `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS` — it does **not** let users edit/persist per-action permission overrides. Role assignment is the only mutable capability.

### Backend team-member role endpoints (`backend/src/routes/tenants.js`)

| Endpoint | Guard | Role validation |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /me/members` | `requirePermission("team","view")` | — |
| `POST /me/members` (invite) | `requireRole("tenant_owner")` + `checkPlanLimit("users")` | `allowedRoles = ["manager","sales_agent","accountant","operations"]`; invalid → falls back to `sales_agent` (line 88–89). |
| `PATCH /me/members/:userId` | `requireRole("tenant_owner")` | Cannot change **own** role (400); cannot change a `tenant_owner`/`owner`/`admin` target (400, line 188); new role must be in `allowedRoles` else 400 "Invalid role for team member" (lines 192–198). |
| `DELETE /me/members/:userId` | `requireRole("tenant_owner")` | — |

All member mutations write to the audit log (actor id/name/email/role, old/new values).

---

## 7. Custom-Role Capability

**There is NO custom-role feature.** Findings:

- Roles are a **fixed enumeration** of 6 `AppRole` values. There is **no** `Role` table, no `custom_role`/`isCustom`/dynamic-role construct in `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` (only `User.role String @default("sales_agent")` at line 111, and an unrelated `approverRole String @default("manager")` at line 1584).
- The permission matrix is **hardcoded** in two static maps (`DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS` frontend, `ROLE_PERMISSIONS` backend). Neither is stored in or read from the database.
- The `hasPermission`/`canAccessModule`/`getModulePermissions`/`getAccessibleModules` helpers accept an optional `overrides` parameter (typed `Partial<Record<AppRole, Partial<PermissionMatrix>>>`) — this is the **only** extensibility seam for per-role customization — but **no caller supplies it** (the `usePermissions` hook never passes overrides, and there is no persistence layer or UI to author them). It is dead/forward-looking capability.
- Tenant owners can only **assign** one of 4 invitable roles (`manager`, `sales_agent`, `accountant`, `operations`) to members — they cannot create new roles or alter any role's permission set.
- The only role-adjacent customization that IS persisted and editable is **PlatformStaff** — super-admin internal accounts get a freely-editable `permissions` string array chosen from 9 fixed `PERMISSION_OPTIONS` keys (`backend/src/routes/adminPlatformStaff.js`). This is a platform-side ACL, not a tenant custom-role system.

**Relevant files:**
- `src/lib/permissions.ts` — roles, modules, actions, `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS`, helpers, metadata
- `backend/src/middleware/auth.js` — `authenticate`, `requireRole`, `requirePermission`, `requireSuperAdmin`, static `ROLE_PERMISSIONS`
- `src/hooks/usePermissions.ts` — frontend permission hook
- `src/pages/RoleManagement.tsx` — role UI (matrix view + role assignment)
- `backend/src/routes/tenants.js` — member invite/update/delete with `allowedRoles` validation
- `backend/src/routes/adminPlatformStaff.js` — platform staff `PERMISSION_OPTIONS` ACL
- `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` (line 111) — `User.role String @default("sales_agent")`


---

# 9. PLAN-WISE ACCESS (Basic / Pro / Business / Enterprise)

# Subscription Plans, Limits & Feature Flags

## 1. Overview & Source-of-Truth Files

| Layer | File | Role |
|---|---|---|
| Frontend plan catalog | `src/lib/plans.ts` | `PLANS[]`, `FEATURE_COMPARISON`, prices, limits, feature booleans, usage helpers, TS types |
| Backend limits + flags | `backend/src/lib/planFeatures.js` | `PLAN_LIMITS`, `PLAN_FEATURES`, `RESOURCE_MODEL_MAP`, `getPlanLimit()`, `planHasFeature()`, `normalizePlan()` — **single server-side source of truth** |
| Backend pricing | `backend/src/lib/planPricing.js` | `PLAN_PRICES`, `getPlanPrice()`, `normalizePlan()`, `normalizeBillingCycle()` |
| Frontend block state | `src/lib/subscriptionAccess.ts` | `getSubscriptionBlockState()` — UI blocking for expired/suspended/cancelled/trial-ended |
| Backend access gate | `backend/src/middleware/subscriptionAccess.js` | `subscriptionAccessGate` (app-level 402 block), `tenantIsBlocked()` |
| Backend limit/feature middleware | `backend/src/middleware/auth.js` (lines 160–229) | `checkPlanLimit(resource)`, `requireFeature(flag)` |

Plan IDs (`PlanType`, `src/lib/plans.ts:3`): `free | basic | pro | business | enterprise`. **`free` is deprecated** — `getPlan()` falls back to Basic; backend `normalizePlan()` maps `free`/`""` → `basic` and `unlimited` → `enterprise`. Enterprise is labelled "Unlimited" in the UI.

Limit convention across all files: **`-1` = unlimited, `0` = not allowed / none**.

## 2. Plans — Identity, Pricing & Badges

Source: `src/lib/plans.ts:57-160`, `backend/src/lib/planPricing.js:3-9`

| Plan ID | Name | Badge | Monthly (BDT) | Yearly (BDT) | Trial | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `basic` | Basic | — | 500 | 5,000 | 0 days | For small travel agencies getting started |
| `pro` | Pro | Most Popular | 800 | 8,000 | 0 days | For growing agencies that need an online presence |
| `business` | Business | Best Value | 1,500 | 15,000 | 0 days | For established agencies scaling operations |
| `enterprise` | Enterprise | — | 5,000 *(catalog)* / custom | 50,000 *(catalog)* / custom | 0 days | Large agencies — unlimited scale, full automation, dedicated support |
| `free` | *(deprecated)* | — | 0 | 0 | — | Legacy only; maps to Basic |

**Pricing rules:**
- Yearly = `monthly × 10` (pay for 10 months, get 12 — 2 months free). Helper `yearly()` in both `plans.ts:49` and `planPricing.js:1`.
- `getYearlySavings()` = `monthlyPrice × 12 − yearlyPrice` (2 months' worth).
- `getDisplayMonthlyPrice()` divides yearly by 12 for display.
- **Pricing discrepancy (verified):** In `src/lib/plans.ts` Enterprise has concrete `monthlyPrice: 5000 / yearlyPrice: 50000`. In `backend/src/lib/planPricing.js:7` Enterprise is `{ monthly: -1, yearly: -1 }` → `getPlanPrice()` clamps negatives to `0` (line 24), so **the backend treats Enterprise as custom/quote (returns 0)** while the frontend catalog shows 5,000/mo. `planPricing.js` still carries a `free: { 0, 0 }` row.

## 3. Numeric Limits per Plan

### 3a. Frontend catalog limits (`src/lib/plans.ts`, full set)

| Limit field | Basic | Pro | Business | Enterprise |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `maxClients` | 500 | 1,000 | 2,000 | Unlimited (−1) |
| `maxBookings` | 500 | 1,000 | 2,000 | Unlimited (−1) |
| `maxUsers` (team members) | 3 | 10 | 25 | Unlimited (−1) |
| `maxDomains` | 0 | 1 | 2 | Unlimited (−1) |
| `maxBranches` | 1 | 2 | 5 | Unlimited (−1) |
| `maxSmsPerMonth` | 0 | 500 | 2,000 | Unlimited (−1) |
| `maxStorageMB` | 500 | 2,048 (2 GB) | 10,240 (10 GB) | Unlimited (−1) |
| `maxReports` | 10 | 30 | Unlimited (−1) | Unlimited (−1) |

### 3b. Backend-enforced limits (`backend/src/lib/planFeatures.js:6-11`, `PLAN_LIMITS`)

The backend enforces a **subset** of the catalog: `clients, bookings, users, domains, leads, quotations, whatsapp`. (No server-side enforcement of `branches`, `sms/month`, `storage`, or `reports` via `PLAN_LIMITS`.)

| Resource | Basic | Pro | Business | Enterprise |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `clients` | 500 | 1,000 | 2,000 | −1 |
| `bookings` | 500 | 1,000 | 2,000 | −1 |
| `users` | 3 | 10 | 25 | −1 |
| `domains` | 0 | 1 | 2 | −1 |
| `leads` | 500 | 1,000 | 2,000 | −1 |
| `quotations` | 500 | 1,000 | 2,000 | −1 |
| `whatsapp` (msgs/month) | 0 | 200 | −1 | −1 |

**Notable frontend/backend divergence:** `maxBranches` differs — frontend catalog says Basic 1 / Pro 2 / Business 5, but `FEATURE_COMPARISON` (`plans.ts:265`) displays Branches as `1 / 1 / 1 / Unlimited`. Branches are **not** in the backend `PLAN_LIMITS`, so no server enforcement exists.

`RESOURCE_MODEL_MAP` (`planFeatures.js:13-20`) maps enforced resources → Prisma models for counting: `clients→client, bookings→booking, users→user, domains→tenantDomain, leads→lead, quotations→quotation` (whatsapp has no map entry — quota counted separately from `whatsAppLog`).

## 6. Server-Side Enforcement Points

Frontend menu-hiding is explicitly **not** the security boundary (comment, `planFeatures.js:2-3`). Three enforcement layers:

### 6a. App-level subscription gate — `backend/src/middleware/subscriptionAccess.js`
- `subscriptionAccessGate` mounted at `app.use("/api", ...)`. Blocks expired/suspended/cancelled/trial-ended tenants with **HTTP 402** `{ code: "SUBSCRIPTION_INACTIVE" }`.
- `tenantIsBlocked()` logic: blocked if status `suspended`/`cancelled`; or `trial` past expiry; or status `expired`; or (plan ≠ `free` AND past expiry).
- **Exempt path prefixes** (`EXEMPT_PREFIXES`, lines 5-17): `/auth, /payment-requests, /admin, /cron, /public, /portal, /contact, /demo-requests, /health, /email, /sms`. Also exempt: `GET /tenants/me` (for renewal UI). `super_admin` bypasses entirely (line 54).
- Calls `expireTenantIfNeeded()` then re-reads tenant before deciding (lines 63-68).

### 6b. Per-resource count limits — `checkPlanLimit(resource)` (`auth.js:165-201`)
Reads `subscriptionPlan`, calls `getPlanLimit()`. `undefined`/`-1` → pass; `0` → **403** "plan does not include"; else counts `prisma[model].count({where:{tenantId}})` and returns **403** `{message, limit, current}` when `count >= limit`. **Fails closed** (503) on error. Mount points:

| Endpoint | Resource guarded | File:line |
|---|---|---|
| `POST /clients` | `clients` | `routes/clients.js:174` |
| `POST /bookings` | `bookings` | `routes/bookings.js:284` |
| `POST /leads` | `leads` | `routes/leads.js:86` |
| `POST /quotations` | `quotations` | `routes/quotations.js:183` |
| `POST /tenants/me/members` | `users` | `routes/tenants.js:80` |

### 6c. Feature-flag gates — `requireFeature(flag)` (`auth.js:207-227`)
`super_admin` bypasses; else reads plan, `planHasFeature(plan, flag)` → pass, else **403** `{ code: "FEATURE_NOT_IN_PLAN", feature }`. Fails closed (503). Mount points:

| Endpoint / router | Feature flag required | File:line |
|---|---|---|
| `/campaigns` (whole router) | `hasMarketingTools` | `routes/campaigns.js:9` |
| `/blogs` (whole router) | `hasWebsiteTemplates` | `routes/blogs.js:5` |
| `/website` (from line 366 onward) | `hasWebsiteTemplates` | `routes/website.js:366` |
| `/whatsapp` (whole router) | `hasWhatsApp` | `routes/whatsapp.js:7` |
| `/sms` (whole router) | `hasSmsIntegration` | `routes/sms.js:7` |

### 6d. WhatsApp monthly quota enforcement (service-level)
- `whatsappService.js:21-35` `_withinWhatsAppQuota(tenantId)`: `getPlanLimit(plan,"whatsapp")`; `-1` unlimited; `<=0`/undefined → not allowed; else counts current-month `whatsAppLog` rows with `status:"sent"` (month start = UTC 1st) and allows only if `used < limit`. Enforced on every send at `whatsappService.js:65`. **Fails OPEN** (returns `true`) on error — never blocks a send due to counter failure (deliberate, unlike the count-limit middleware which fails closed).
- `GET /whatsapp/config` (`routes/whatsapp.js:10-22`) returns live `quota:{ limit, used, unlimited }` for the current month.
- Cron daily WhatsApp summary skips tenants lacking `hasWhatsApp` (`routes/cron.js:232`).

### 6e. Custom-domain limit enforcement (two separate maps — **not** using `PLAN_LIMITS`)
Both `routes/domains.js:5` (super-admin) and `routes/tenantDomains.js:5` (tenant-facing) hardcode their own `DOMAIN_PLAN_LIMITS = { free:0, basic:0, pro:1, business:2, enterprise:-1, unlimited:-1 }` — consistent with `PLAN_LIMITS.domains` but duplicated. Tenant-facing enforcement (`tenantDomains.js`): requires active/trial subscription (**403** "must be active", line 116); `maxDomains === 0` → **403** "does not support custom domains" (line 118); `count >= maxDomains` → **403** "Domain limit reached (N)" (line 147). First domain auto-set `isPrimary` (line 157).

## 7. Frontend Usage / Blocking Helpers

- `checkUsage(sub)` (`plans.ts:233-255`): builds `UsageCheck[]` for Clients, Bookings, Users, SMS, Storage (MB), Branches, Leads, Quotations — with `isUnlimited`, `percentage` (capped 100), `isNearLimit` (≥80%), `isAtLimit` (used ≥ limit).
- `getSubscriptionBlockState(tenant, currentPlan)` (`subscriptionAccess.ts`): returns `{isBlocked, reason}` with reason ∈ `trial_ended | expired | suspended | cancelled`. Logic mirrors backend `tenantIsBlocked()`: suspended → `suspended`; cancelled → `cancelled`; trial past expiry → `trial_ended`; status expired → `expired`; plan ≠ `free` past expiry → `expired`.
- Label helpers: `getLimitLabel(v)` → `-1`→"Unlimited", `0`→"None", else `toLocaleString()`; `getDomainLimitLabel(planId)`; `getPlanPrice(planId, cycle)`; `getYearlySavings(planId)`.

## 8. Type Definitions (`src/lib/plans.ts:3-46, 189-231`)
- `PlanType`, `BillingCycle` (`monthly | yearly`), `SubscriptionStatus` (`trial | active | overdue | expired | suspended | cancelled`).
- `PlanConfig` interface: id, deprecated `price`, name, `monthlyPrice`/`yearlyPrice` (0=free, −1=custom), description, badge?, trialDays, 10 `max*` limit fields, `features[]`, `restrictions[]`, `paymentGateways[]`, and 13 `has*` boolean feature flags.
- `TenantSubscription` interface: identity + billing fields + usage counters (`usedClients, usedBookings, usedUsers, usedSms, usedStorageMB, usedBranches, usedLeads, usedQuotations`).

## 9. Key Inconsistencies / Notes for Master Doc
1. **Enterprise price mismatch:** frontend catalog 5,000/50,000 BDT vs backend `planPricing.js` `-1`→0 (custom/quote).
2. **Branches:** catalog limits (1/2/5) contradict `FEATURE_COMPARISON` display (1/1/1) and have **no backend enforcement**.
3. **Backend enforces only 7 resources** (`clients, bookings, users, domains, leads, quotations, whatsapp`); catalog advertises `branches, sms/month, storage, reports` limits that are **not** enforced by `PLAN_LIMITS`/`checkPlanLimit`.
4. **Domain limits duplicated** in two route files rather than sourced from `planFeatures.js`.
5. **Fail-mode asymmetry:** count-limit & feature gates **fail closed** (503/block); WhatsApp quota **fails open** (allows send).
6. `normalizePlan` maps `unlimited`→`enterprise` and `free`/`""`→`basic` on the backend (`planFeatures.js:51-57`); pricing's `normalizePlan` (`planPricing.js:11`) does not remap aliases and keeps a live `free` price row.

Relevant absolute paths:
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/src/lib/plans.ts`
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/src/lib/subscriptionAccess.ts`
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/src/lib/planFeatures.js`
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/src/lib/planPricing.js`
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/src/middleware/subscriptionAccess.js`
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/src/middleware/auth.js` (lines 160–229)
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/src/services/whatsappService.js` (lines 21–65)
- Enforcement route files: `routes/{clients,bookings,leads,quotations,tenants,campaigns,blogs,website,whatsapp,sms,domains,tenantDomains,cron}.js`

# 10. OPTIONAL MODULES + SERVICE TYPES

# Optional / Advanced Modules & Service Types

## 1. Two independent opt-in axes

The tenant's sidebar and desks are shaped by **two orthogonal mechanisms**, both stored on the `Tenant` model and edited from one Settings screen (`src/components/ModuleSettings.tsx`):

| Axis | Tenant field(s) | What it controls | Gated by |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Advanced modules** (bundle opt-in) | `enabledModules String[]` | Whole sidebar bundles (CRM hub, Hajj ops, Visa tracker, Website builder, etc.) | Plan floor (`minPlan`, default `business`) + owner toggle. Enforced client & server side. |
| **Service focus** (service-type / subcategory selection) | `enabledServiceTypes String[]`, `enabledSubcategories String[]` | Which package-catalog tabs / operations desks are relevant; derives legacy module flags | Free-form selection; no plan floor. Empty = "all types shown". |

Legacy boolean flags `enableHajjUmrahModule` (`@default(true)`) and `enableBdOperationsModule` (`@default(false)`) still exist on the schema (`backend/prisma/schema.prisma:25-26`) and are derived from the service selection, but `NavigationOptions` marks both as **`@deprecated` and ignored** — operations desks now derive from `enabledServiceTypes` (`src/config/navigation.ts:64-67`).

---

## 2. Advanced (opt-in) module bundles

Source of truth: `src/lib/moduleAccess.ts` (`ADVANCED_MODULES`), mirrored server-side in `backend/src/lib/moduleAccess.js` (`ADVANCED_MODULE_IDS` + `MODULE_MIN_PLAN`).

Design: the **lean core menu is visible to every plan by default**; every bundle below is **OFF by default** and must be switched on in Settings by a tenant owner. Default plan floor is **`business`**; a bundle may lower its floor via `minPlan`. `autoOn` bundles are shown automatically for eligible plans (a plan feature, not a hidden toggle) and are **not** listed as a Settings switch.

| # | Bundle id | Label key | Nav item ids unlocked | `minPlan` (floor) | `autoOn` |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `crm` | `modules.crm` | `crm-hub` | business (default) | no |
| 2 | `subAgents` | `modules.subAgents` | `commissions` | business (default) | no |
| 3 | `corporate` | `modules.corporate` | `corporate`, `travel-approvals` | business (default) | no |
| 4 | `ticketing` | `modules.ticketing` | `ticket-transactions`, `flight-reminders` | business (default) | no |
| 5 | `tourGroups` | `modules.tourGroups` | `group-tours`, `mice` | business (default) | no |
| 6 | `visa` | `modules.visa` | `visa-tracker` | business (default) | no |
| 7 | `hajj` | `modules.hajj` | `hajj-operations` | business (default) | no |
| 8 | `studentManpower` | `modules.studentManpower` | `bd-operations` | business (default) | no |
| 9 | `documentsDesk` | `modules.documentsDesk` | `documents`, `service-operations` | business (default) | no |
| 10 | `hrPayroll` | `modules.hrPayroll` | `hrm`, `activity-log`, `payroll` | business (default) | no |
| 11 | `marketing` | `modules.marketing` | `loyalty`, `referrals` | business (default) | no |
| 12 | `website` | `modules.website` | `website-home`, `website-builder`, `website-blog`, `website-publish`, `website-seo` | **pro** | **yes** |

`AUTO_ON_MODULE_IDS` currently resolves to `["website"]` — Website is a Pro plan feature, so it is auto-shown for Pro+ and never rendered as a Settings toggle (`ModuleSettings.tsx:115` filters out `isModuleAutoOn(m.id)` bundles).

### Gating helper functions (`src/lib/moduleAccess.ts`)

- `PLAN_RANK = { free:0, basic:1, pro:2, business:3, enterprise:4 }`; `unlimited` is normalized to `enterprise`.
- `moduleMinPlan(bundleId)` — returns the bundle's floor, defaulting to `business`.
- `planCanUseModule(bundleId, plan)` — `planRank(plan) >= planRank(floor)`.
- `planCanUseAdvancedModules(plan)` — true if plan can activate **any** bundle (drives the "Business only" lock badge in Settings).
- `isNavItemModuleEnabled(itemId, plan, enabledModules)` — the actual render gate. Returns `true` for core items (no bundle owns them); `false` if below the bundle's floor; `true` if the bundle is `autoOn`; otherwise requires the bundle id to be present in `enabledModules`.
- `sanitizeEnabledModules(values, plan)` — keeps only known bundle ids the plan is allowed to activate (dedupes, trims).
- `isModuleAutoOn(bundleId)` / `AUTO_ON_MODULE_IDS` — auto-on set.

### Enforcement path

- **Frontend render:** `AppSidebar.tsx` passes `tenant.enabledModules` + `currentPlan` into `AppSidebarNavGroup`; `filterVisibleItems` (`AppSidebarNav.tsx:84-93`) shows an item only when `canAccess(item.module)` (RBAC) **and** `isNavItemModuleEnabled(...)` pass. Items below their `minPlan` render as a locked, tooltip'd "Upgrade to X" row (`LockedNavRow`).
- **Backend enforcement (anti-bypass):** `PATCH /tenants/me` (`backend/src/routes/tenants.js:56-63`, `requireRole("tenant_owner")`) runs `sanitizeEnabledModules(data.enabledModules, current.subscriptionPlan)` against the tenant's **real** plan before persisting, so the API cannot be used to enable a bundle the plan tier doesn't allow. Comment: "Menu-hiding on the frontend is not enough — this enforces the plan floor."

---

## 3. Service-type presets

Source of truth: `src/lib/serviceTypes.ts` (`SERVICE_TYPES` const tuple). 14 canonical types; anything unrecognized normalizes to `custom` (`normalizeServiceType`). Each has an EN + BN label (`getLocalizedServiceTypeLabel`).

| Service type | EN label | BN label |
|---|---|---|
| `hajj_umrah` | Hajj / Umrah | হজ্জ / উমরাহ |
| `tour_domestic` | Domestic Tour | ডোমেস্টিক ট্যুর |
| `tour_international` | International Tour | ইন্টারন্যাশনাল ট্যুর |
| `visa` | Visa | ভিসা |
| `air_ticket` | Air Ticket | এয়ার টিকেট |
| `hotel` | Hotel | হোটেল |
| `transport` | Transport / Car Rental | ট্রান্সপোর্ট / কার রেন্টাল |
| `cruise` | Cruise / Launch | ক্রুজ / লঞ্চ |
| `study_abroad` | Study Abroad | স্টাডি অ্যাবরড |
| `medical_tourism` | Medical Tourism | মেডিকেল ট্যুরিজম |
| `corporate_travel` | Corporate Travel | কর্পোরেট ট্রাভেল |
| `mice_event` | MICE / Event Travel | মাইস / ইভেন্ট ট্রাভেল |
| `b2b_agent` | B2B Agent / Sub-Agent | বি টু বি এজেন্ট / সাব-এজেন্ট |
| `custom` | Custom | কাস্টম |

### Nav items keyed to service types

`requiredServiceTypes` is declared on `NavItemConfig` and set on the items below (`src/config/navigation.ts`). **Important:** in the current code it is **informational only** — the render path (`AppSidebarNav.filterVisibleItems`) filters exclusively on RBAC `module` + `isNavItemModuleEnabled` (bundle gating). `requiredServiceTypes` is not read by any filter in `src/` or `backend/src/` (grep-confirmed), so these mappings document the *intended* service affinity but do not currently hide items on their own.

| Nav item | `requiredServiceTypes` | Owning module bundle |
|---|---|---|
| `hajj-operations` | `hajj_umrah` | `hajj` |
| `bd-operations` (Study/Manpower desk) | `study_abroad`, `b2b_agent` | `studentManpower` |
| `corporate` | `corporate_travel`, `mice_event` | `corporate` |
| `ticket-transactions` | `air_ticket` | `ticketing` |
| `flight-reminders` | `air_ticket` | `ticketing` |
| `group-tours` | `tour_domestic`, `tour_international`, `mice_event` | `tourGroups` |
| `mice` | `mice_event` | `tourGroups` |
| `travel-approvals` | `corporate_travel`, `mice_event` | `corporate` |
| `visa-tracker` | `visa`, `study_abroad` | `visa` |
| `commissions` | `air_ticket`, `b2b_agent`, `tour_domestic`, `tour_international`, `hajj_umrah` | `subAgents` |

### Service-type helpers (`src/lib/enabledServiceTypes.ts`)

- `ALL_SERVICE_TYPES` / `ONBOARDING_SERVICE_TYPES` — full catalog (spread of `SERVICE_TYPES`); shown in onboarding + settings.
- `getFullServiceTypesPayload()` — enables all service types + both legacy module flags (for full-service agencies).
- `normalizeEnabledServiceTypes(values)` — trims, lowercases, keeps only valid types.
- `showsAllServiceTypes(enabled, subcategories)` — true when no subcategories and `enabled` is empty ⇒ **empty selection means "show everything"**.
- `resolveEffectiveServiceTypes(...)` — if subcategories chosen, derives types from them; else uses `enabled`.
- `isServiceTypeEnabled(type, enabled, subs)` — membership test used to decide relevance.
- `presetAllowedForServiceTypes(preset, enabled)` — checks a package preset against enabled types (`single`/`multi` filter modes, see `packageRoutePresets.ts`).
- `deriveModuleFlagsFromServiceTypes(...)` — sets `enableHajjUmrahModule` when `hajj_umrah` (or "all") is present; sets `enableBdOperationsModule` when `study_abroad` or `b2b_agent` (or "all") is present.
- `filterServiceTypesForTenant(...)` — narrows a list of types to the tenant's effective set.
- `buildServiceSelectionPayload(subcategoryIds)` — the payload the Settings save sends: `{ enabledSubcategories, enabledServiceTypes, ...derivedModuleFlags }`.
