## 4. Service catalog (categories → subcategories → legacy service type)

Source of truth: `src/lib/serviceCatalog.ts`. Bangladesh travel-agency catalog: **14 top-level categories**, each with subcategories, each subcategory mapping to one legacy `ServiceType` via `legacyServiceType`. Subcategories are the granular checkboxes in `ServiceCatalogPicker`; selecting them derives both `enabledSubcategories` and `enabledServiceTypes` (`deriveServiceTypesFromSubcategories`). All categories + subcategories carry EN/BN labels.

| Category id | Label (EN) | Legacy service type | # subcats | Notable service-type spread |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `air_ticketing` | Air Ticketing | `air_ticket` | 10 | all → `air_ticket` |
| `hajj_umrah` | Hajj & Umrah | `hajj_umrah` | 11 | all → `hajj_umrah` |
| `visa_processing` | Visa Processing | `visa` | 10 | all → `visa` |
| `manpower` | Manpower & Overseas Employment | `b2b_agent` | 11 | all → `b2b_agent` |
| `hotel_accommodation` | Hotel & Accommodation | `hotel` | 8 | all → `hotel` |
| `transportation` | Transportation | `transport` | 9 | all → `transport` |
| `tour_packages` | Tour Packages | `tour_domestic` | 19 | mixes `tour_domestic`, `tour_international`, `corporate_travel` (`tour_corporate`), `medical_tourism` (`tour_medical`) |
| `cruise_holiday` | Cruise & Holiday | `cruise` | 8 | all → `cruise` |
| `study_abroad` | Study Abroad | `study_abroad` | 8 | all → `study_abroad` |
| `corporate_travel` | Corporate Travel | `corporate_travel` | 8 | mixes `mice_event` (`corp_event`), `visa` (`corp_visa`), `hotel` (`corp_hotel`) |
| `travel_documentation` | Travel Documentation | `custom` | 8 | all → `custom` |
| `event_attraction` | Event & Attraction Tickets | `mice_event` | 8 | all → `mice_event` |
| `travel_insurance` | Travel Insurance | `custom` | 7 | all → `custom` |
| `additional_services` | Additional Services | `custom` | 10 | all → `custom` |

**Cross-category service-type mapping notes** (from subcategory `legacyServiceType`):
- `tour_corporate` → `corporate_travel`; `tour_medical` → `medical_tourism`; `tour_honeymoon`/`tour_dubai`/etc. → `tour_international`; `tour_group`/`tour_educational`/`tour_adventure` → `tour_domestic`.
- `corp_event` → `mice_event`; `corp_visa` → `visa`; `corp_hotel` → `hotel`.
- `manpower` category maps entirely to `b2b_agent` (so choosing Manpower turns on the B2B / Study-Manpower desk logic).

### Agency presets (`AGENCY_PRESETS`)

One-click bundles of categories for onboarding (`getSubcategoryIdsForPreset` expands to subcategory ids):

| Preset id | Label (EN) | Categories bundled |
|---|---|---|
| `hajj_agency` | Hajj & Umrah Agency | `hajj_umrah`, `visa_processing`, `transportation` |
| `tour_agency` | Tour & Travel Agency | `tour_packages`, `hotel_accommodation`, `transportation` |
| `visa_center` | Visa Center | `visa_processing`, `travel_documentation` |
| `manpower_agency` | Manpower Agency | `manpower`, `visa_processing`, `travel_documentation` |
| `study_consultancy` | Study Abroad Consultancy | `study_abroad`, `visa_processing` |
| `full_service` | Full Service Travel Agency | **all 14 categories** |

### Catalog helpers
`getCategoryById`, `getSubcategoryIdsForCategories`, `getSubcategoryIdsForPreset`, `normalizeEnabledSubcategories` (keeps only valid subcat ids), `deriveServiceTypesFromSubcategories`, `deriveCategoryIdsFromSubcategories`, `getLocalizedCategoryLabel` / `getLocalizedSubcategoryLabel` / `getLocalizedPresetLabel` (EN/BN), `buildTenantServicePayload`. `ALL_SUBCATEGORY_IDS` = flat list of every subcategory id (used for validation).

---

## 5. Opt-in flow (end-to-end)

**A. Advanced module bundles** (`ModuleSettings.tsx`):
1. Owner (`appRole` = `tenant_owner`/`owner`) opens Settings → Modules. Non-owners see read-only ("owner only").
2. UI lists `ADVANCED_MODULES` **minus** `autoOn` bundles. Each row shows a Switch; if `planCanUseModule(id, currentPlan)` is false, the switch is disabled and a lock badge shows the floor label (`{pro:"Pro", business:"Business", enterprise:"Ultimate"}`).
3. If the whole plan can't use any advanced module (`planCanUseAdvancedModules` false), a "Business only" badge + upgrade hint show and no save button renders.
4. Toggling edits local `modules` state; "Save Modules" calls `tenantApi.update({ enabledModules })` → `PATCH /tenants/me`.
5. Backend re-sanitizes against the real plan (`sanitizeEnabledModules`) and persists to `Tenant.enabledModules`.
6. `refreshTenant()` reloads the tenant; `AppSidebar` re-derives visible nav via `isNavItemModuleEnabled`.

**B. Service focus** (same screen):
1. Owner picks subcategories in `ServiceCatalogPicker` (backed by `SERVICE_CATALOG`).
2. "Save Service Types" calls `tenantApi.update(buildServiceSelectionPayload(selectedSubs))` → sends `{ enabledSubcategories, enabledServiceTypes, enableHajjUmrahModule?, enableBdOperationsModule? }` (flags derived automatically — no separate toggle).
3. Backend `PATCH /tenants/me` normalizes: `enabledServiceTypes` lowercased/deduped; `enabledSubcategories` validated via `normalizeEnabledSubcategories`; legacy flags coerced to Boolean.
4. Empty selection ⇒ `showsAllServiceTypes` true ⇒ all catalog tabs/desks treated as relevant.

**C. Registration/onboarding** (`backend/src/routes/auth.js:203-267`): new tenants may pass `enabledSubcategories` + `enabledServiceTypes` at signup; both are normalized and stored. `enabledModules` is **not** set at registration (defaults to `[]`), so every new tenant starts on the lean core menu with all advanced bundles off (except `autoOn` Website, which appears automatically once the plan reaches Pro).

---

## Key file references
- `src/lib/moduleAccess.ts` — `ADVANCED_MODULES`, gating fns, `AUTO_ON_MODULE_IDS`.
- `backend/src/lib/moduleAccess.js` — server mirror + `sanitizeEnabledModules` enforcement.
- `src/lib/serviceTypes.ts` — 14 `SERVICE_TYPES` + localized labels.
- `src/lib/enabledServiceTypes.ts` — service-type resolution + module-flag derivation.
- `src/lib/serviceCatalog.ts` — 14 categories / ~135 subcategories, agency presets, localized labels.
- `src/config/navigation.ts` — nav tree, `requiredServiceTypes` (informational), `minPlan`, `requiredFeature`.
- `src/components/AppSidebarNav.tsx` / `AppSidebar.tsx` — render-time module + RBAC gating.
- `src/components/ModuleSettings.tsx` — the opt-in UI for both axes.
- `backend/src/routes/tenants.js` — `PATCH /tenants/me` persistence + sanitization.
- `backend/prisma/schema.prisma:25-29` — `Tenant.enableHajjUmrahModule / enableBdOperationsModule / enabledServiceTypes / enabledSubcategories / enabledModules`.

# 11. SETTINGS

# Settings

The Travel Agency ERP splits settings into two planes: **Tenant (agency) settings** — self-service configuration each agency owner manages for their own account — and **Platform (super-admin) settings** — global system config managed by the SaaS operator. Access to every settings surface is gated by the RBAC permission matrix (`src/lib/permissions.ts`) and by subscription plan (`src/lib/moduleAccess.ts`).

## Routes & Access Control

| Path | Page / Component | Route Guard | File |
|------|------------------|-------------|------|
| `/settings` | `SettingsPage` | `PM module="settings"` | `src/pages/SettingsPage.tsx` |
| `/settings/billing` | `SettingsBilling` | `PM module="subscription"` | `src/pages/SettingsBilling.tsx` |
| `/settings/tax` | `SettingsTax` (lazy) | `PM module="settings"` | `src/pages/SettingsTax.tsx` |
| `/organization` | `Organization` | `PM module="organization"` | `src/pages/Organization.tsx` |
| `/team` | `Team` | `PM module="team"` | `src/pages/Team.tsx` |
| `/roles` | `RoleManagement` | `PM module="team"` | `src/pages/RoleManagement.tsx` |
| `/crm-settings` | `CrmSettings` | `P` (authed) | `src/pages/CrmSettings.tsx` |
| `/subscription` | `SubscriptionRoute` | `P` (authed) | `src/App.tsx` |
| `/admin/settings` | `AdminSettings` | `A` (super-admin) | `src/pages/admin/AdminSettings.tsx` |
| `/admin/roles` | `AdminRoles` | `A` (super-admin) | `src/pages/admin/AdminRoles.tsx` |

---

## A. Account & System Settings (`/settings` — `SettingsPage.tsx`)

Main tenant settings page, `max-w-4xl`, composed of inline cards plus four embedded components (`ModuleSettings`, `SmtpSettings`, `WhatsAppSettings`, `DataExport`). Fetches/persists via `apiFetch` to `VITE_API_URL` (default `/api`).

- **My Profile** (all users)
  - **Full Name** — editable text; saved via `PATCH /auth/me` `{ name }`, then `refreshTenant()`.
  - **Email** — read-only/disabled ("Email cannot be changed. Contact support.").
  - Action: **Update Profile** button (disabled while saving or name empty).

- **Change Password** (all users)
  - **Current Password** (with show/hide eye toggle).
  - **New Password** ("Min 8 characters", show/hide toggle).
  - **Confirm New Password** (inline "Passwords don't match" validation, red border on mismatch).
  - Action: **Change Password** → `PATCH /auth/me` `{ currentPassword, newPassword }`; clears all three fields on success.

- **Notification Preferences** (owner-only — `appRole` is `tenant_owner`/`owner`)
  - Loads/saves at `GET|PATCH /tenants/me/notification-settings` (backend `PATCH` route requires role `tenant_owner`). Each toggle saves immediately (optimistic, reverts on error).
  - **New Web Inquiries / Leads** (`notifyOnLead`) — "when a visitor submits a booking or inquiry from your website."
  - **New Bookings & Quotations** (`notifyOnBooking`) — "when a new booking or quotation is created."
  - **Payment Received** (`notifyOnPayment`) — "when a payment is recorded against an invoice."
  - **SMS Notifications** (`smsEnabled`) — global on/off for all SMS ("requires SMS provider configured").
  - Description: "Choose which events trigger WhatsApp and SMS alerts to your agency."

- **Module Settings** — embedded `ModuleSettings` component (see Section E).

- **Billing & Subscription** — summary card with a **Manage Billing** link → `/settings/billing`.

- **SMTP Email Configuration** — embedded `SmtpSettings` (see Section F).

- **WhatsApp Notifications** — embedded `WhatsAppSettings` (see Section G).

- **Data Export / Backup** — embedded `DataExport` (see Section H).

- **Tax & VAT Rules** — summary card with a **Manage Tax Rules** link → `/settings/tax`.

---

## B. Organization / Company Profile (`/organization` — `Organization.tsx`)

Agency profile & branding. Each section saves independently via `tenantApi.update(...)` then `refreshTenant()`. Header includes a **View Website** external link (opens the tenant's public site: `https://<current-host>` or `https://app.travelagencyweb.com/site/<slug>`).

- **1. Agency Identity**
  - **Agency / Company Name** (required) — editable → `save("identity", { name })`.
  - **Website Slug (URL)** — read-only display `app.travelagencyweb.com/site/<slug>` ("Slug is set at registration. Contact support to change.").

- **2. Contact Information** → `save("contact", { phone, whatsapp, website })`
  - **Primary Phone** (placeholder `+880 1XXX-XXXXXX`).
  - **WhatsApp Number**.
  - **Agency Website** (URL).

- **3. Office Address** ("Shown on your public website and invoices") → `save("address", { address, city, country })`
  - **Street Address**.
  - **City / District**.
  - **Country** — select of 23 countries: Bangladesh, Afghanistan, Bahrain, Egypt, India, Indonesia, Jordan, Kuwait, Malaysia, Maldives, Nepal, Oman, Pakistan, Qatar, Saudi Arabia, Singapore, Sri Lanka, Thailand, Turkey, UAE, United Kingdom, United States, Other.

- **4. Currency & Timezone** ("Used across invoices, reports, and the entire ERP system") → `save("locale", { currency, timezone })`
  - **Default Currency** — 12 options: BDT, USD, EUR, GBP, SAR, AED, MYR, SGD, THB, INR, PKR, NPR.
  - **Timezone** — 18 options (Asia/Dhaka default; Karachi, Kolkata, Kathmandu, Colombo, Dubai, Riyadh, Kuwait, Qatar, Muscat, Bahrain, Kuala_Lumpur, Singapore, Bangkok, Jakarta, Europe/London, America/New_York, America/Los_Angeles).

- **5. Business Modules** (read-only status, derived from services)
  - **Hajj & Umrah Operations** — Active/Off badge, derived via `deriveModuleFlagsFromServiceTypes` (`enableHajjUmrahModule`).
  - **Student & Manpower Operations** — Active/Off badge (`enableBdOperationsModule`).
  - Note: "To change these, update your services in Settings → Optional modules."

- **6. Quick Links** — navigation shortcuts to: Website & CMS (`/website`), Domain & Publish (`/website/publish`), SEO Settings (`/website/seo`), Team Members (`/team`), Subscription & Billing (`/settings/billing`), System Settings (`/settings`).

- **7. Subscription & Plan** (read-only)
  - Current plan badge (Free/Basic/Pro/Business/Enterprise via `PLAN_LABELS`), status pill (Active/Trial), "Valid until" date.
  - **Upgrade Plan** button (hidden on enterprise) → `/settings/billing`.
  - Stat tiles: Plan, Modules (Hajj/Umrah, BD Ops, or "Core"), Custom Domain ("Enabled" on Pro/Business/Enterprise else "Pro+ only"), Email Notify ("Enabled").

---

## C. Team / Users (`/team` — `Team.tsx`)

Staff account management. Loads via `tenantApi.getMembers()`. Add/edit actions gated by `usePermissions().canManageTeam`.

- **Add Member** dialog (invite) → `tenantApi.inviteMember(email, role, name?)`
  - **Full Name** (optional), **Email Address** (required), **Role** select:
    - `manager` — "full access except billing"
    - `sales_agent` — "bookings, clients, leads" (default)
    - `accountant` — "invoices, payments, reports"
    - `operations` — "bookings, packages, vendors"
  - Explains login flow: if SMTP configured → password-setup link emailed; else → temporary password shown for manual sharing.

- **Staff Account Created** credentials dialog (post-invite)
  - Shows Login URL (`<host>/login`), Email, Password (one-time temp password *or* "sent via email"), Role.
  - **Copy Login Details** button (copies formatted block to clipboard); **Done**.
  - Amber alert if SMTP not configured (password shown once); green alert if setup link emailed.

- **Members** table — columns: Name, Email, Role, Joined, Actions.
  - **Role** cell: inline `Select` to change role (manager/sales_agent/accountant/operations) via `tenantApi.updateMember(id, { role })` — disabled for self, `tenant_owner`, `owner`, `admin` (shown as colored badge instead).
  - **Actions**: remove member (`tenantApi.removeMember`) — cannot remove self or `tenant_owner`.
  - **Refresh** button.

---

## D. Roles & Permissions (`/roles` — `RoleManagement.tsx`)

Tenant-side role management + permission-matrix viewer (read-only matrix sourced from `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS`). Role editing gated by `canManageTeam`.

- **Role summary cards** — one per tenant role (`getTenantRoles()`), showing member count and description; click opens the View-Permissions dialog.
- **Team Members tab**
  - Search (name/email) + **Filter by role** select.
  - Table: Name, Email, Role (colored badge), Status (active/invited/suspended pill), Joined, Actions.
  - Actions: **View permissions** (eye) and **Change role** (pencil).
- **Permission Matrix tab** — grid of Modules × tenant Roles; each cell shows "Full", a set of action abbreviations, or "—". Excludes `admin_panel`.
- **Change Role** dialog — shows member + current role, New Role select (with description), Update → `tenantApi.updateMember`.
- **View Permissions** dialog — per-role table of Modules × Actions (View/Create/Edit/Delete/Approve/Export) with ✓/✗.

### Roles (`ROLE_METADATA`)

| Role id | Label | Tenant role? | Description |
|---------|-------|--------------|-------------|
| `super_admin` | Super Admin | No (platform) | Full platform access; manages all tenants, plans, system settings |
| `tenant_owner` | Tenant Owner | Yes | Full access within tenant; manages team, subscription, all modules |
| `manager` | Manager | Yes | Day-to-day ops; all modules except settings/billing |
| `sales_agent` | Sales Agent | Yes | Clients, leads, bookings; limited view elsewhere |
| `accountant` | Accountant | Yes | Invoices, payments, accounts, financial reports |
| `operations` | Operations Staff | Yes | Bookings, Hajj/Umrah, vendors, tasks |

Legacy mapping (`mapLegacyRole`): `owner`/`admin` → `tenant_owner`, `member` → `sales_agent`.

### Modules & Actions

- **Modules** (`MODULE_METADATA`, 19): dashboard, clients, agents, vendors, leads, tasks, quotations, packages, bookings, invoices, accounts, reports, hajj_umrah, subscription, team, organization, settings, website, admin_panel.
- **Actions** (`ALL_ACTIONS_LIST`): view, create, edit, delete, approve, export.
- **Key gates**: `settings` module = only `tenant_owner`/`super_admin` (all actions); `manager` has `settings: NONE`; `sales_agent`/`accountant`/`operations` have `settings`, `team`, `organization`, `subscription`, `website` all `NONE` (except `manager` gets view on subscription/team/organization and view+edit on website).

---

## E. Optional / Business Modules (`ModuleSettings.tsx`, embedded in `/settings`)

Owner-only (`appRole` tenant_owner/owner) module and service configuration. Title "Optional modules" — "Show or hide features your agency does not use."

- **Advanced modules** — per-bundle switches; saved via `tenantApi.update({ enabledModules })`. Availability governed by `moduleAccess.ts`: default floor is **Business**, unless a bundle sets its own `minPlan`. Bundles below the plan floor show a lock badge with the required plan label ("Business & Ultimate"). Auto-on bundles (Website) are hidden from this list. If plan can't use advanced modules at all → upgrade hint shown.

  | Bundle id | Label | Description | Min plan | Nav items |
  |-----------|-------|-------------|----------|-----------|
  | `crm` | CRM suite | Leads, follow-ups, complaints, campaigns, CRM reports | business | crm-hub |
  | `subAgents` | Sub-agents & commission | B2B sub-agent accounts + commission tracking | business | commissions |
  | `corporate` | Corporate travel | Corporate client desk + travel-approval workflow | business | corporate, travel-approvals |
  | `ticketing` | Air ticketing extras | Ticket transactions (refunds/voids/reissues) + flight reminders | business | ticket-transactions, flight-reminders |
  | `tourGroups` | Tour & group travel | Group tours + MICE | business | group-tours, mice |
  | `visa` | Visa tracker | Visa application tracking desk | business | visa-tracker |
  | `hajj` | Hajj & Umrah operations | Pilgrim/group/installment ops desk | business | hajj-operations |
  | `studentManpower` | Student & manpower | BD ops desk for study abroad + manpower | business | bd-operations |
  | `documentsDesk` | Documents & service desk | Document library + service ops desk | business | documents, service-operations |
  | `hrPayroll` | HR & payroll | Attendance, leave, activity log, payroll | business | hrm, activity-log, payroll |
  | `marketing` | Marketing & loyalty | Loyalty program + referrals | business | loyalty, referrals |
  | `website` | Website & CMS | Public website builder, blog, SEO, publishing | **pro** (auto-on) | website-home, website-builder, website-blog, website-publish, website-seo |

- **Services you sell** (service focus) — `ServiceCatalogPicker`, saved via `tenantApi.update(buildServiceSelectionPayload(subs))` which also derives the Hajj/BD ops-desk flags automatically. "Pick the travel services your agency offers. Empty selection shows all catalog tabs (backward compatible)." Persists `enabledSubcategories` (+ derived `enabledServiceTypes`).
  - **Agency presets** (`AGENCY_PRESETS`): Hajj Agency, Tour Agency, Visa Center, Manpower Agency, Study Consultancy, Full Service.
  - **Catalog categories** (`SERVICE_CATALOG`, 14): air_ticketing, hajj_umrah, visa_processing, manpower, hotel_accommodation, transportation, tour_packages, cruise_holiday, study_abroad, corporate_travel, travel_documentation, event_attraction, travel_insurance, additional_services — each with many toggleable subcategories.

- Info note: core menu is always on for every plan; advanced modules are Business/Ultimate owner opt-in (English + Bangla).

---

## F. Email / SMTP Config (`SmtpSettings.tsx`, embedded in `/settings`)

Per-tenant SMTP for booking confirmations & invoices. Saves via `emailApi.updateSmtpConfig(config)`.

- Fields: **SMTP Host** (`smtp.gmail.com`), **Port** (default 587), **Use SSL/TLS** toggle (`secure`), **Username**, **Password**, **From Name**, **From Email**.
- **Save Configuration** button.
- **Send Test Email** — email input + **Send Test** → `emailApi.testSmtp(testEmail)`.

---

## G. WhatsApp Config (`WhatsAppSettings.tsx`, embedded in `/settings`)

Per-tenant WhatsApp notifications, plan-gated by `usePlanAccess(currentPlan).canUseWhatsApp`. If plan can't use WhatsApp → an "Upgrade required" card is shown instead. Loads `GET /whatsapp/config`.

- **Status badge**: Active (configured) / Not configured.
- **Monthly WhatsApp quota** panel (when `config.quota` present): shows `used / limit` or "Unlimited", a progress bar (turns destructive at limit), and plan-quota copy.
- **Provider** select: **WasenderAPI** (recommended), **Meta WhatsApp Business API**, **Twilio WhatsApp**, **Console** (testing).
- Provider-specific read-only status blocks:
  - **Wasender**: shows active state, Instance ID (`WASENDER_INSTANCE_ID`), and .env setup guide (`WHATSAPP_PROVIDER`, `WASENDER_API_KEY`, `WASENDER_INSTANCE_ID`).
  - **Meta**: Phone Number ID (`META_WHATSAPP_PHONE_ID`), plus `META_WHATSAPP_TOKEN`.
  - **Twilio**: From Number (`WHATSAPP_FROM_NUMBER`), plus `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID`, `TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN`.
  - (Credentials are configured server-side via `.env`, not entered here — the UI displays status only.)
- **Send Test Message** — phone input + **Test** → `POST /whatsapp/test { to }`.

---

## H. Backup & Export (`DataExport.tsx`, embedded in `/settings`)

Data export and full database backup. Uses bearer token to hit `/export/*` endpoints.

- **Export data to Excel** — one organised workbook: Summary dashboard sheet + a sheet per module (every field per record). Sheet tags shown: Summary, Clients, Leads, Vendors, Agents, Quotations, Bookings, Invoices, Payments, Expenses, Ledger, Accounts, Tasks, Complaints, Campaigns, Packages, Hajj, Visa, Tours, Support, "…every module."
  - Optional **From / To** date range.
  - **Download full Excel (all sheets)** → `GET /export/workbook`.
  - **Excel for date range** → `GET /export/workbook?from&to`.
  - **Download CSV instead** → `GET /export/csv` (all or date-ranged).
- **Full Database Backup**
  - Shows server auto-backup stats (count + last-backup timestamp) from `GET /export/backup-info`.
  - Security warning ("complete agency data — keep secure").
  - **Download Full SQL Backup** → `POST /export/db-backup` (returns `.sql` dump).

---

## I. Tax / VAT Rules (`/settings/tax` — `SettingsTax.tsx`)

Tax rates applied to invoices & quotations. CRUD via `taxRuleApi` (`list`/`create`/`update`/`delete`).

- **Active Tax Rules** table — columns: Name (default-starred), Rate (`%` or `BDT` fixed), Applies To, Status (Active/Inactive badge), edit/delete actions.
- **Add / Edit Tax Rule** dialog:
  - **Rule Name** (required, e.g. "VAT 15%, Service Tax").
  - **Type**: Percentage (%) / Fixed Amount.
  - **Rate** (numeric; label switches `(%)`/`(BDT)`).
  - **Applies To**: All Services / Domestic Tours / International Tours / Hajj/Umrah / Corporate Travel.
  - **Set as Default** toggle ("Auto-apply to new invoices").
  - **Active** toggle.

---

## J. Billing & Subscription (`/settings/billing` — `SettingsBilling.tsx`)

Plan, trial, and payment history. Reads plan meta from `src/lib/plans.PLANS`; trial math from `src/lib/trialConfig`; payments via `paymentRequestApi.list()`.

- **Trial banner** (when `isTrialActive`) — "N-Day Pro Trial Active", days-remaining copy, progress bar, **Choose a paid plan** → `/subscription`.
- **Subscription-expired banner** (when `isSubscriptionExpired`) — **Renew now** → `/subscription`.
- **Current Plan** card — plan name, status badge (from `tenant.subscriptionStatus`), `৳price/month`, renew/trial-end date, **Change plan** → `/subscription`, and the plan's first 6 features.
- **Payment History** — list of payment requests (plan/name, date, method, `৳amount`, status badge: approved/paid → default, pending → secondary, else destructive); empty state when none.

---

## K. CRM Settings (`/crm-settings` — `CrmSettings.tsx`)

CRM lists, custom fields, and automation rules. Loads via `crmSettingsApi.get()`; saves via `crmSettingsApi.update(...)`.

- **List editors** (chip add/remove):
  - **Lead sources** — where leads come from.
  - **Follow-up types** — how the team follows up.
  - **Complaint categories** — used on the complaints desk.
- **Custom fields** — extra fields on customer/lead records. Each: **Field label**, **Type** (text/number/date/select), **Applies To** (Customer/Lead). Key auto-slugged from label. Add/remove; customer fields appear on each customer profile.
- **Automation rules** (toggles):
  - **Create a task when a lead is won** (`leadWonCreateTask`) — auto-adds high-priority onboarding task to lead owner.
  - **Notify the owner on a new complaint** (`complaintNotifyOwner`) — in-app notification to agency owner.
- Single **Save changes** button persists all lists + custom fields + automations.

---

## L. Platform / Super-Admin Settings (`/admin/settings` — `AdminSettings.tsx`)

Global SaaS configuration (7 tabs), super-admin only (`AdminLayout` + `A` guard). Most tabs are largely display/env-managed (save is a stubbed toast except SMS, which persists via `smsApi.updateConfig`). Base URL `VITE_API_URL` (default `http://localhost:4000/api`).

- **General** — App Name, Support Email, Logo (URL/upload placeholder), Currency (BDT/USD/EUR/GBP/SAR/INR), Currency Symbol, Timezone (Asia/Dhaka, Kolkata, Riyadh, UTC, New_York, London), Meta Description, **Maintenance Mode** toggle.
- **Security** — **Super Admin Password Reset** (New/Confirm password, strength meter, ≥8 chars; `POST /auth/super-admin/reset-password`); **Two-Factor Authentication** info card linking to `/admin/two-factor` (Google Authenticator).
- **Email** — Enable toggle, SMTP Host/Port, SSL toggle, User, Password, From Name, From Email; Send Test Email.
- **SMS** (persists via `smsApi`) — Enable toggle, Provider (**SSL Wireless** / **BulkSMS BD**), Sender ID, API Key (read-only, env-managed `SMS_API_KEY`), Base URL; env-managed message; Send Test SMS (`smsApi.testSms`).
- **WhatsApp** — Configured badge; provider cards (**WasenderAPI** / **Meta Cloud API** / **Twilio** / **Console**) each with credential fields + `.env` var guidance (`WASENDER_API_KEY`, `META_WHATSAPP_TOKEN`/`_PHONE_ID`, `TWILIO_*`/`WHATSAPP_FROM_NUMBER`); "What sends WhatsApp messages?" event list (subscription lifecycle, payment request/approve/reject, trial drips); **Test WhatsApp Message** (`POST /whatsapp/send`).
- **Payment** — `GatewayStatusPanel`; **SSLCommerz** (Enable, Store ID, Store Password, Sandbox toggle); **bKash** (Enable, App Key, App Secret, Username, Password, Sandbox toggle); **Manual Payment** (Enable, Instructions textarea).
- **Domain** — Main Domain, Subdomain Prefix, Default Subdomain pattern (`{company}.travelagencyweb.com`), **SSL Enabled** toggle, **Custom Domain Enabled** toggle, DNS setup guide (A/CNAME records).

## M. Platform Role & Permission Management (`/admin/roles` — `AdminRoles.tsx`)

Super-admin permission-matrix editor across all 6 roles × all modules × 6 actions, initialised from `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS`. Bilingual (EN/BN). Per-role summary cards with permission counts, per-role **Reset to defaults**, tabbed editors with per-cell Switches, and a full-permission View dialog. **Save is preview-only** — the live matrix is code-managed in `src/lib/permissions.ts` (an `AdminConfigNotice` states this). `super_admin` permissions are locked (cannot toggle).


---

# 12 & 13. REPORTS + DASHBOARD WIDGETS

# Dashboards, Widgets & Reports

Two source layers power this area: the backend `GET /api/dashboard/stats` endpoint (`backend/src/routes/dashboard.js`) plus report endpoints in `backend/src/routes/finance.js` and `backend/src/routes/crmAnalytics.js`; and the frontend pages `Dashboard.tsx`, `Reports.tsx`, `FinancialStatements.tsx`, `SalesAnalytics.tsx`, `CrmAnalytics.tsx`, `FinanceReminders.tsx` plus six report sub-components under `src/components/reports/`.

## Backend metric endpoint — `GET /api/dashboard/stats`

`backend/src/routes/dashboard.js` (permission `dashboard:view`). Returns a **base** payload to everyone; **finance-sensitive** fields are added only for roles in `super_admin, tenant_owner, manager, accountant`.

| Field (base payload) | Definition / source (line) |
|---|---|
| `totalUsers` | `prisma.user.count` for tenant (L27, L80) |
| `totalClients` | `prisma.client.count` (L16, L81) |
| `totalBookings` | length of all tenant bookings (L82) |
| `recentBookings` | latest 5 bookings, `clientName` joined (L32–35, L83) |
| `activeLeads` | leads not in `won`/`lost` (L30) |
| `followUpsDueToday` | leads with `nextFollowUp <= today`, still open (L31) |
| `quotationsSentThisMonth` | quotations `status=sent` created this month (L43) |
| `quotationsAwaitingApproval` | all quotations `status=sent` (L44) |
| `confirmedBookings` | bookings `status=confirmed` (L36) |
| `upcomingDepartures` | bookings with `travelDateFrom` in next 7 days (L37) |
| `topDestinations` | top 5 destinations by booking count (L47–49) |
| `bookingsByType` | booking counts grouped by `type`, desc (L51–58) |
| `openSupportTickets` | `supportTicket.count` status in `open/assigned/in_progress` (L60–62) |
| `passportsExpiringSoon` | clients with `passportExpiry` within next 30 days (L64–76) |
| `passportsExpired` | clients whose `passportExpiry < today` (L75) |
| `totalRevenue` *(finance only)* | sum of `invoice.paidAmount` (L102) |
| `recentPayments` *(finance only)* | latest 10 payments (L24, L103) |
| `overdueInvoices` *(finance only)* | count of overdue invoices (L38, L104) |
| `overdueInvoiceAmount` *(finance only)* | sum of `dueAmount` on overdue invoices (L105) |
| `vendorDues` *(finance only)* | sum of `dueAmount` on unpaid/partial/overdue vendor bills (L42, L106) |
| `salesThisMonth` *(finance only)* | sum of payment amounts dated this month (L39–41, L107) |

`Dashboard.tsx` has a **client-side fallback** (L166–241): if `/dashboard/stats` fails it recomputes every stat from individual list endpoints (members, clients, bookings, payments, leads, quotations, invoices, vendor payable report).

---

## Dashboard Widgets

`src/pages/Dashboard.tsx`. Rendered top-to-bottom. Currency shown as `৳` (BDT). All page copy is i18n (English/Bengali).

| # | Widget / Card | Type | What it shows | Data source | Click / action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Onboarding Checklist** | Component | Guided setup steps for new tenants | `<OnboardingChecklist>` (L269) | — |
| 2 | **Your Plan / Usage** | Card w/ 3 progress bars | Plan name + status badge; usage of **Clients**, **Bookings**, **Team members** vs plan limits (amber at ≥80%) | `getPlan(tenant.subscriptionPlan)` limits vs `stats` counts (L271–315) | "Manage / Upgrade plan" → `/subscription` |
| 3 | **Welcome empty-state** | Card (conditional) | Shown when no bookings/clients/leads; quick-start buttons | `hasAnyData` flag (L253, L317–335) | Add Lead / Setup catalog / Create quotation / Collect payments |
| 4 | **Active Leads** | KPI card | `activeLeads`; subtitle "N follow-ups due" (orange) | `stats.activeLeads`, `followUpsDueToday` (L338–347) | → `/leads` |
| 5 | **Quotations Sent** | KPI card | `quotationsSentThisMonth`; subtitle "N awaiting approval" | `stats.quotationsSentThisMonth`, `quotationsAwaitingApproval` (L348–356) | → `/quotations` |
| 6 | **Confirmed Bookings** | KPI card | `confirmedBookings` | `stats.confirmedBookings` (L357–364) | → `/bookings` |
| 7 | **Upcoming Departures** | KPI card | `upcomingDepartures`; subtitle "Next 7 days" | `stats.upcomingDepartures` (L365–373) | → `/operations/services?desk=departures` |
| 8 | **Sales This Month** | KPI card (finance) | `৳salesThisMonth` | `stats.salesThisMonth` (L377–383) | — |
| 9 | **Overdue Invoices** | KPI card (finance) | `overdueInvoices` count; subtitle "৳outstanding" (red) | `stats.overdueInvoices`, `overdueInvoiceAmount` (L384–393) | → `/invoices` |
| 10 | **Vendor Dues** | KPI card (finance) | `৳vendorDues` | `stats.vendorDues` (L394–401) | → `/vendors` |
| 11 | **Total Revenue** | KPI card (finance) | `৳totalRevenue` | `stats.totalRevenue` (L402–408) | — |
| 12 | **Inquiries to Follow Up** | Component | Open inquiry bookings split into "Call today (due)", "Needs a follow-up date", "Scheduled later"; per-row WhatsApp link, snooze (Tomorrow/3d/1wk/custom date + note), Convert-to-booking | `<InquiryFollowUpWidget>` → `bookingApi.getFollowUps()` (L411; component `src/components/InquiryFollowUpWidget.tsx`) | Row → `/bookings/:id`; "View all inquiries" → `/bookings?status=inquiry` |
| 13 | **Open Support Tickets** | KPI card | `openSupportTickets`; subtitle "View support" | `stats.openSupportTickets` (L414–422) | → `/support` |
| 14 | **Passports Expiring Soon** | KPI card | `passportsExpiringSoon`; subtitle "N expired" (red) or window text | `stats.passportsExpiringSoon`, `passportsExpired` (L423–437) | → `/clients?filter=passport` |
| 15 | **Recent Bookings** | Table (5 rows) | Columns: Booking (title/client/type), Destination, Amount `৳`, Status badge, Travel Date | `stats.recentBookings` (L440–486) | Row → `/bookings/:id`; "View all" → `/bookings` |
| 16 | **Top Destinations** | Ranked bar list | Top destinations by booking count with proportional Progress bars + count badge | `stats.topDestinations` (L489–516) | — |
| 17 | **Bookings by Type** | Ranked bar list | Booking counts per type (visa/flight/hotel/etc.), i18n type labels, Progress bars | `stats.bookingsByType` (L518–547) | — |
| 18 | **Recent Payments** | Table (finance) | Columns: Method, Amount `৳` (green), Reference (`transactionRef`), Date | `stats.recentPayments` (L552–585) | "View all" → `/payments` |
| 19 | **Quick Summary** | Card w/ 4 mini-stats + action hub | MiniStats: Total Clients, Total Bookings, Team Members, Follow-ups Today (highlighted); plus **Service Hub** quick-action buttons | `stats` + `quickActions` array (L587–618) | Quick actions: Leads, Packages & Services, up-to-4 dynamic service shortcuts (visa/flight/hotel/transport/insurance based on tenant enabled services, L79–96), Quotations (new), Bookings, Payments, Publish & Domain |
| — | **Refresh** | Header button | Re-fetches `dashboardApi.getStats()` | (L263–266) | — |

Reusable renderers: `WidgetCard` (KPI card w/ icon, value, optional colored subtitle, optional onClick; L625–662) and `MiniStat` (L664–674).

---

## Reports

### A. Reports hub — `src/pages/Reports.tsx`

- **Access gate:** wrapped in `<FeatureGate featureId="advanced_analytics">` — locked plans see an upsell with 7 feature bullets and an Upgrade button (L124–150).
- **Global filters** (L162–193): From-date & To-date pickers (default = last 3 months → today), **Staff/Agent** dropdown (auto-populated from lead/booking assignees + agents), **Status** dropdown (all/pending/confirmed/ticketed/completed/cancelled), Reset. Data fetched once via `Promise.allSettled` over leads, bookings, invoices, payments, vendor payable report, quotations, expenses, team members (L63–78); filtering is client-side by date range + agent + status.
- **6 tabbed reports.** Each has a per-report **CSV export** button gated by `PermissionGate module="reports" action="export"`.

| Report (tab) | Component | KPI cards | Charts | Table | CSV export filename |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Sales** | `SalesReport.tsx` | Total Sales `৳`, Total Bookings, Avg Value `৳` | Monthly Revenue (area), Sales by Type (pie), Bookings by Status (pie), Top Destinations (ranked bars, top 10) | Booking Details (first 20: client, type, destination, amount, status) | `sales-report.csv` (Date, Type, Client, Destination, Amount, Status) |
| **Leads & Quotations** | `LeadsQuotationReport.tsx` | Lead: Total, Won, Lost, Conversion %. Quote: Total, Approved, Total Value `৳`, Approval % | Leads by Stage (pie), Conversion by Source (bar total vs won) | Quotation Status Breakdown (status, count, total value, % of value) | `leads-report.csv` (Name, Phone, Source, Destination, Status, Assigned To) |
| **Payments** | `PaymentReport.tsx` | Total Invoiced `৳`, Collected `৳` (green), Outstanding `৳` (orange), Overdue amount + count (red) | Monthly Collected vs Due (bar), Payments by Method (horizontal bar) | Overdue Invoices (invoice #, client, total, paid, due, due date) | `payment-report.csv` (Invoice #, Client, Total, Paid, Due, Status, Due Date) |
| **Vendors** | `VendorReport.tsx` | Total Bills `৳`, Paid to Vendors `৳` (green), Outstanding `৳` (orange), Overdue amount + count (red) | Payable by Vendor (stacked bar paid+due, top 10) | Vendor Bill Details (first 20: vendor, description, total, paid, due, status, due date) | `vendor-report.csv` (Vendor, Booking, Description, Total, Paid, Due, Status, Due Date) |
| **Staff Performance** | `StaffPerformanceReport.tsx` | — | Staff Comparison (bar: leads vs bookings, top 10) | Detailed per-staff table (leads, won, conversion %, quotations, approved, bookings, revenue `৳`) sorted by revenue | `staff-performance.csv` (Staff, Leads, Won Leads, Conversion %, Quotations, Approved, Bookings, Revenue) |
| **Profitability** | `ProfitabilityReport.tsx` | Revenue `৳`, Vendor Costs `৳`, Expenses `৳`, Gross Profit `৳` (green/red), Margin % | Monthly Comparison (bar: revenue, total costs, profit) | Booking Profitability (first 25: booking, client, type, revenue, cost, profit ▲▼, margin %, status), rows link to `/bookings/:id` | `profitability-report.csv` (Booking, Client, Type, Revenue, Cost, Profit, Margin %, Status) |

Data source for all six is the client-side filtered arrays; profitability net profit = `revenue − booking.cost − expenses` (frontend `useMemo`).

### B. Financial Statements — `src/pages/FinancialStatements.tsx`

Four tabs; date range fixed to last 3 months → today; each tab hits a `finance.js` endpoint (permission `reports:view`). Currency `৳` with 2 decimals. **No export button** (print/screen only).

| Tab | Endpoint | KPI cards | Detail |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P&L** | `GET /api/finance/pl` | Total Revenue, Cost of Sales, Gross Profit, Net Profit | Income Statement: Revenue (payments received) → less Tax Collected → Net Revenue → less Vendor/Cost of Sales → Gross Profit → Operating Expenses (broken down by category) → Net Profit/(Loss). Source: `payment`, `expense` (approved/paid), `vendorBillPayment`, `invoice.taxAmount` (finance.js L117–183) |
| **Balance Sheet** | `GET /api/finance/balance-sheet` | Total Assets, Total Liabilities, Net Equity | Assets: per cash/bank account balances + total, Accounts Receivable (due invoices). Liabilities: Accounts Payable (vendor bills due). Equity = Assets − Liabilities. Source: `account` (active cash/bank), unpaid invoices, unpaid vendor bills (finance.js L186–221) |
| **Cash Flow** | `GET /api/finance/cash-flow` | Total Inflows, Total Outflows, Net Cash | Monthly table (Month, Inflows, Outflows, Net). Inflows = payments; Outflows = expenses + vendor bill payments, grouped by `YYYY-MM` (finance.js L224–272) |
| **Tax Report** | `GET /api/finance/tax-report` | Total Revenue (ex-tax), Tax Collected, Invoices with Tax (count) | Per-invoice table (invoice #, client, date, sub-total, tax rate badge, tax, total) for paid/partial invoices with `taxAmount > 0` (finance.js L275–312) |

### C. Sales Analytics — `src/pages/SalesAnalytics.tsx`

Endpoint `GET /api/finance/sales-analytics?from&to` (`reports:view`, finance.js L317–427). From/To date inputs (default last 3 months), Apply button. **No export button.**

- **Summary cards:** Total Bookings, Total Revenue `৳`, Total Profit `৳` (green), Avg Booking Value `৳`.
- **Conversion Funnel** (6 stages, horizontal → arrows): Leads → Leads Converted (+rate %) → Quotations → Quotes Accepted (+rate %) → Bookings → Confirmed. (Leads converted = status `converted`/`booked`; quotes accepted = `accepted`/`booked`; confirmed = `confirmed`/`completed`.)
- **Top Destinations** table (destination, bookings, revenue, profit; top 10 by revenue).
- **Agent Performance** table (agent, bookings, revenue, profit; joined via `booking.agent`).
- **Monthly Trend** table (month, bookings, revenue).
- **Service Breakdown** table (serviceType, bookings, revenue).

### D. CRM Analytics — `src/pages/CrmAnalytics.tsx`

Endpoint `GET /api/crm-analytics?from&to` (permission `clients:view`, crmAnalytics.js). From/To date inputs (optional) + Clear. **No export button.**

- **Metric cards:** Total leads, Won (+conversion %), Lost (+lost %), Repeat customers (repeat rate %, "N of M").
- **Lead Funnel** (bars): counts + % across statuses new/contacted/qualified/quoted/won/lost.
- **Lead Source ROI** (bars): per source — total, won, conversion %; `value` accrues from won-lead budgets server-side.
- **Executive Performance** (bars): per assignee — won/total, conversion %.
- **Service & Engagement** (4 mini-metrics): Complaints (open/resolved), Avg satisfaction (★ from `complaint.rating`), Campaigns count, Messages sent (`campaign.sentCount`).

Data source: `lead`, `user`, `booking` (retention via repeat `clientId`), `complaint`, `campaign` (crmAnalytics.js L19–96).

### E. Finance Reminders — `src/pages/FinanceReminders.tsx`

Endpoint `GET /api/finance/reminders` (`invoices:view`, finance.js L11–74; 7-day horizon). Action endpoint `POST /api/finance/reminders/:invoiceId/send` (`invoices:edit`) dispatches the `invoice_reminder` automation. **No export button.**

- **4 stat cards:** Overdue Invoices, Due-Soon Invoices, Overdue Installments, Due-Soon Installments (counts).
- **Overdue Invoices** table (invoice #, client, due date, due amount `৳`) with per-row **View** (→ `/invoices?open=id`) and **Send reminder** buttons.
- **Overdue Installments** table (installment label, invoice, due date, remaining = `amount − paidAmount`) with **View** button. Source: `invoiceInstallment` pending/partial with due date.
- Footer links to `/commissions` and `/accounts`.

---

## Cross-cutting notes

- **Currency:** all monetary values render as `৳` (Bangladeshi Taka), `toLocaleString`.
- **Export capability:** the 6 Reports-hub sub-reports each export **CSV** client-side (Blob download), gated by `reports:export` permission. Financial Statements, Sales Analytics, CRM Analytics, and Finance Reminders have **no export/print** button (screen-only). (A separate multi-sheet Excel workbook export exists at `backend/src/routes/dataExport.js`, outside these report pages.)
- **Permission/plan gating:** dashboard finance widgets are role-restricted server-side (`super_admin/tenant_owner/manager/accountant`); the entire Reports hub is behind `advanced_analytics` FeatureGate; finance report endpoints require `reports:view`; CRM analytics requires `clients:view`.
- **File paths:** dashboard backend `backend/src/routes/dashboard.js`; report backends `backend/src/routes/finance.js`, `backend/src/routes/crmAnalytics.js`; pages `src/pages/{Dashboard,Reports,FinancialStatements,SalesAnalytics,CrmAnalytics,FinanceReminders}.tsx`; report components `src/components/reports/{Sales,LeadsQuotation,Payment,Vendor,StaffPerformance,Profitability}Report.tsx`; dashboard widget component `src/components/InquiryFollowUpWidget.tsx`.


---

# 14. AUTOMATIONS / CRON / BACKGROUND JOBS / NOTIFICATIONS

# Automation, Cron & Background Jobs + Notification System

This system has **no in-process scheduler** (no `node-cron`, `node-schedule`, `setInterval`, or `CronJob` anywhere in the backend). All scheduled work is triggered by **external OS cron jobs**: application-logic jobs are HTTP endpoints under `/api/cron/*` protected by a shared secret, and infrastructure jobs (DB/uploads backup) are shell scripts run directly from the host crontab.

## 1. Job Trigger Architecture

### 1.1 External cron → HTTP endpoints (`/api/cron/*`)
Mounted in `backend/src/app.js:187` as `app.use("/api/cron", require("./routes/cron"))`.

- **Auth:** `router.use(verifyCronSecret)` in `backend/src/routes/cron.js:15-22`. There is **no JWT** — protection is a shared secret only.
  - Secret read from `process.env.CRON_SECRET` (`cron.js:13`).
  - Accepted via header `x-cron-secret` **or** query param `?secret=` (`cron.js:16`).
  - Returns **500** if `CRON_SECRET` is unset or shorter than 8 chars (`cron.js:17`); **401** on mismatch (`cron.js:18`).
- **How it is invoked in production:** an OS-level cron (or Coolify/host scheduler) issues an authenticated `POST` to these endpoints. Documented in `docs/api-endpoints.md:31` (`/api/cron/*` (CRON_SECRET header) — subscription-expiry, reminders), `docs/Travel-Agency-ERP-Full-Audit-Improvement-Plan.md:567`, and env is provisioned via `CRON_SECRET` in `app/docker-compose.yml:54`, `app/.env.example:22`.

### 1.2 Host crontab → shell scripts (infrastructure)
- **DB backup**: `scripts/backup-db.sh`, crontab line documented in the script header and `docs/backup-restore-guide.md:6`: `0 2 * * * root /srv/travelagencyweb/scripts/backup-db.sh` (2 AM daily).
- **Uploads backup**: `scripts/backup-uploads.sh` (plus `vps/backup/nightly.sh`, `vps/templates/scripts/backup.sh`).

## 2. Scheduled Cron Jobs (application logic)

| Endpoint | Method | Auth | Purpose / Sub-jobs | Schedule (intended) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `/api/cron/process-expiry` | POST | `x-cron-secret` | Master expiry+reminder pipeline — runs **6 sub-jobs** (see 2.1) | Daily (implied) |
| `/api/cron/nightly-report` | POST | `x-cron-secret` | Daily WhatsApp end-of-day summary to each tenant owner | 11 PM Bangladesh time (UTC+6) per file header `cron.js:200` |

`/api/cron/process-expiry` (`cron.js:35-198`) returns a single JSON summary aggregating all sub-jobs: `{ processed, remindersSent, scheduledActivated, trialDrip, passportAlerts, travelReminders, total, totalScheduled, totalExpiringSoon, timestamp }`.

### 2.1 Sub-jobs inside `/api/cron/process-expiry`

| # | Sub-job | Source | What it does | Idempotency guard |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Expiring-soon reminders** | `cron.js:44-92` | Finds tenants with `subscriptionExpiry` between now and now+3 days, status `active`/`trial`; fires `notifyEvent("subscription_expiring")` to owner + super admin | `auditLog` action `subscription_expiring_reminder_sent` scoped to start-of-day (one/day) |
| 2 | **Auto-expire past-due tenants** | `cron.js:94-106` → `subscriptionExpiryService.expireTenantAndNotify` | Finds tenants `subscriptionExpiry < now` & status `active`/`trial`; sets status `expired`, fires `trial_expired` or `subscription_expired` | Status change + `auditLog` `auto_expired` |
| 3 | **Trial drip** | `cron.js:108-111` → `trialDripService.processTrialDrips` | Day-1 / Day-2 / last-day onboarding messages (see 2.2) | `auditLog` per drip action |
| 4 | **Passport expiry alerts** | `cron.js:113-116` → `passportExpiryAlertService.processPassportExpiryAlerts` | Client passport-renewal reminders (see 2.3) | `auditLog` `passport_expiry_alert_{daysLeft}` |
| 5 | **Travel departure reminders** | `cron.js:118-121` → `travelDepartureReminderService.processTravelDepartureReminders` | Pre-trip reminders (see 2.4) | `auditLog` `departure_reminder_{daysLeft}` |
| 6 | **Scheduled subscription activation** | `cron.js:123-181` | Activates `subscription` rows with `status:"scheduled"` and `startDate <= now`; updates tenant plan/status/expiry, writes `subscriptionHistory` (`scheduled_activation_applied`) + `auditLog` (`scheduled_subscription_activated`), fires `notifyEvent("subscription_activated")` | Subscription `status` flips scheduled→active |

Each sub-job is wrapped in `.catch()` so one failing job never aborts the pipeline (`cron.js:108-121`).

### 2.2 Trial Drip (`trialDripService.js`)
- Gated by `TRIAL_DRIP_ENABLED` (default `true`; `trialDripService.js:43`).
- Iterates tenants with `subscriptionStatus:"trial"`.
- `DRIP_CONFIG` (`trialDripService.js:28-32`):
  - `day1` → trialDayNumber == 1 → event `trial_drip_day1`, audit `trial_drip_day1_sent`
  - `day2` → trialDayNumber == 2 → event `trial_drip_day2`, audit `trial_drip_day2_sent`
  - `last` → `daysUntilExpiry` == 0 or 1 → event `trial_drip_last`, audit `trial_drip_last_sent`
- **Idempotent forever** (not per-day): `wasDripSent` checks any historical `auditLog` for the action (`trialDripService.js:34-40`) — each drip fires exactly once per tenant.
- Skips tenants with neither email nor phone.

### 2.3 Passport Expiry Alerts (`passportExpiryAlertService.js`)
- Gated by `PASSPORT_ALERT_ENABLED` (disable with `="false"`; line 36).
- `ALERT_THRESHOLDS = [30, 14, 7, 3, 1]` days before `passportExpiry` (line 4).
- Scans all clients with a non-null `passportExpiry`; skips expired/out-of-window.
- Fires `notifyEvent("passport_expiry_alert")` (client SMS/email/WhatsApp) **and** `notifyTenantStaff` (in-app to whole tenant).
- Idempotent per-threshold-per-day via `auditLog` `passport_expiry_alert_{daysLeft}` (module `clients`).

### 2.4 Travel Departure Reminders (`travelDepartureReminderService.js`)
- Gated by `TRAVEL_REMINDER_ENABLED` (disable with `="false"`; line 30).
- `DEPARTURE_THRESHOLDS = [3, 2, 1]` days before `travelDateFrom` (line 4).
- Scans bookings with status in `confirmed, ticketed, traveling, pending`.
- Fires `notifyEvent("travel_departure_reminder")` (client) + `notifyTenantStaff` (in-app) + `auditLog` `departure_reminder_{daysLeft}` (module `bookings`).
- Also exposed for manual/on-demand runs via the `flightReminders` route (see 2.6).

### 2.5 Nightly Report / Daily Summary + "Auto-backup" line (`cron.js:200-356`)
- Endpoint `/api/cron/nightly-report`; header comment states **11 PM BD time (UTC+6)**.
- Builds a WhatsApp "End of Day Report" per tenant (active/trial) and sends via `whatsappService.sendWhatsApp`.
- **Feature-gated**: only tenants where `planHasFeature(plan, "hasWhatsApp")` is true (`cron.js:232`) and who have a WhatsApp/phone contact.
- Per-tenant stats queried in parallel (`cron.js:237-287`): today's bookings (+ yesterday for trend arrow 📈/📉/➡️), new clients today + total clients, today's payments received (`payment.aggregate`), total outstanding due (`invoice.aggregate`), open inquiries, overdue follow-ups, unpaid invoices, departures in next 3 days.
- Message includes an **"Action Needed"** alert block, a **subscription expiry warning** (≤7 days / expired), and a literal **"🔒 Auto-backup done — your data is safely backed up today."** line (`cron.js:333`). Note: this is a **message string only** — the actual DB backup is the separate host-crontab `backup-db.sh` job, not performed by this endpoint.
- Response: `{ sent, total, results, date }`.

### 2.6 On-demand / manual reminder route (`flightReminders.js`)
Mounted `app.js:158` as `/api/flight-reminders` (JWT-authenticated, tenant-scoped):

| Endpoint | Method | Role | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/api/flight-reminders/upcoming?days=N` | GET | any auth | Lists upcoming departures (≤30 days), with `reminderSentToday` flag (matches `departure_reminder_{daysLeft}` or `departure_reminder_manual`) |
| `/api/flight-reminders/send/:bookingId` | POST | any auth | Manually sends one departure reminder; fires `notifyEvent("travel_departure_reminder")` + `notifyTenantStaff`; audit `departure_reminder_manual` |
| `/api/flight-reminders/run-now` | POST | `super_admin`, `tenant_owner` | Runs the full `processTravelDepartureReminders` batch immediately (manual trigger of the cron sub-job) |

## 3. Two Distinct Notification Layers

There are **two separate automation systems** in the backend, plus a legacy in-memory frontend engine.

### 3.1 Global event dispatcher — `notificationService.notifyEvent` (backend)
Event-based fan-out to **email + SMS + WhatsApp**, fire-and-forget via `Promise.allSettled`; errors logged never thrown (`notificationService.js:20-33`). Registered handlers in `EVENT_HANDLERS` (`notificationService.js:285-631`):

| Event | Channels & recipients | Trigger source |
|---|---|---|
| `agency_signup` | Owner email + owner SMS; super-admin email + super-admin SMS | `telegramService.notifyNewSignup` (`telegramService.js:68`) |
| `booking_confirmed` | Client email (booking confirmation) + SMS + WhatsApp | via `notifyEvent` API |
| `payment_received` | Client invoice email + SMS | `paymentGateway.js:225` |
| `subscription_order_alert` | Super-admin email+SMS, owner email+SMS+WhatsApp | `subscriptionNotificationService.js:113` |
| `subscription_activated` | Owner email + SMS + WhatsApp (immediate vs. scheduled variants) | `cron.js:172`, `subscriptionNotificationService.js:168`, `paymentGateway.js:171` |
| `subscription_expiring` | Owner email+SMS+WhatsApp, super-admin email+SMS | `cron.js:65` |
| `trial_expired` | Owner email+SMS+WhatsApp (`sendRenewalExpiryChannels`) + super-admin email+SMS | `subscriptionExpiryService.js:73` |
| `subscription_expired` | Same as trial_expired (wasTrial=false) | `subscriptionExpiryService.js:73` |
| `invoice_reminder` | Client email + SMS + WhatsApp | `notifyEvent` API |
| `passport_expiry_alert` | Client SMS + email + WhatsApp (Bn/En variants) | `passportExpiryAlertService.js:78` |
| `travel_departure_reminder` | Client SMS + email + WhatsApp (Bn/En variants) | `travelDepartureReminderService.js:78`, `flightReminders.js:95` |
| `trial_drip_day1` / `day2` / `last` | Owner email + SMS (WhatsApp drip **disabled** — `notificationService.js:281`) | `trialDripService.js:86` |
| `password_reset` | Registered but no-op (handled directly by `emailService.sendPasswordReset`) | — |

- Super-admin alert targets default to `SUPER_ADMIN_ALERT_PHONE` (`+8801674533303`) and `SUPER_ADMIN_ALERT_EMAIL` (`bditengineer@gmail.com`), overridable via env (`notificationService.js:13-14`).
- Bengali/English language toggles per job via env: `TRIAL_EXPIRY_SMS_LANG`, `PASSPORT_ALERT_SMS_LANG`, `TRAVEL_REMINDER_SMS_LANG` (`="bn"` selects Bn templates).
- This layer does **not** persist to `NotificationDelivery`; it relies on the channel services' own logs (`SmsLog`, `WhatsAppLog`).

### 3.2 Tenant-scoped automation — `tenantAutomationService.dispatchTenantAutomation` (backend)
Per-tenant, configurable **in-app + SMS only** (never throws to HTTP handlers; `tenantAutomationService.js:1-3`).

- **`AUTOMATION_EVENTS`** (`tenantAutomationService.js:11`): `lead_created`, `booking_created`, `payment_received`, `quotation_sent`.
- **Master toggles** (on `SmsSettings`) map events → a boolean (`EVENT_MASTER_TOGGLE`, lines 13-18): `notifyOnLead`, `notifyOnBooking` (also gates `quotation_sent`), `notifyOnPayment`.
- **Per-event channel rows** in `NotificationAutomation` with `sms` + `inApp` booleans; auto-seeded via `ensureTenantSettings` (`tenantAutomationService.js:75-98`).
- **Dispatch logic** (`dispatchTenantAutomation`, lines 286-347): checks master toggle → for `inApp`, creates `Notification` + `NotificationDelivery`; for `sms`, requires `settings.smsEnabled` + `isSmsEnvConfigured()` + a phone, then creates `SmsLog` + `NotificationDelivery`. If SMS env not configured but requested, records a **failed** delivery ("SMS environment not configured").
- **Trigger points** (route handlers):

| Event | Called from |
|---|---|
| `lead_created` | `backend/src/routes/leads.js:91` |
| `booking_created` | `backend/src/routes/bookings.js:316` |
| `payment_received` | `backend/src/routes/invoices.js:147` |
| `quotation_sent` | `backend/src/routes/quotations.js:133` |
| `invoice_reminder` | `backend/src/routes/finance.js:85` (note: uses `dispatchTenantAutomation`, though not in `AUTOMATION_EVENTS`, so it is a no-op) |

- **SMS message resolution** (`resolveSmsMessage`, lines 219-259): looks up active `smsTemplate` (tenant-specific or global `tenantId:null`) by type (`custom`/`booking`/`payment`), renders `{{var}}` placeholders via `buildVariableMap`, with hardcoded fallbacks per event.
- **In-app title/message** built by `buildInAppTitle`/`buildInAppMessage`; notification `link` mapped per event (`/leads`, `/bookings`, `/invoices`, `/quotations`).
- Uses its own `PrismaClient` instance (line 9), separate from the auth middleware's.

### 3.3 Legacy frontend engine — `src/lib/notificationEngine.ts` + `smsAutomation.ts`
- **In-memory only, demo/simulation** — `deliveryLog` is an in-memory array (`notificationEngine.ts:59`), with `seedDeliveryLog()` producing hardcoded sample deliveries (lines 254-268). Email channel is **simulated** ("For demo we simulate success", line 189-190); `retryDelivery` uses `Math.random()` (line 228).
- Event types (`NotificationEventType`, lines 8-15): `lead_assigned`, `quotation_sent`, `quotation_approved`, `booking_confirmed`, `payment_received`, `payment_overdue`, `subscription_expiring`. Channels: `sms` | `email` only.
- `EVENT_CONFIGS` (lines 48-56) define default SMS/email enablement per event and `smsTemplateType` mapping (`booking`/`payment`/`reminder`).
- `smsAutomation.ts` (`triggerSmsAutomation`) fetches active templates via `smsTemplateApi`, renders `{{var}}` via `renderTemplate`, and sends through the backend `smsApi.send`. Convenience wrappers: `sendBookingSms`, `sendPaymentSms`, `sendReminderSms`.
- This is the client-side legacy layer; the authoritative persistence lives in the backend (`NotificationDelivery`, `SmsLog`, `WhatsAppLog`).

## 4. Notification Channels (delivery services)

| Channel | Service | Providers (env `*_PROVIDER`) | Fallback | Persistence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Email** | `emailService.js` | Nodemailer SMTP (`SMTP_HOST/PORT/USER/PASS/SECURE/FROM`) | `console.log` if `SMTP_HOST` unset (`emailService.js:41-52`) | none |
| **SMS** | `smsService.js` | `bulksmsbd` (BulkSMSBD), `twilio`, `console` | `console` (returns success) | `SmsLog` (written by tenantAutomationService) |
| **WhatsApp** | `whatsappService.js` | `twilio`, `meta` (Cloud API v18.0), `wasender` (WasenderAPI), `console` | `console` | `WhatsAppLog` (always written via `_saveLog`) |
| **Telegram** | `telegramService.js` | Telegram Bot API (`TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` + `TELEGRAM_ADMIN_CHAT_ID`) | skipped if unconfigured | none |
| **In-app** | `Notification` model via `tenantAutomationService` / `staffInAppNotification.js` | DB | — | `Notification` + `NotificationDelivery` |
| **Platform (super-admin)** | `platformNotificationService.js` | DB | — | `PlatformNotification` |

### 4.1 SMS specifics (`smsService.js`)
- **BulkSMSBD** (`sendViaBulkSmsBD`, lines 116-162): GET to `{SMS_BASE_URL}/smsapi` (default `http://bulksmsbd.net/api`); needs `SMS_API_KEY` + `SMS_SENDER_ID`; success = response code `202`; error-code map `BULKSMSBD_ERRORS` (1001–1032). Number normalized to `88017XXXXXXXX` via `toBulkSmsNumber`.
- **Twilio** (`sendViaTwilio`, lines 85-114): POST to Messages API; needs `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID/AUTH_TOKEN/FROM_NUMBER`.
- **Balance check** `getSmsBalance` (bulksmsbd only, lines 165-210) — surfaced at `GET /api/sms/balance` (super admin).
- Global disable via `SMS_ENABLED="false"` (checked in `isSmsEnvConfigured`, `tenantAutomationService.js:42`).

### 4.2 WhatsApp specifics (`whatsappService.js`)
- **Monthly quota enforcement** (`_withinWhatsAppQuota`, lines 21-35): per-tenant, from `getPlanLimit(plan, "whatsapp")` in `planFeatures.js` — **basic: 0, pro: 200/mo, business: unlimited (-1), enterprise: unlimited (-1)**. Counts `WhatsAppLog` rows with `status:"sent"` since 1st of month; blocks send with `{ error: "WhatsApp monthly limit reached", limitReached: true }` when exceeded. Fail-open on counter error.
- Providers: **Meta Cloud API** (`sendViaMetaWhatsApp`, supports named templates + params, `graph.facebook.com/v18.0`), **Twilio WhatsApp**, **WasenderAPI** (`wasenderapi.com`, auto-normalizes to `8801XXXXXXXXX`, handles `retry_after` rate limiting).
- Every send is logged to `WhatsAppLog` regardless of outcome (`_saveLog`, lines 37-57).
- Config surfaced via `getWhatsAppConfig` (provider, configured flag, key presence).

### 4.3 Telegram specifics (`telegramService.js`)
- Only used for **super-admin operational alerts**: `notifyNewSignup` (bridges into `notifyEvent("agency_signup")`) and `notifyUserApproved`.
- Sends HTML-formatted messages to `TELEGRAM_ADMIN_CHAT_ID`; silently skipped when token/chat-id absent.

## 5. Notification Templates

### 5.1 SMS templates (`smsService.js:213-293`, `getSmsTemplate`)
Function-based `{{var}}`-free interpolation. Keys (Bn variants noted): `bookingConfirmation`, `paymentReceived`, `agencySignupOwner`, `agencySignupAdminAlert`, `subscriptionActivated`, `subscriptionExpiring`, `subscriptionExpiringAdmin`, `trialExpired` / `trialExpiredBn`, `subscriptionExpired` / `subscriptionExpiredBn`, `trialExpiredAdmin`, `subscriptionOrderAlert`, `subscriptionOrderConfirmation`, `passwordResetOtp`, `invoiceReminder`, `trialDripDay1` / `Bn`, `trialDripDay2` / `Bn`, `trialDripLast` / `Bn`, `passportExpiryAlert` / `Bn`, `travelDepartureReminder` / `Bn`.

### 5.2 WhatsApp templates (`whatsappTemplateService.js`, `getWhatsAppTemplate`)
- **DB-first, fallback-second**: looks up global `whatsAppTemplate` (`tenantId:null`, active) by type, else uses `FALLBACK_TEMPLATES`. Supports `metaTemplateName` for Meta approved templates and `{{var}}` rendering.
- Fallback keys: `subscriptionRenewal` / `Bn`, `subscriptionExpiring`, `paymentReminder`, `trialDripLast`, `passportExpiryAlert` / `Bn`, `travelDepartureReminder` / `Bn`.

### 5.3 Email templates (`emailService.js`, HTML render functions)
Prebuilt: `sendPasswordReset`, `sendEmailVerification`, `sendDemoRequestConfirmation`, `sendDemoRequestNotification`, `sendContactConfirmation`, `sendContactNotification`, `sendBookingConfirmation`, `sendInvoiceEmail`. Additional inline HTML renderers live in `notificationService.js` (agency signup owner/admin, subscription order owner/admin, expiring owner/admin, expired owner, trial-expired admin, trial-drip day1/day2/last, subscription activated/scheduled, invoice reminder, passport & travel emails).

## 6. Notification & Delivery-Log API (`notifications.js` + `notificationAutomation.js`)

Mounted `app.js:186` as `/api/notifications` (all routes `authenticate`-gated; `notifications.js:4`). Visibility rule: rows where `userId == null` (tenant-wide) OR `userId == req.userId` (`notifications.js:7-12`).

| Endpoint | Method | Role | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/api/notifications` | GET | auth | Paginated notifications `{ items, total, page, limit, unreadCount }` |
| `/api/notifications/unread-count` | GET | auth | `{ count }` of unread |
| `/api/notifications/read-all` | PATCH | auth | Mark all visible as read |
| `/api/notifications/:id/read` | PATCH | auth | Mark one as read (404 if not visible) |
| `/api/notifications/:id` | DELETE | auth | Delete one (404 if not visible) |
| `/api/notifications/automation` | GET | auth | Returns `SmsSettings` + per-event `NotificationAutomation` toggles (sms/inApp), labels from `EVENT_LABELS` |
| `/api/notifications/automation` | PATCH | `tenant_owner` | Update `SmsSettings` (`smsEnabled`, `notifyOnBooking`, `notifyOnPayment`, `notifyOnLead`) and per-event `sms`/`inApp`; rejects unknown eventType (400) |
| `/api/notifications/automation/logs/stats` | GET | auth | `{ total, sent, failed, pending, bySms, byInApp }` from `NotificationDelivery` |
| `/api/notifications/automation/logs` | GET | auth | Paginated `NotificationDelivery` list (filters: `status`, `channel`, `eventType`, `search` over recipient/name/message); `messagePreview` truncated |
| `/api/notifications/automation/logs/:id` | GET | auth | Full single `NotificationDelivery` row (404 if not tenant-owned) |

## 7. Related Persistence Models (referenced by these jobs)
- **`Notification`** — in-app (tenant/user-scoped; `userId:null` = tenant-wide).
- **`NotificationDelivery`** — tenant automation delivery log (channel `in_app`/`sms`, status `pending`/`sent`/`failed`).
- **`NotificationAutomation`** — per-event sms/inApp toggle rows.
- **`SmsSettings`** — master per-tenant SMS toggles (`smsEnabled`, `notifyOnBooking/Payment/Lead`).
- **`SmsLog`** / **`WhatsAppLog`** — provider-level send logs (WhatsAppLog also drives monthly quota counting).
- **`SmsTemplate`** / **`WhatsAppTemplate`** — DB templates (tenant-specific or global).
- **`PlatformNotification`** — super-admin notifications.
- **`AuditLog`** — used pervasively as the **idempotency ledger** for every scheduled job (per-day or per-lifetime guards).

## 8. Environment Variables Governing Automation
- **Cron auth:** `CRON_SECRET` (required, ≥8 chars).
- **Enable/disable jobs:** `TRIAL_DRIP_ENABLED` (default true), `PASSPORT_ALERT_ENABLED`, `TRAVEL_REMINDER_ENABLED` (set `="false"` to disable).
- **Language:** `TRIAL_EXPIRY_SMS_LANG`, `PASSPORT_ALERT_SMS_LANG`, `TRAVEL_REMINDER_SMS_LANG` (`bn`|`en`).
- **Trial length:** `TRIAL_DAYS` (default 7, max 90; via `getTrialDays`).
- **Super-admin alerts:** `SUPER_ADMIN_ALERT_PHONE`, `SUPER_ADMIN_ALERT_EMAIL` / `ADMIN_NOTIFICATION_EMAIL`.
- **Channels:** `SMS_PROVIDER`, `SMS_ENABLED`, `SMS_API_KEY`, `SMS_SENDER_ID`, `SMS_BASE_URL`, `TWILIO_*`; `WHATSAPP_PROVIDER`, `META_WHATSAPP_TOKEN`, `META_WHATSAPP_PHONE_ID`, `WASENDER_API_KEY`, `WASENDER_INSTANCE_ID`, `WHATSAPP_FROM_NUMBER`; `SMTP_*`, `SMTP_FROM`; `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`, `TELEGRAM_ADMIN_CHAT_ID`.
- **URLs in messages:** `APP_URL`, `FRONTEND_URL` (default `https://app.travelagencyweb.com`).

## Key Files
- `backend/src/routes/cron.js` — external-cron endpoints (`process-expiry`, `nightly-report`), secret auth
- `backend/src/routes/flightReminders.js` — manual/on-demand departure reminders
- `backend/src/routes/notifications.js`, `backend/src/routes/notificationAutomation.js` — notification + delivery-log API
- `backend/src/services/notificationService.js` — global multi-channel event dispatcher (`notifyEvent`, `EVENT_HANDLERS`)
- `backend/src/services/tenantAutomationService.js` — tenant-scoped in-app+SMS automation (`dispatchTenantAutomation`)
- `backend/src/services/{trialDrip,passportExpiryAlert,travelDepartureReminder,subscriptionExpiry,subscriptionNotification,platformNotification}Service.js` — job logic
- `backend/src/services/{email,sms,whatsapp,telegram}Service.js` + `whatsappTemplateService.js` — channel delivery + templates
- `backend/src/lib/staffInAppNotification.js` — tenant-wide staff in-app notifications
- `src/lib/notificationEngine.ts`, `src/lib/smsAutomation.ts` — legacy in-memory frontend engine (demo/simulation)
- `scripts/backup-db.sh` — host-crontab nightly DB backup (`0 2 * * *`), the real "auto-backup"
