# 15. INTEGRATIONS

# Integrations & Frontend Architecture

## Part 1 — External Integrations

All third-party integrations live in `backend/src/services/` and `backend/src/routes/`. Integration **credentials are environment-variable–driven** (server `.env`); DB tables (`SmsSettings`, `PaymentMethodConfig`, `WhatsAppLog`, `NotificationAutomation`) hold only per-tenant *toggles / receiver metadata / logs / quotas* — never provider secrets. Config-management endpoints explicitly refuse to store secrets (`sms.js PUT /config` returns 400 for `apiKey`/`apiSecret`).

### 1.1 Integration Inventory

| Integration | Provider(s) | Service / Route file | Configured via |
|---|---|---|---|
| Online payment | **SSLCommerz** | `routes/sslcommerz.js` + `services/paymentGateway.js` | env: `SSLCOMMERZ_STORE_ID`, `SSLCOMMERZ_STORE_PASSWORD`, `SSLCOMMERZ_SANDBOX` |
| Online payment | **bKash** (tokenized checkout) | `routes/bkash.js` + `services/paymentGateway.js` | env: `BKASH_APP_KEY`, `BKASH_APP_SECRET`, `BKASH_USERNAME`, `BKASH_PASSWORD`, `BKASH_SANDBOX` |
| Manual MFS / bank (proof-based) | bKash / Nagad / Rocket / Bank Transfer | `lib/paymentGatewayConfig.js` | env: `*_ACCOUNT_NAME`, `*_ACCOUNT_NUMBER`, `*_INSTRUCTIONS`, `BANK_NAME`, `BANK_BRANCH`; tenant overrides in `PaymentMethodConfig` table |
| SMS | **BulkSMSBD**, **Twilio**, console fallback | `services/smsService.js` | env: `SMS_PROVIDER`, `SMS_API_KEY`, `SMS_SENDER_ID`, `SMS_BASE_URL`, `TWILIO_*` |
| WhatsApp | **WasenderAPI**, **Meta WhatsApp Cloud API**, **Twilio WhatsApp**, console fallback | `services/whatsappService.js` | env: `WHATSAPP_PROVIDER`, `WASENDER_API_KEY`, `META_WHATSAPP_TOKEN`, `META_WHATSAPP_PHONE_ID`, `WHATSAPP_FROM_NUMBER`, `TWILIO_*` |
| Email / SMTP | **Nodemailer SMTP** (any host), console fallback | `services/emailService.js` | env: `SMTP_HOST`, `SMTP_PORT`, `SMTP_SECURE`, `SMTP_USER`, `SMTP_PASS`, `SMTP_FROM`, `SMTP_FROM_NAME` |
| Telegram | **Telegram Bot API** (admin alerts) | `services/telegramService.js` | env: `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`, `TELEGRAM_ADMIN_CHAT_ID` |

Every messaging channel **fails soft**: if the provider isn't configured, the message is `console.log`ged instead of sent (no throw).

### 1.2 Payment Gateways

**Shared service — `services/paymentGateway.js`** (used by both bKash + SSLCommerz):
- `handlePaymentSuccess({ transactionId, invoiceId, paymentRequestId, amount, method, gateway, tenantId, metadata })` — the single idempotent success handler. Two branches:
  1. **Invoice branch** (`invoiceId` set): creates a `Payment` (idempotent on `transactionRef`), recomputes invoice `paidAmount`/`dueAmount`/`status` (`paid`/`partial`/`unpaid`), rolls the totals up to the parent `Booking` (`paymentStatus`), writes a ledger `Transaction` (type `income`, category `online_payment`, via `ensureLedgerTransaction`, idempotent on `referenceType:"payment"`+`referenceId`), then fires `notifyEvent("payment_received", …)`.
  2. **Subscription branch** (`paymentRequestId` set): `activateSubscriptionFromPaymentRequest()` — approves the `PaymentRequest`, sets `tenant.subscriptionPlan/Status/Expiry` (adds 12 mo for yearly / 1 mo for monthly; for a `renew` on a still-valid plan it extends from current expiry), inserts `Subscription` + `SubscriptionHistory` rows (`source: "payment_gateway"`), and fires `notifyEvent("subscription_activated", …)` to the tenant owner.
- `auditPaymentEvent({ action, gateway, … })` — writes an `AuditLog` row (`module: "payment_gateway"`, actor `system`) for every gateway event.
- `getCallbackUrls(gateway)` — builds `{success, fail, cancel, ipn, frontendCallback}` from `API_BASE_URL` (default `http://localhost:${PORT|4000}/api`) and `FRONTEND_URL` (default `https://travelagencyweb.com`).

**bKash — `routes/bkash.js`** (mounted at `/api/payments/bkash`):
- Tokenized Checkout v1.2.0-beta. Sandbox URL `tokenized.sandbox.bka.sh`, live `tokenized.pay.bka.sh` (switch on `BKASH_SANDBOX !== "false"`).
- **In-memory token cache** (`tokenCache`, refreshed 60s before `expires_in`; comment notes "production should use Redis").
- `POST /create` (authenticated) — grants token, `POST /create` to bKash with `mode:"0011"`, stores the `{invoiceId, paymentRequestId, tenantId, amount}` context in an `AuditLog` row (`action:"bkash_created"`, keyed by `paymentID`), returns `bkashURL` redirect.
- `GET /callback` — on `status=success`, executes the payment (`/execute`), and if `transactionStatus==="Completed"` re-reads the stored context from `AuditLog`, calls `handlePaymentSuccess`, then redirects to `${FRONTEND}/payment/callback?status=…&gateway=bkash`.
- Returns **HTTP 503** if any of the four bKash env vars are missing.

**SSLCommerz — `routes/sslcommerz.js`** (mounted at `/api/payments/sslcommerz`):
- Sandbox `sandbox.sslcommerz.com`, live `securepay.sslcommerz.com` (switch on `SSLCOMMERZ_SANDBOX !== "false"`).
- `POST /initiate` (authenticated) — form-POST to `/gwprocess/v4`; carries `invoiceId`/`paymentRequestId`/`tenantId`/`amount` in the SSLCommerz `value_a…value_d` passthrough fields; returns `GatewayPageURL`.
- `POST /success` — **validates** `val_id` against `/validator/api/validationserverAPI.php` (must be `VALID`/`VALIDATED`) before calling `handlePaymentSuccess`; redirects to frontend callback.
- `POST /fail`, `POST /cancel` — audit + redirect.
- `POST /ipn` — server-to-server; on `status VALID|VALIDATED` calls `handlePaymentSuccess` (source `ipn`) and returns `{status:"ok"}`. Provides at-least-once delivery guarantee (idempotency handled downstream).
- Returns **HTTP 503** if store creds missing.

**Gateway status config — `lib/paymentGatewayConfig.js`** (env only, no DB):
- `isBkashOnlineConfigured()` (needs all 4 bKash vars), `isSslcommerzConfigured()` (needs store id+pass).
- `getGatewayStatus()` → `{bkash, sslcommerz, manual}` each with `configured/sandbox/mode` (`sandbox`|`live`|`disabled`).
- `getGatewayStatusDetailed()` adds `apiBaseUrl`, all callback URLs, and manual method summary.
- `getDefaultManualPaymentMethods()` — returns 4 receivers (`bkash`, `nagad`, `rocket`, `bank_transfer`); each `enabled` iff an account number env var exists. `isOnlinePaymentMethod(code)` = `code.startsWith("online_")`.
- **Payments router** `routes/payments.js` mounts the two sub-routers and exposes `GET /gateway-status` (auth), `POST /initiate`, `GET /status/:transactionId`, `POST /callback/:gateway`.

### 1.3 SMS — `services/smsService.js`

- `sendSms({ to, message })` dispatches on `SMS_PROVIDER` (`twilio` | `bulksmsbd` | `console` default).
- **BulkSMSBD** (`sendViaBulkSmsBD`): GET `${SMS_BASE_URL||http://bulksmsbd.net/api}/smsapi` with `api_key`, `type:"text"`, `number`, `senderid`, `message`. Numbers normalized to `88017XXXXXXXX` (no `+`, via `toBulkSmsNumber`). Response code `202` = success; a full `BULKSMSBD_ERRORS` map (1001–1032) translates failures (invalid number, sender disabled, insufficient balance, IP not whitelisted, etc.).
- **Twilio** (`sendViaTwilio`): POST to `api.twilio.com/2010-04-01/Accounts/{SID}/Messages.json` with Basic auth; needs `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID`+`TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN`+`TWILIO_FROM_NUMBER`.
- `getSmsBalance()` — BulkSMSBD-only diagnostic (`/getBalanceApi`), super-admin use.
- **Templates**: `getSmsTemplate(name, data)` renders from a large built-in `templates` map — bookingConfirmation, paymentReceived, agencySignupOwner/AdminAlert, subscriptionActivated/Expiring(+Admin)/Expired(+Bn), trialExpired(+Bn/+Admin), subscriptionOrderAlert/Confirmation, passwordResetOtp, invoiceReminder, trialDripDay1/Day2/Last (each with `*Bn` Bengali variant), passportExpiryAlert(+Bn), travelDepartureReminder(+Bn). Currency rendered as `৳`.
- **Per-tenant gating** via `SmsSettings` table (`smsEnabled`, `notifyOnBooking`, `notifyOnPayment`, `notifyOnLead`) — checked in `tenantAutomationService.js` / `notificationAutomation.js`, not in the send function itself.
- Routes `routes/sms.js`: `GET/PUT /config` (super-admin, env-derived — **rejects secret writes**), `GET /balance`, `POST /send` (tenant_owner/manager/super_admin), `POST /send-bulk`, `POST /test`, `GET /logs` (+`/stats`,`/:id`), template CRUD (`/templates` …).

### 1.4 WhatsApp — `services/whatsappService.js`

- `sendWhatsApp({ to, message, templateName, templateParams, tenantId, templateType, createdByUserId })` dispatches on `WHATSAPP_PROVIDER` (`twilio` | `meta` | `wasender` | `console` default).
- **Per-tenant monthly quota** enforced *before* every send via `_withinWhatsAppQuota(tenantId)`: reads `getPlanLimit(plan,"whatsapp")` from `planFeatures.js` (`basic:0`, `pro:200`, `business:-1` unlimited, `enterprise:-1`), counts month-to-date `WhatsAppLog` rows with `status:"sent"`. `-1`=unlimited, `0`/undefined=blocked. **Fail-open** on counter error.
- Every send is persisted to **`WhatsAppLog`** (`_saveLog`): phone, message, status (`sent`/`failed`), provider, error, providerMessageId, templateType, createdByUserId.
- **WasenderAPI** (`sendViaWasender`): POST `wasenderapi.com/api/send-message` (Bearer `WASENDER_API_KEY`); auto-normalizes to `8801XXXXXXXXX`; treats `retry_after` rate-limit as non-fatal.
- **Meta Cloud API** (`sendViaMetaWhatsApp`): POST `graph.facebook.com/v18.0/{PHONE_ID}/messages` (Bearer `META_WHATSAPP_TOKEN`); supports both free-text and named-template messages (`language:{code:"en"}`, body params).
- **Twilio WhatsApp** (`sendViaTwilioWhatsApp`): same Twilio Messages API, `From` defaults `whatsapp:+14155238886`.
- `getWhatsAppConfig()` / `isWhatsAppConfigured(provider)` return provider + `configured` flag with no secrets.
- **Templates** — `services/whatsappTemplateService.js`: `getWhatsAppTemplate(type, data, {useBn})` prefers a DB `WhatsAppTemplate` (global `tenantId:null`, active) and falls back to a `FALLBACK_TEMPLATES` map (subscriptionRenewal(+Bn), subscriptionExpiring, paymentReminder, trialDripLast, passportExpiryAlert(+Bn), travelDepartureReminder(+Bn)). `renderMessage` interpolates `{{var}}` placeholders; `metaTemplateName` passed through for Meta-approved templates. Bengali selected by `*Bn` key.
- Routes `routes/whatsapp.js` (all gated by `requireFeature("hasWhatsApp")`): `GET /config` (adds live `{quota:{limit,used,unlimited}}`), `POST /test`, template CRUD, `GET /logs`, `POST /send`.

### 1.5 Email / SMTP — `services/emailService.js`

- Singleton **Nodemailer** transporter created lazily from `SMTP_HOST/PORT/SECURE/USER/PASS`. `sendEmail({to,subject,html,text})` returns `true` if sent, `false` if only console-logged (no SMTP).
- Pre-built templates: `sendPasswordReset`, `sendEmailVerification` (24h link, references configured trial days), `sendDemoRequestConfirmation`/`Notification`, `sendContactConfirmation`/`Notification`, `sendBookingConfirmation`, `sendInvoiceEmail`. Admin-notification emails go to `ADMIN_NOTIFICATION_EMAIL`. `from` = `SMTP_FROM` (default `noreply@travelagencyweb.com`).
- `testSmtpConnection(config)` verifies a connection (`transporter.verify()`) for the settings UI.
- Routes `routes/email.js`: `GET /config` (env-derived, **never returns password**), `POST /test`, `POST /send/booking-confirmation`, `POST /send/invoice`.

### 1.6 Telegram — `services/telegramService.js`

- Admin-alert channel. `sendTelegramMessage(text, {chatId})` POSTs to `api.telegram.org/bot{TOKEN}/sendMessage` (`parse_mode: "HTML"`, previews disabled); skips silently if `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`/`TELEGRAM_ADMIN_CHAT_ID` unset. HTML-escapes user data via `esc()`.
- `notifyNewSignup({name,email,phone,tenantName,userId,plan})` — sends a formatted new-agency-signup alert to the admin chat **and bridges** into the multi-channel `notifyEvent("agency_signup", …)`.
- `notifyUserApproved({name,email})` — user-approved alert.

### 1.7 Notification Orchestration — `services/notificationService.js`

Central fan-out: `notifyEvent(event, data)` renders per-event content and dispatches across **email + SMS + WhatsApp + in-app**, composing `emailService`, `smsService`, `whatsappService`, and `whatsappTemplateService`. Events include `payment_received`, `subscription_activated`, `agency_signup`, subscription expiry/renewal (with Bengali variants), trial-drip, passport-expiry, travel-departure, plus super-admin alert emails/SMS (`SUPER_ADMIN_ALERT_EMAIL`/`SUPER_ADMIN_ALERT_PHONE`). Owner contact resolved via `lib/tenantOwnerContact.js`. This is the layer that reads `SmsSettings` / `NotificationAutomation` toggles to decide which channels fire.

---

## Part 2 — Frontend Architecture

Stack: **React 18 + Vite + TypeScript**, React Router v6, TanStack Query, react-i18next, shadcn/ui (Radix) + Sonner/Toaster. Path alias `@/` → `src/`.

### 2.1 Provider Tree — `src/App.tsx`

Nesting (outer → inner), assembled in `AppContent`:

1. **`BrowserRouter`** (`AppWithRouter`) — only added if not already inside a router context (`useInRouterContext` guard, so `App` is embeddable).
2. **`QueryClientProvider`** — one module-level `new QueryClient()` (default options).
3. **`TooltipProvider`** (Radix).
4. **`AuthProvider`** — the app-wide auth/tenant/plan/role context.
5. **`<Toaster/>` + `<Sonner/>`** — dual toast systems mounted as siblings of `<Routes>`.
6. **`WebsiteProvider`** — *not* global; wrapped only around public tenant-site routes (`/site*`, tenant-host `/`, `/about`, `/packages`, `/blog`, `/contact`).

**Route guards** (composable wrappers):
- `P` = `<ProtectedRoute>` (authenticated).
- `PM` = `<ProtectedRoute><PermissionRoute module=…>` (authenticated + RBAC module check).
- `A` = `<AdminRoute>` (super-admin only).
- Plus `SubscriptionRoute`, `HajjModuleGate`, `BdModuleGate`, `BookingSegmentRoute` for finer gating.

**Route surface**: ~90 routes across four zones — public marketing (`/`, `/features`, `/pricing`, `/demo`, `/faq`, `/privacy`, `/terms`), auth (`/login`, `/register`, `/forgot-password`, `/reset-password`, `/verify-email`, `/onboarding`), public tenant sites (`/site*`, `/site/:slug*`), the authenticated ERP app (dashboard, CRM, clients, agents, vendors, leads, quotations, bookings, invoices, accounts, reports, hajj-umrah, operations, website builder, HRM/payroll, settings, subscription, loyalty/referrals, MICE, visa-tracker, inventory, recruitment, ticket-transactions, flight-reminders), and `/admin/*` (super-admin). Heavier pages (`SettingsTax`, `FinancialStatements`, `Payroll`, `WebsiteSeo`, `Loyalty`, `Referrals`, `SalesAnalytics`, `GroupTours`, `MiceEvents`, `TravelApprovals`, `VisaTracker`, `Inventory`, `Recruitment`, `TicketTransactions`, `FlightReminders`) are **`lazy()`-loaded** behind `<Suspense>` with a `Skeleton` fallback.

### 2.2 Contexts — `src/contexts/`

**`AuthContext.tsx`** (`AuthProvider`, `useAuth`) — single source of truth for identity/plan/subscription. Exposes:

| Value | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `user`, `tenant` | current `User` / `Tenant` objects |
| `currentPlan` | `tenant.subscriptionPlan` or `"free"` |
| `appRole` | RBAC role via `mapLegacyRole(user.role)` (`sales_agent` default) |
| `isTrialActive`, `trialDaysLeft` | derived from `subscriptionStatus==="trial"` + expiry |
| `isSubscriptionBlocked` / `isSubscriptionExpired` | from `getSubscriptionBlockState`; **super_admin never blocked** |
| `subscriptionBlockReason` | typed reason |
| `loading` | bootstrap flag |
| `login(email,password,totpCode?)` | throws `2FA_REQUIRED` if `requires2FA`; stores JWT in `localStorage`, fetches tenant |
| `register(data)` | instant-token Pro-trial flow (falls back to pending-approval on legacy backend) |
| `logout()` | fire-and-forget audit log, clears token + state |
| `refreshTenant()` | re-fetch `tenantApi.get()` |

Bootstrap `useEffect`: reads `localStorage.token`; runs `authApi.me()` + `tenantApi.get()` in parallel with a **12s safety timeout** to release `loading`; clears token on `me()` failure.

**`WebsiteContext.tsx`** (`WebsiteProvider`, `useWebsite`) — powers the public agency websites. Resolves the hostname synchronously (`resolveHostname()`), then loads `{tenant, packages, websiteConfig}` in parallel via `publicApi` + `websiteApi` (by slug or by custom domain). **Gates first paint** for real tenant sites (spinner until loaded, so no demo-flash); renders `<DomainErrorPage>` on resolution failure. Ships built-in `demoTenant` + `demoPackages` fallback for the marketing app. Provides `domainResolution`.

### 2.3 Custom Hooks — `src/hooks/`

| Hook | File | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `useAuth` | (contexts/AuthContext) | consume AuthContext (throws if outside provider) |
| `useWebsite` | (contexts/WebsiteContext) | consume public-site context |
| `usePermissions` | `usePermissions.ts` | RBAC helper off `useAuth`: `can(module,action)`, `canAccess`, `modulePerms`, `accessibleModules`, plus role booleans (`isSuperAdmin`, `isTenantOwner`, `isManager`, `isSalesAgent`, `isAccountant`, `isOperations`) and `canManageTeam/Subscription/AccessAdmin`. Delegates to `lib/permissions.ts` |
| `usePlanAccess` | `usePlanAccess.ts` | plan-feature gate off `getPlan(plan)`: exposes limits (`maxClients/Bookings/Users/Domains/SmsPerMonth/StorageMB/Reports`) and `canUse*` feature flags (payment gateways, SSLCommerz, bKash, custom domain, website templates, SMS, WhatsApp, email, agent commission, analytics, marketing, API, refund, Hajj/Umrah); helpers `hasFeature`, `requiresUpgrade`, `getUpgradePlan` |
| `useGatewayStatus` | `useGatewayStatus.ts` | fetches `paymentGatewayApi.gatewayStatus()`; returns `{status, loading, hasOnlineGateway}` (bKash/SSLCommerz configured) |
| `useConfiguredTrialDays` | `useConfiguredTrialDays.ts` | fetches signup trial length from API health (`fetchConfiguredTrialDays`, `DEFAULT_TRIAL_DAYS` fallback) |
| `useSubscriptionPayment` | `useSubscriptionPayment.ts` | drives the whole plan-purchase flow: loads payment methods + prior requests (`tenantSubscriptionWorkflowApi`), manages the request dialog/form, coupon validation (`subscriptionCouponApi`), payment-proof upload, create/resubmit/cancel; exports `nextSuggestedPlan()` |
| `useHajjData` | `useHajjData.ts` | loads/manages Hajj packages+groups+pilgrims (`hajjApi`); computes `summary` + `pkgProfitability`; save/delete mutations with optimistic state |
| `useHajjPilgrim` | `useHajjPilgrim.ts` | Hajj pilgrim table: filters, pagination, pilgrim + payment forms, CRUD |
| `useHumanError` | `useHumanError.ts` | i18n-aware error formatting (`formatError`, `errorTitle` via `lib/humanErrors` + `react-i18next`) |
| `usePermissions`… | | (listed above) |
| `useToast` / `toast` | `use-toast.ts` | shadcn toast store: external reducer + listener pattern, `TOAST_LIMIT=1`, `ADD/UPDATE/DISMISS/REMOVE_TOAST` actions |
| `useIsMobile` | `use-mobile.tsx` | `matchMedia` breakpoint hook (`< 768px`) |

### 2.4 API Layer — `src/lib/api.ts` (~1500 lines) + companion `lib/*Api.ts` modules

**Core client** (top of `api.ts`):
- `BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "http://localhost:4000/api"`.
- `request<T>(path, options)` — the single fetch wrapper: injects `Content-Type: application/json` + `Authorization: Bearer <localStorage.token>`, parses JSON, and on non-2xx throws `new Error(parsed.message || parsed.error || statusText)` (guards against HTML bodies). All auth is stateless JWT in `localStorage`.
- `createCrudApi<T>(resource)` — factory yielding `{list, get, create, update(PATCH), delete}` for a REST resource. Used directly for `taskApi`, `paymentApi`, `transactionApi`, `subscriptionApi`, `paymentRequestApi`.

**Domain API objects** exported from `api.ts` (each a namespaced object of typed `request()` calls): `authApi` (login/2FA-TOTP/register/checkEmail/WhatsApp-OTP/me/forgot+reset password/verify+resend email/logout), `tenantApi` (me + members CRUD), `dashboardApi`, `clientApi`, `agentApi`, `visaStockApi`, `vendorApi`, `complaintApi`, `campaignApi`, `crmAnalyticsApi`, `crmSettingsApi`, `leadApi`, `documentHubApi`, `taskApi`, `supportTicketApi`, `blogApi`, `hrmApi`, `bookingApi`, `invoiceApi`, `financeApi`, `taxRuleApi`, `payrollApi`, `paymentApi`, `accountApi`, `transactionApi`, `expenseApi`, `subscriptionApi`, `paymentRequestApi`, `quotationApi`, `adminApi`, `domainApi`, `loyaltyApi`, `referralApi`, `groupTourApi`, `analyticsApi`, `miceApi`, `travelPolicyApi`, `visaApi`, `inventoryApi`, `recruitmentApi`. It also exports the full **domain type layer** (`User`, `Tenant`, `Client`, `Booking`, `Invoice`, `Payment`, `Quotation`, `Lead`, `Vendor`, `Agent`, plus stats/report/enum types).

**Companion API modules** (same `request`-style pattern, split by domain) in `src/lib/`: `adminNotificationApi`, `auditLogApi`, `automationApi`, `domainApi`, `emailApi`, `exportApi`, `hajjApi`, `masterDataApi`, `notificationApi`, `paymentGatewayApi`, `platformAdminApi`, `platformHealthApi`, `portalApi`, `publicApi`, `smsApi`, `smsTemplateApi`, `subscriptionWorkflowApi`, `tenantDomainApi`, `travelPackageApi`, `websiteApi`, `websiteCmsApi`, `whatsappLogApi`. Non-API support libs of note: `permissions.ts` (RBAC matrix), `plans.ts` (plan configs/limits/pricing), `moduleAccess.ts`/`features.ts` (module gating), `domainResolver.ts` (tenant-from-hostname), `serviceCatalog.ts`, `notificationEngine.ts`.

**`domainResolver.ts`** — multi-tenant hostname routing used by `WebsiteContext`/`App`. `resolveHostname()` classifies the host into `main-app` | `slug` (`{slug}.APP_DOMAIN` wildcard) | `custom-domain` | `portal`; `RESERVED_SUBDOMAINS = [app, portal, api, www, admin]`; localhost/`127.0.0.1`/no `VITE_APP_DOMAIN` → main-app. Helpers: `getReservedSubdomain`, `isPortalHost`, `getSubdomainUrl`, `extractSlugFromHostname`, `fetchTenantByDomain`, `resolveTenant`.

**Notable API-layer conventions**: state is JWT-in-localStorage (no cookies); TanStack Query wraps these `request()` calls in components; all mutating calls use `PATCH` for updates (per the CRUD factory); errors surface as `Error.message` for `useHumanError`/toasts to render.

Relevant files: `backend/src/services/{paymentGateway,emailService,smsService,whatsappService,telegramService,whatsappTemplateService,notificationService}.js`, `backend/src/lib/paymentGatewayConfig.js`, `backend/src/lib/planFeatures.js`, `backend/src/routes/{bkash,sslcommerz,payments,sms,whatsapp,email}.js`, `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` (models `PaymentMethodConfig`, `SmsSettings`, `WhatsAppLog`, `NotificationAutomation`); `src/contexts/{AuthContext,WebsiteContext}.tsx`, `src/hooks/*`, `src/lib/api.ts`, `src/lib/domainResolver.ts`, `src/App.tsx`.

# 16. WORKFLOW (end-to-end transactional flows)

**Primary sales-to-cash spine** (verified working end-to-end in live testing):

```
LEAD                      Lead captured (source, destination, budget, score, assignee)
 │  status: new → contacted → qualified → quoted        LeadActivity logged at each step
 ▼
CLIENT                    Lead converts to Client (KYC, wallet). Or client created inline on booking.
 │
 ▼
QUOTATION                 Builder: line items + itinerary → totalCost / totalSelling / totalProfit / grandTotal
 │  status: draft → sent → approved      (QuotationVersion snapshot per revision)
 │  [convert-to-booking]  carries profit + amounts; lead → won
 ▼
BOOKING                   amount / cost / profit(=amount−cost) / paidAmount / dueAmount
 │  status: pending → confirmed → …       ops: pending → …   (Segments, Travelers, Checklist, Timeline)
 │  agent assigned → BookingAgentCommission accrued (pending)
 ▼
INVOICE                   auto-sequenced INV-#####; taxRule → subTotal/taxAmount/total; installments optional
 │  status: unpaid → partial → paid       (InvoiceAuditEvent trail)
 ▼
PAYMENT                   recorded against invoice (cash/bank/bkash/nagad/gateway) + proof
 │  → invoice.paidAmount ↑, dueAmount ↓, status recalculated
 │  → booking.paidAmount/dueAmount/paymentStatus cascaded (invoice-derived)
 ▼
ACCOUNTING                Payment/Expense/VendorBillPayment → Transaction (general ledger)
 │  Accounts (cash/bank) · Tax collected · Expenses (approval) · Vendor payables
 ▼
REPORTS                   Dashboard KPIs · Sales/Payments/Profitability/Vendor reports ·
                          P&L / Balance Sheet / Cash Flow / Tax statements · Sales & CRM analytics · Excel export
```

**Parallel / sub-workflows:**

- **Agent ledger & commission:** Agent deposit/payment → atomic running balance (`AgentTransaction`). Booking with agent → commission accrued → *mark paid* (payout) at agent profile.
- **Vendor payable (purchase side):** Vendor → VendorBill (payable) → VendorBillPayment → synced expense `Transaction`; payable/due tracked; overpay floored at 0 (fixed).
- **Visa stock (BD ops):** Buy visa quota (buy/sell → profit) → processing → **sell** (buyer) → completed; stats roll up stock value + sold profit.
- **Hajj pilgrim:** Package → Group → Pilgrim (KYC/mahram/rooming) → pilgrim payments/installments → visa & departure status tracking.
- **Air ticket lifecycle:** Booking (air_ticket) → Refund / Void / Reissue (each a financial adjustment) → BSP reconciliation → flight departure reminders (auto cron + manual).
- **Client wallet:** credit/debit → atomic running balance (`WalletTransaction`), used for advances/receivables.
- **Support & CRM:** Complaint (rating) → resolve; Campaign (bulk SMS/WhatsApp/Email, plan-gated) → sentCount; Loyalty accrual + Referral conversions.
- **Subscription lifecycle:** trial/active → expiry → 402 gate → renewal (manual/bKash/SSLCommerz) → PaymentRequest → super-admin approval → SubscriptionHistory.
- **HR/Payroll:** Staff profile + salary structure + attendance → Payroll run → payslip entries (gross/deductions/net).

---

# 17. MISSING FEATURES (vs. a modern enterprise travel-agency SaaS)

**Accounting / Finance**
- No **double-entry / journal** ledger — `Transaction` is single-sided; `Account.balance` is a stored field **not** posted from ledger activity (drifts from reality).
- **No multi-currency** — BDT `৳` hardcoded across UI/reports; `currency` field exists only on packages, never applied to bookings/invoices.
- No server-side **invoice/receipt PDF** generation (print views only), no e-invoice / QR / digital signature.
- No Bangladesh tax compliance depth (VAT/AIT/mushak, TDS on commission) beyond a flat `TaxRule`.
- No accounting export/sync to **Tally / QuickBooks / Xero**; no bank reconciliation import.
- No recurring/scheduled client invoicing; no credit-note workflow separate from refunds.

**Travel-domain integrations**
- **No GDS/airline integration** (Amadeus / Sabre / Galileo / Travelport) — no live fare search, PNR import, ticketing, or auto BSP feed.
- No hotel/OTA supplier APIs (Hotelbeds, TBO), no real-time inventory; visa/ticket "stock" is manual.
- No IATA/BSP electronic reconciliation (BSP is manual upload).

**Platform / architecture**
- No **background job queue** (BullMQ/Redis) — scheduled work runs via an external cron hitting `/api/cron/*`.
- No **caching layer** (Redis) or read replicas; frontend ships as a **~2.5 MB monolith** (minimal code-splitting).
- No real-time layer (WebSockets) — notifications are stored/polled, WhatsApp is **send-only** (no two-way inbox / omnichannel).
- No observability (APM / metrics / tracing / error tracking) evident; thin automated test coverage (~17 backend + ~24 frontend tests).
- No object storage (S3) integration surfaced; storage quota advertised but **not enforced**.

**Access / identity**
- **No custom roles** — permission matrix is hardcoded in two static maps; the `overrides` seam is dead code (no persistence/UI).
- No **SSO / SAML / OAuth** login; no per-user MFA (2FA is super-admin only).
- No public **API keys / developer API / webhooks** despite a `hasApiAccess` flag.
- **Branches / multi-location** advertised but not enforced or data-isolated.

**Product gaps**
- No **global search** / command palette; several pages are deep-link-only (no nav entry).
- No **dark-mode toggle** (CSS is dark-ready).
- Customer portal is thin; **no agent/customer mobile app**.
- No visual **workflow/automation builder** (rules are code-defined).
- No bulk **data import** (CSV) counterpart to export; no data-migration tooling.
- No commission **slabs/tiers**; no itinerary/visa document generator; no e-sign.

**Known data-integrity items (from live audit)**
- Wallet debit overdraft unguarded (policy); CRM funnel drops out-of-enum lead statuses; Enterprise price mismatch (frontend 5,000 vs backend custom/0); Branches limit inconsistency (catalog 1/2/5 vs comparison 1/1/1, unenforced).

# 18. IMPROVEMENT SCORE (out of 100)

| Dimension | Score | Rationale |
|---|---:|---|
| **Architecture** | 72 | Clean multi-tenant layering, strong Prisma schema, transactional integrity where it matters. Held back by monolith bundle, no queue/cache, single-sided ledger, cron-over-HTTP. |
| **Security** | 74 | Solid: strict tenant scoping, RBAC on every route, fail-closed plan gates, bcrypt+JWT, WhatsApp-OTP signup, super-admin TOTP, module-gate anti-bypass. Gaps: hardcoded roles, no SSO/webhook signing, one IDOR found (now fixed), env-only secrets. |
| **Scalability** | 65 | Postgres+Prisma scale fine; but no caching/queue/read-replica, per-tenant PM2 co-hosting, 2.5 MB SPA, no CDN/asset strategy surfaced. |
| **UX** | 78 | Bilingual EN/BN, polished shadcn UI, plan-aware locked "upgrade" rows, onboarding checklist, role-aware dashboard. Missing global search, dark toggle; many deep-link-only pages. |
| **Accounting** | 66 | Good SME coverage (invoices, installments, refunds, tax, P&L/BS/CF statements, vendor payables). Lacks double-entry, live account balances, multi-currency, e-invoice, external sync. |
| **CRM** | 82 | Comprehensive: leads+pipeline+scoring, complaints, campaigns, analytics, loyalty, referrals, corporate. Minor funnel-enum reconciliation gap. |
| **Reports** | 78 | 6 CSV reports + 4 financial statements + sales/CRM analytics + multi-sheet Excel workbook. No scheduled/emailed reports or custom report builder. |
| **Automation** | 70 | Departure/passport/subscription/trial reminders, daily WhatsApp summary + auto-backup, notification engine across 4 channels. No visual builder, no durable queue. |
| **OVERALL** | **73** | A strong, feature-broad SME travel-agency SaaS with disciplined multi-tenancy and RBAC. To reach enterprise grade: double-entry accounting + live balances, GDS/supplier integrations, custom roles, job queue/caching, real-time omnichannel, and code-splitting. |

# APPENDIX A — COMPLETE API ENDPOINT REFERENCE

_Every backend route module, mounted under `/api/*`. Grouped as scanned._



## routes-core

## Backend Core Sales Routes

All eight routers are mounted under `/api/*` in `backend/src/app.js`. `/api/auth` additionally has a `credentialAuthLimiter` rate-limiter applied at the mount point. Every router except `auth.js` calls `router.use(authenticate)` globally (all endpoints require a valid JWT); `auth.js` applies `authenticate` per-route. RBAC via `requirePermission(module, action)` and `requireRole(role)` from `backend/src/middleware/auth.js`; plan-limit gates via `checkPlanLimit(resource)`.

### `auth.js` — mounted at `/api/auth`
Public (no auth) unless noted. Heavy audit-logging into `prisma.auditLog`. TOTP (Google Authenticator via `speakeasy`) is super_admin-only.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description / logic |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/login` | Public | Email+password login (bcrypt). super_admin with `totpEnabled` must send `totpCode` (returns `{requires2FA:true}` if absent). Blocks `status` `pending`/`rejected`. Issues 7-day JWT `{userId,tenantId,role}`. Updates `lastLoginAt`; writes `auth/login` audit log. |
| GET | `/check-email` | Public | Pre-signup email availability check via `validateEmail`; fail-open on error. |
| POST | `/whatsapp-otp/send` | Public | Sends 6-digit WhatsApp OTP (purpose `signup`). Rate-limited: 60s cooldown, 5/hour cap, 5-min TTL. Persists `whatsappOtp`; delivers via `whatsappService.sendWhatsApp`. |
| POST | `/whatsapp-otp/verify` | Public | Verifies OTP (max 5 attempts, TTL check). On success marks `verified` and returns a 30-min `whatsappVerifyToken` JWT (purpose `wa_verify`) bound to the number. |
| POST | `/register` | Public | Self-service signup. Validates name/agency/email/phone; **requires** valid `whatsappVerifyToken`. Creates `tenant` (unique slug loop, `enabledSubcategories`/`enabledServiceTypes` normalized) + `tenant_owner` user (auto-approved `active`), sets `tenant.ownerId`. free→instant `active` no trial; paid→`trial` with `TRIAL_DAYS` expiry. Sends email verification (24h token), platform notification, Telegram notify; writes `auth/signup` audit. Returns 7-day JWT. |
| POST | `/verify-email` | Public | Verifies email by token (`emailVerifyExpiry > now`); clears token. |
| POST | `/resend-verification` | `authenticate` | Regenerates 24h email-verify token and resends. |
| GET | `/me` | `authenticate` | Returns current user minus password/reset tokens. |
| PATCH | `/me` | `authenticate` | Update own `name` and/or password (requires `currentPassword` bcrypt check + `validatePassword`). |
| POST | `/forgot-password` | Public | Generates 1-hour `resetToken`, emails reset link. Does not reveal whether email exists. |
| POST | `/reset-password` | Public | Validates reset token (unexpired) + `validatePassword`; sets new bcrypt hash; writes `auth/password_reset` audit. |
| POST | `/logout` | `authenticate` | Writes `auth/logout` audit log (best-effort). |
| GET | `/totp/setup` | `authenticate` + super_admin | Generates TOTP secret + QR data-URL; stores temp `totpSecret`. Rejects if already enabled. |
| POST | `/totp/enable` | `authenticate` + super_admin | Verifies first code (window 1), sets `totpEnabled=true`. |
| POST | `/totp/disable` | `authenticate` + super_admin | Verifies code, clears `totpEnabled`+`totpSecret`. |
| GET | `/totp/status` | `authenticate` + super_admin | Returns `{totpEnabled}`. |
| POST | `/super-admin/reset-password` | `authenticate` + super_admin | Self password reset with no current-password check (min 8 + `validatePassword`). |
| GET | `/whatsapp-config` | `authenticate` + super_admin | Returns WhatsApp provider config via `whatsappService.getWhatsAppConfig`. |

Role check for super_admin endpoints is an inline `user.role !== "super_admin"` guard (not `requireRole`).

### `tenants.js` — mounted at `/api/tenants`
Global `authenticate`. Self-service tenant + team-member management. `PATCH /me` uses an `ALLOWED_TENANT_FIELDS` allow-list (`pickAllowed`) to prevent privilege escalation; `enabledModules` is sanitized against the tenant's real plan via `sanitizeEnabledModules` (module gating). Team roles restricted to `manager|sales_agent|accountant|operations`.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description / logic |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/me` | authenticate | Returns tenant record + `trialDaysConfigured`. |
| PATCH | `/me` | `requireRole("tenant_owner")` | Update tenant, allow-listed fields only. Coerces boolean module flags, normalizes `enabledServiceTypes`/`enabledSubcategories`, sanitizes `enabledModules` by plan. |
| GET | `/me/members` | `requirePermission("team","view")` | List tenant users (id/name/email/role/createdAt). |
| POST | `/me/members` | `requireRole("tenant_owner")` + `checkPlanLimit("users")` | Invite member. Role validated to allowed set (default `sales_agent`). Creates user with placeholder pw + 1h `resetToken`; emails setup link. If SMTP fails, returns a one-time `temporaryPassword`. Writes `team/member_added` audit. |
| GET | `/me/notification-settings` | authenticate | Ensures + returns SMS/notification settings (`tenantAutomationService`). |
| PATCH | `/me/notification-settings` | `requireRole("tenant_owner")` | Update `smsEnabled`/`notifyOnBooking`/`notifyOnPayment`/`notifyOnLead` (booleans). Rejects `apiKey`/`apiSecret` (env-managed). |
| PATCH | `/me/members/:userId` | `requireRole("tenant_owner")` | Update member `role`/`name`. Blocks changing self and any owner/admin. Writes `team/member_updated` audit with old/new values. |
| DELETE | `/me/members/:userId` | `requireRole("tenant_owner")` | Remove member (tenant-scoped `deleteMany`); blocks self-removal. Writes `team/member_removed` audit. |

### `clients.js` — mounted at `/api/clients`
Global `authenticate`. All queries tenant-scoped via `getTenantClient` helper / `tenantId` in where. RBAC module `clients`. **Wallet ledger** is here.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description / logic |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `clients/view` | List all tenant clients (desc). |
| GET | `/corporate-summary` | `clients/view` | Per-`corporate` client invoice roll-up for a `month` (count/total/paid/due/openInvoices) + tenant totals. |
| GET | `/birthdays` | `clients/view` | Upcoming client birthdays within N days (default 30, capped 366), month/day match ignoring year. |
| GET | `/expiring-passports` | `clients/view` | Passports expiring within N days (default 30, cap 365) + already-expired; returns summary counts + items with `daysLeft`/`status`. |
| GET | `/:id` | `clients/view` | Single client incl. `documents`. |
| POST | `/` | `clients/create` + `checkPlanLimit("clients")` | Create client (`{...body, tenantId}`). |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `clients/edit` | Tenant-scoped `updateMany`, then returns fresh record. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `clients/delete` | Tenant-scoped `deleteMany`. |
| GET | `/:id/family` | `clients/view` | List `clientFamilyMember` for client. |
| POST | `/:id/family` | `clients/edit` | Add family member (`name` required, `relation` default `family`). |
| DELETE | `/:id/family/:memberId` | `clients/edit` | Remove family member (tenant+client scoped). |
| GET | `/:id/wallet` | `clients/view` | **Client wallet**: returns `{balance: client.walletBalance, transactions}` (last 50 `walletTransaction`). |
| POST | `/:id/wallet` | `clients/edit` | **Wallet ledger entry** in a `prisma.$transaction`: updates `client.walletBalance` and creates `walletTransaction` (`credit`/`debit`, running `balance` snapshot, `note`, `createdBy`). |
| GET | `/:id/bookings` | `clients/view` | Bookings for client. |
| GET | `/:id/invoices` | `clients/view` | Invoices for client. |
| GET | `/:id/payments` | `clients/view` | Payments across the client's invoices. |
| GET | `/:id/documents` | `clients/view` | List `clientDocument` (desc by `uploadedAt`). |
| POST | `/:id/documents` | `clients/edit` | Multi-file upload (`multer`, up to `CLIENT_DOC_MAX_FILES`); creates `clientDocument` rows with public URLs. |
| DELETE | `/:id/documents/:docId` | `clients/edit` | Deletes DB row + unlinks file from `UPLOAD_DIR`. |

### `agents.js` — mounted at `/api/agents`
Global `authenticate`. RBAC module `agents`. Agent core fields live on `agent`; commission rate/status/user-link on the 1:1 `agentCommissionProfile` (`AGENT_INCLUDE`), flattened via `flattenAgent`. `sales_agent` role is scoped to their own linked agent (`assertAgentAccess` / `where.commissionProfile.userId === req.userId`). Aggregates (bookings/revenue/pending+paid commission) computed from active bookings' `agentCommission`. **Agent ledger** is here.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description / logic |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `agents/view` | List agents (sales_agent sees only own). Filters: `status` active/inactive, `search` name/email. Returns flattened agents with aggregates. |
| GET | `/me` | `agents/view` | The agent linked to the current user (`commissionProfile.userId`), or `null`. |
| GET | `/:id/summary` | `agents/view` | Agent detail: totals over active bookings + 10 recent bookings (client name, commission amount/status). Enforces `assertAgentAccess` (403 for other agents when sales_agent). |
| GET | `/:id` | `agents/view` | Single flattened agent + aggregates; `assertAgentAccess` guard. |
| GET | `/:id/ledger` | `agents/view` | **Agent ledger**: `{balance: agent.balance, transactions}` (last 100 `agentTransaction`). |
| POST | `/:id/ledger` | `agents/edit` | **Agent ledger entry** in `prisma.$transaction`: updates `agent.balance` and creates `agentTransaction`. Type `deposit`/`adjustment` add, `payment` subtracts (payout); stores `method`, `note`, `createdBy`, running `balance`. |
| POST | `/` | `agents/create` | Create agent + nested `commissionProfile` (clamped rate 0–100, normalized status, optional `userId` validated via `resolveUserId` to a same-tenant user). |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `agents/edit` | Update agent scalars and/or upsert `commissionProfile` (rate/status/userId). Returns flattened + aggregates. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `agents/delete` | Blocks delete if agent has any bookings → **409 `AGENT_HAS_BOOKINGS`** ("Set status to inactive instead"); else tenant-scoped delete. |

### `vendors.js` — mounted at `/api/vendors`
Global `authenticate`. RBAC module `vendors`. Sub-resources: **bills**, **bill payments**, **notes**. Vendor-bill payments sync into the finance `transaction` ledger (`referenceType: "vendor_bill_payment"`).

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description / logic |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `vendors/view` | List tenant vendors (desc). |
| GET | `/reports/payables` | `vendors/view` | Open payables: `vendorBill` with status `unpaid`/`partial`/`overdue`, incl. vendor + payments; hydrated with booking titles. |
| GET | `/:id` | `vendors/view` | Single vendor (tenant-scoped). |
| POST | `/` | `vendors/create` | Create vendor. |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `vendors/edit` | Tenant-scoped update. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `vendors/delete` | Tenant-scoped delete. |
| GET | `/:id/bills` | `vendors/view` | List vendor's bills incl. payments; hydrated with booking titles. |
| POST | `/:id/bills` | `vendors/create` | Create bill under vendor. |
| PATCH | `/:id/bills/:billId` | `vendors/edit` | Update bill (tenant+vendor scoped). |
| DELETE | `/:id/bills/:billId` | `vendors/delete` | Delete bill **and** its synced `transaction` expense rows (by payment ids). |
| POST | `/:id/bills/:billId/payments` | `vendors/edit` | Record a `vendorBillPayment` (`paidBy=userId`); recomputes bill `paidAmount`/`dueAmount`/`status` (paid/partial/unpaid); **syncs an `expense` `transaction`** (`category: vendor_payment`, upsert by `referenceId`). |
| GET | `/:id/notes` | `vendors/view` | List vendor notes. |
| POST | `/:id/notes` | `vendors/edit` | Add vendor note (`createdBy=userId`). |

### `leads.js` — mounted at `/api/leads`
Global `authenticate`. RBAC module `leads`. Pipeline stages: `new → contacted → qualified → quoted → won → lost`. **Lead→client conversion** and status-change activity logging. Fires `dispatchTenantAutomation("lead_created")`.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description / logic |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `leads/view` | List tenant leads (desc). |
| GET | `/pipeline-stats` | `leads/view` | Aggregates: total/won/lost/active, conversion rate, counts byStage and bySource (with per-source conversion). |
| GET | `/check-duplicate` | `leads/view` | Checks if a `client` already exists by email/phone; returns `{exists, client}`. |
| GET | `/:id` | `leads/view` | Single lead. |
| POST | `/` | `leads/create` + `checkPlanLimit("leads")` | Create lead; dispatches `lead_created` automation (name/phone/source). |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `leads/edit` | Tenant-scoped update. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `leads/delete` | Tenant-scoped delete. |
| PATCH | `/:id/status` | `leads/edit` | **Status transition**: updates status, records `leadActivity` (`status_change`, old→new). On `won` (and CRM config `automations.leadWonCreateTask`) auto-creates a follow-up `task`. |
| GET | `/:id/activities` | `leads/view` | List `leadActivity` (desc). |
| POST | `/:id/activities` | `leads/create` | Add activity (`createdBy=userId`). |
| POST | `/:id/convert` | `leads/edit` | **Lead→Client conversion**: reuses existing client (email/phone match) or creates one; sets lead `status=won` + `clientId`; writes `lead/converted` audit. Returns the client. |
| GET | `/:id/quotations` | `leads/view` | Quotations linked to lead. |

### `bookings.js` — mounted at `/api/bookings`
Global `authenticate`. RBAC module `bookings` (except commission approval which uses `agents/approve`). Rich core sales entity: **service-details flattening** (`bookingServiceDetails` lib nests non-scalar fields under `serviceDetails` JSON then flattens on read), inline **client/agent resolution** (creates a client from `clientName`, matches active agents by id/name/email/phone), **package enrichment** (`enrichBookingFromPackage`), auto **profit** derivation (`amount − cost`), and **agent commission sync**. Sub-resources: segments, travelers, checklist, timeline, documents. Status changes logged to `bookingTimelineEvent` + `auditLog`. Fires `dispatchTenantAutomation("booking_created")`.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description / logic |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `bookings/view` | List bookings (`buildListWhere` filters, limit≤1000 default 500, offset). Includes client/agent/package/commission; `formatBooking` flattens. |
| GET | `/:id` | `bookings/view` | Single booking with full detail include (segments/travelers/checklist). |
| POST | `/` | `bookings/create` + `checkPlanLimit("bookings")` | Create booking. `normalizeBookingInput` resolves client (inline-create allowed) + active agent, buries service details, enriches from package, derives profit, defaults `opsStatus=pending`. Then `syncAgentCommission`. Writes `booking/created` audit + `booking_created` automation. |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `bookings/edit` | Update booking via `normalizeBookingInput` (partial-safe profit calc); re-syncs agent commission. |
| PATCH | `/:id/commission` | `agents/approve` | **Commission approval**: set `bookingAgentCommission.agentCommissionStatus` to `pending`/`paid`. 400 if no commission record. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `bookings/delete` | Tenant-scoped delete. |
| GET | `/board/follow-ups` | `bookings/view` | Inquiry bookings (`status:"inquiry"`) bucketed into `due`/`upcoming`/`noDate` by `followUpDate` + counts. |
| PATCH | `/:id/follow-up` | `bookings/edit` | Set/snooze `followUpDate`/`followUpNote`, optional status change → logs `bookingTimelineEvent` on change. |
| PATCH | `/:id/status` | `bookings/edit` | **Status transition**: updates status, always logs `bookingTimelineEvent` (old→new) + `booking/status_changed` audit. |
| GET | `/:id/segments` | `bookings/view` | List `bookingSegment` (flight/service segments). |
| POST | `/:id/segments` | `bookings/edit` | Add segment. |
| DELETE | `/:id/segments/:segId` | `bookings/delete` | Delete segment. |
| GET | `/:id/travelers` | `bookings/view` | List `bookingTraveler`. |
| POST | `/:id/travelers` | `bookings/edit` | Add traveler. |
| DELETE | `/:id/travelers/:travId` | `bookings/delete` | Delete traveler. |
| GET | `/:id/checklist` | `bookings/view` | List `bookingChecklistItem`. |
| POST | `/:id/checklist` | `bookings/edit` | Add checklist item (`label`). |
| PATCH | `/:id/checklist/:itemId` | `bookings/edit` | Toggle `done` (+`doneAt`/`doneBy`). |
| GET | `/:id/timeline` | `bookings/view` | List `bookingTimelineEvent` (desc). |
| POST | `/:id/timeline` | `bookings/edit` | Add timeline event (`createdBy=userId`). |
| GET | `/:id/documents` | `bookings/view` | List `bookingDocument`. |
| POST | `/:id/documents` | `bookings/edit` | Single-file upload (`multer`, 10 MB, PDF/JPG/PNG/WEBP/DOC/DOCX only). |
| DELETE | `/:id/documents/:docId` | `bookings/delete` | Delete document row. |

### `quotations.js` — mounted at `/api/quotations`
Global `authenticate`. RBAC module `quotations` (except conversion which uses `quotations/approve`). Write fields restricted by `QUOTATION_WRITE_FIELDS` allow-list. **Client resolution** on-the-fly (from `clientId`/`leadId`/`clientName`, linking lead→client). Package enrichment via `enrichQuotationFromPackage`. **`sent` status transition** dispatches `quotation_sent` automation and resolves/creates the client. **Quotation→Booking conversion** is the key core-sales bridge.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description / logic |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `quotations/view` | List quotations incl. client/lead (formatted with clientName/leadName). |
| GET | `/:id` | `quotations/view` | Single quotation. |
| POST | `/` | `quotations/create` + `checkPlanLimit("quotations")` | Create. Enriches from package, allow-lists fields, resolves client (from clientName/leadId). If `leadId`: bumps lead to `quoted` (unless won/lost) + logs `leadActivity`. Handles `sent` transition → `quotation_sent` automation. |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `quotations/edit` | Update allow-listed fields; resolves client; runs `sent`-transition handler (fires automation only on first entry into `sent`). |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `quotations/delete` | Tenant-scoped delete. |
| PATCH | `/:id/status` | `quotations/edit` | **Status transition**: updates `status`; runs `sent`-transition handler (client resolution + automation). |
| GET | `/:id/versions` | `quotations/view` | List `quotationVersion` (desc by versionNumber). |
| POST | `/:id/duplicate` | `quotations/create` | Clone quotation as `status:"draft"`, `version:1`, title "(Copy)". |
| POST | `/:id/convert-to-booking` | `quotations/approve` | **Quotation→Booking conversion**: ensures a `clientId` (resolves/creates, 400 if none), creates a `booking` snapshotting title/package/dates/travelerCount and finance (`amount=grandTotal`, `cost=totalCost`, `profit=totalProfit`, `paidAmount=0`, `dueAmount=grandTotal`, `paymentStatus:"unpaid"`, `status:"pending"`, `quotationId` link). Sets quotation `status="approved"`. If `leadId`: marks lead `won` + `clientId` (unless lost). Returns the new booking. |

**Cross-module core-sales flow:** Lead → (`/leads/:id/convert` or quotation auto-link) → Client; Quotation (`sent` auto-creates/links Client) → (`/quotations/:id/convert-to-booking`) → Booking (`quotationId` back-reference, finance snapshot) → agent commission auto-computed from `agentCommissionProfile.commissionRate` and approvable via `PATCH /bookings/:id/commission`. Client wallet ledger (`walletTransaction`) and agent ledger (`agentTransaction`) both use `prisma.$transaction` to keep balance + ledger row atomic. Vendor-bill payments sync into the finance `transaction` ledger.

## routes-finance

# Backend Finance Routes

All finance routers mount under `/api` (see `backend/src/app.js:136-161`). Every router calls `router.use(authenticate)` at the top, so **all endpoints require a valid JWT**. RBAC is enforced per-endpoint via `requirePermission(module, action)` (checks `req.userRole`/permission matrix; `super_admin` always bypasses — `middleware/auth.js:149-151`) or `requireRole(...roles)` (`super_admin` always passes — `auth.js:52-55`). All queries are tenant-scoped by `req.tenantId`.

| Mount point | Router file |
|---|---|
| `/api/invoices` | `routes/invoices.js` |
| `/api/finance` | `routes/finance.js` |
| `/api/payments` | `routes/payments.js` |
| `/api/accounts` | `routes/accounts.js` |
| `/api/transactions` | `routes/crud.js("transaction")` |
| `/api/expenses` | `routes/expenses.js` |
| `/api/tax-rules` | `routes/taxRules.js` |
| `/api/payment-requests` | `routes/paymentRequests.js` |

---

## 1. Invoices — `/api/invoices` (`routes/invoices.js`)

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `invoices:view` | List all tenant invoices, `orderBy createdAt desc`. |
| GET | `/:id` | `invoices:view` | Single invoice (tenant-scoped), 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/` | `invoices:create` | Create invoice. Auto-generates `invoiceNumber` via `getNextInvoiceNumber` (`INV-00001` padded, max sequence+1). Sets `createdBy`, `tenantId`. Writes an `AuditLog` (module `invoice`, action `created`). |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `invoices:edit` | Update invoice (`updateMany` tenant-scoped), returns refreshed row. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `invoices:delete` | Delete invoice (tenant-scoped). |
| PATCH | `/:id/status` | `invoices:edit` | Change status. If `cancelled`, also stores `cancelReason`. Records an `InvoiceAuditEvent` (`type: status_change`, `oldStatus`→`newStatus`). |
| GET | `/:id/payments` | `invoices:view` | List payments for the invoice, `orderBy createdAt desc`. |
| POST | `/:id/payments` | `invoices:create` | **Add payment** (see §Payment posting below). |
| DELETE | `/:id/payments/:payId` | `invoices:delete` | Delete a payment (tenant + invoice scoped). Does NOT recalculate invoice totals. |
| POST | `/:id/payments/:payId/proof` | `invoices:edit` | Multipart upload (`multer`, field `file`) — attaches `proofUrl` (`/uploads/<filename>`) to the payment. |
| GET | `/:id/refunds` | `invoices:view` | List refunds for the invoice. |
| POST | `/:id/refunds` | `invoices:approve` | **Create refund** (see §Refunds). Note: higher RBAC action (`approve`). |
| GET | `/:id/installments` | `invoices:view` | List installments ordered by `sortOrder`, `dueDate`, `createdAt`. |
| POST | `/:id/installments` | `invoices:edit` | Add an installment (`label`, `amount`, `dueDate`, `sortOrder`; `paidAmount:0`, `status:pending`). Logs an `InvoiceAuditEvent` (`type: note`). |
| PATCH | `/:id/installments/:instId` | `invoices:edit` | Update installment; recomputes `status` via `installmentStatus(amount, paidAmount)`. |
| DELETE | `/:id/installments/:instId` | `invoices:edit` | Delete an installment (tenant + invoice scoped). |
| GET | `/:id/audit` | `invoices:view` | Full `InvoiceAuditEvent` trail for the invoice, `orderBy createdAt desc`. |

### Payment posting (`POST /:id/payments`) — full flow
1. Creates a `Payment` with `bookingId` (body or invoice's), `invoiceId`, `receivedBy = req.userId`, `tenantId`.
2. Recomputes invoice totals from all payments: `paidAmount = Σ payments.amount`, `dueAmount = totalAmount − paid`, and `status` = `paid` (paid ≥ total) / `partial` (paid > 0) / `unpaid`.
3. If a `bookingId` exists, rolls up ALL invoices for that booking and updates the `Booking`'s `paidAmount`, `dueAmount`, `paymentStatus`.
4. **Ledger sync** — `ensureLedgerIncomeTransaction(...)` creates an idempotent `Transaction` (`type: income`, `category: invoice_payment`, `referenceType: payment`, `referenceId: payment.id`, `status: completed`). Idempotency is keyed on `(tenantId, referenceType=payment, referenceId=payment.id)`; returns existing if present (`lib/invoiceInstallments.js:33-59`).
5. **Installment allocation** — `allocatePaymentToInstallments(...)` waterfalls the payment amount across installments ordered by `dueDate → sortOrder → createdAt`, filling each up to its `owed = amount − paidAmount`, updating `paidAmount` + `status`, rounded to 2 decimals (`lib/invoiceInstallments.js:7-31`).
6. Records `InvoiceAuditEvent` (`type: payment`, includes `amount`).
7. Writes global `AuditLog` (action `payment_received`).
8. Fires `dispatchTenantAutomation("payment_received", …)` with invoice/client/balance payload (fire-and-forget).

### Refunds (`POST /:id/refunds`)
- Requires `invoices:approve`. Creates `InvoiceRefund` (`amount`, `reason`, optional `method`, `processedBy = req.userId`).
- Recomputes `invoice.refundedAmount = Σ refunds.amount`; sets `status = "refunded"` when `refundedAmount ≥ totalAmount`, else leaves the existing status.

### Invoice audit events (`InvoiceAuditEvent`)
Written for: `status_change` (status PATCH), `payment` (payment posted), and `note` (installment added). Fields: `type`, `content`, `oldStatus`, `newStatus`, `amount`, `createdBy`. Distinct from the global `AuditLog` written on `created` and `payment_received`.

---

## 2. Payments (unified gateway) — `/api/payments` (`routes/payments.js`)

Dispatcher for online gateways. Mounts sub-routers `/sslcommerz` (`routes/sslcommerz`) and `/bkash` (`routes/bkash`).

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| — | `/sslcommerz/*` | (sub-router) | SSLCommerz gateway routes. |
| — | `/bkash/*` | (sub-router) | bKash gateway routes. |
| GET | `/gateway-status` | authenticated only | Returns `getGatewayStatus()` — which online gateways are configured. |
| GET | `/` | authenticated only | List all tenant payments, `orderBy createdAt desc`, includes invoice `{id, invoiceNumber, clientId}`. **No `requirePermission`.** |
| POST | `/initiate` | authenticated only | Universal payment initiation. Requires `gateway` + positive `amount`. `cod` → instant confirmation via `handlePaymentSuccess` + `auditPaymentEvent(payment_cod_confirmed)`; `sslcommerz` → proxies to `/sslcommerz/initiate`; `bkash` → proxies to `/bkash/create`. |
| GET | `/status/:transactionId` | authenticated only | Reads latest `AuditLog` (module `payment_gateway`) for the tran; maps action→status (`success`/`failed`/`cancelled`/`pending`). 404 if no record. |
| POST | `/callback/:gateway` | **none (no `authenticate`)** | Frontend callback validation. Looks up latest gateway `AuditLog` by `tran_id`/`paymentID` and returns mapped status. Public endpoint. |

Gateway payment persistence is done in `services/paymentGateway.js`: `handlePaymentSuccess` creates an idempotent `Payment` (keyed on `transactionRef`), calls `updateInvoiceAndBooking`, then `ensureLedgerTransaction`, and (for `paymentRequestId`) activates a subscription. `auditPaymentEvent` writes a `payment_gateway` `AuditLog` with `actorId: "system"`.

---

## 3. Finance reports — `/api/finance` (`routes/finance.js`)

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/reminders` | `invoices:view` | Due/overdue lists. Returns overdue vs due-soon (7-day horizon) for both **invoices** (status in unpaid/partial/overdue, dueDate-based, take 200) and **installments** (status in pending/partial, with invoice snapshot), plus `counts`. |
| POST | `/reminders/:invoiceId/send` | `invoices:edit` | Fires `dispatchTenantAutomation("invoice_reminder", …)` with client + due details. |
| GET | `/pl` | `reports:view` | **Profit & Loss** (`from`/`to`). Revenue = Σ payments; vendor costs = Σ `VendorBillPayment`; operating expenses = Σ approved/paid `Expense`; tax collected from paid/partial invoices. Returns `revenue{total,taxCollected,netRevenue}`, `costOfSales.vendorCosts`, `grossProfit` (revenue − vendorCosts), `operatingExpenses{total,byCategory}`, `netProfit` (gross − expenses). |
| GET | `/balance-sheet` | `reports:view` | **Balance sheet** snapshot. Assets = cash/bank account balances + accounts receivable (Σ dueAmount of unpaid/partial/overdue invoices). Liabilities = accounts payable (Σ dueAmount of unpaid/partial vendor bills). `equity = assets − liabilities`. |
| GET | `/cash-flow` | `reports:view` | **Cash flow** by month (`YYYY-MM`). Inflows = payments; outflows = approved/paid expenses + vendor bill payments. Returns `summary{totalInflows,totalOutflows,net}` + `monthly[]` with per-month `net`. |
| GET | `/tax-report` | `reports:view` | **Tax collected** from paid/partial invoices with `taxAmount > 0` (filtered by `issuedDate`). Returns `summary{totalSubTotal,totalTaxCollected,invoiceCount}` + invoice rows. |
| GET | `/sales-analytics` | `reports:view` | **Sales/booking analytics** (`from`/`to`). Top 10 destinations (by revenue), agent performance, service-type breakdown, monthly trend, and a conversion funnel (leads→converted, quotations→accepted, bookings→confirmed) with rates. |

## 4. Accounts — `/api/accounts` (`routes/accounts.js`)

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `accounts:view` | List accounts (`orderBy createdAt desc`). |
| GET | `/summary` | `accounts:view` | **AR/AP dashboard**: `totalReceivable`, `totalReceived`, `totalPayable`, overdue receivable/payable, `cashBankBalance` (Σ active account balances), `totalExpenses` (approved), `netProfit = received − payable − expenses`, and receivable/payable/overdue counts. |
| GET | `/ledger` | `accounts:view` | **Account ledger** — all `Transaction` rows for the tenant, `orderBy createdAt desc`. |
| GET | `/profitability` | `accounts:view` | Per-booking profitability (`from`/`to` on `createdAt`). Maps approved expenses to bookings (`bookingId` or `unassigned`); computes `grossProfit`, `netProfit = profit − mapped expenses`, `marginPercent`; returns per-booking rows + `summary` (revenue, cost, expenses, gross/net profit, `overallMargin`, count). |
| GET | `/:id` | `accounts:view` | Single account (tenant-scoped), 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/` | `accounts:create` | Create account (`tenantId` injected). |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `accounts:edit` | Update account (tenant-scoped `updateMany`). |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `accounts:delete` | Delete account (tenant-scoped). |

**Route-ordering note:** `/summary`, `/ledger`, `/profitability` are declared before `/:id`, so they are not shadowed by the param route.

## 5. Transactions (ledger CRUD) — `/api/transactions` (`routes/crud.js("transaction")`)

Generic CRUD factory. For `modelKey = "transaction"`: Prisma model `transaction`, **permission module `accounts`** (`PERMISSION_MODULE_MAP.transaction = "accounts"`).

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `accounts:view` | List all tenant transactions, `orderBy createdAt desc`. |
| GET | `/:id` | `accounts:view` | Single transaction (tenant-scoped), 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/` | `accounts:create` | Create transaction (`tenantId` injected; body passed through directly). |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `accounts:edit` | Update (tenant-scoped `updateMany`), returns refreshed row. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `accounts:delete` | Delete (tenant-scoped). |

The travel-package child sub-routes in `crud.js` do **not** apply to `transaction` (guarded by `isTravelPackage`). Transactions are also written automatically by invoice payments (`type: income`) and expense sync (`type: expense`).

## 6. Expenses — `/api/expenses` (`routes/expenses.js`)

All endpoints use permission module **`accounts`** (not a dedicated `expenses` module).

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `accounts:view` | List tenant expenses, `orderBy createdAt desc`. |
| GET | `/:id` | `accounts:view` | Single expense (tenant-scoped), 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/` | `accounts:create` | Create expense (`createdBy`, `tenantId` injected), then `syncExpenseTransaction`. |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `accounts:edit` | Update expense (tenant-scoped), then `syncExpenseTransaction` on the fresh row. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `accounts:delete` | Deletes the linked `Transaction` (`referenceType: expense`) first, then the expense. |
| POST | `/:id/approve` | `accounts:edit` | Sets `status: approved`, `approvedBy = req.userId`, then `syncExpenseTransaction`. |
| POST | `/:id/reject` | `accounts:edit` | Sets `status: rejected`, then `syncExpenseTransaction` (cancels the ledger row). |

### Expense → Transaction sync (`syncExpenseTransaction`)
- Looks up an existing ledger `Transaction` keyed on `(tenantId, referenceType=expense, referenceId=expense.id)`.
- If expense **not `approved`**: existing transaction (if any) is set to `status: cancelled` and function returns (no ledger entry for pending/rejected).
- If **`approved`**: upserts a `Transaction` (`type: expense`, `category` from expense, `status: completed`, copies `accountId`, `vendorId`, `paymentMethod`, `date`, `createdBy`). Updates in place if it already exists.
- On delete: the linked transaction is hard-deleted before the expense.

## 7. Tax rules — `/api/tax-rules` (`routes/taxRules.js`)

RBAC uses the **`settings`** module (view/edit), not `accounts`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `settings:view` | List all tenant tax rules, `orderBy createdAt asc`. |
| POST | `/` | `settings:edit` | Create rule. Requires `name` + `rate`. Fields: `type` (default `percentage`), `appliesTo` (default `all`), `isDefault`, `isActive` (default true). If `isDefault`, clears any existing default first. |
| PATCH | `/:id` | `settings:edit` | Partial update (only provided fields). If `isDefault`, clears other defaults (excluding self). 404 if not found. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | `settings:edit` | Delete rule (existence-checked, tenant-scoped). |
| GET | `/default` | `settings:view` | Returns the single `isDefault && isActive` rule (or `null`). **Declared after `/:id`** — but it is a GET and `/:id` is also GET, so `/default` would be captured by `GET /:id` param match; the literal `/default` route is registered after the param route, meaning `GET /tax-rules/default` is actually served by `GET /:id` with `id="default"` (returns 404 "Tax rule not found" unless a rule with that id exists). *(Ordering quirk worth noting.)* |

Model `TaxRule`: `name`, `rate` (Float), `type`, `appliesTo`, `isDefault`, `isActive`, indexed on `[tenantId, isActive]`.

## 8. Payment requests (subscription billing) — `/api/payment-requests` (`routes/paymentRequests.js`)

Tenant-facing subscription payment submission (distinct from invoice `Payment`s). Write endpoints require `requireRole("tenant_owner")` (super_admin bypasses); read endpoints only require `authenticate`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/upload-proof` | `role: tenant_owner` | Upload a base64/dataURL payment screenshot (PNG/JPG/JPEG/WEBP, ≤5 MB) to `uploads/payment-proofs/`; returns `proofUrl` + `proofFileName`. |
| GET | `/methods` | authenticated only | Enabled manual payment methods (merges `PaymentMethodConfig` rows with built-in defaults; seeds defaults if empty). |
| GET | `/summary` | authenticated only | Tenant subscription snapshot (`currentPlan`, `subscriptionStatus`, `subscriptionExpiry`) + enabled payment methods. |
| GET | `/` | authenticated only | List tenant's payment requests, `orderBy createdAt desc`. |
| GET | `/:id` | authenticated only | Single payment request (tenant-scoped), 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/online-checkout` | `role: tenant_owner` | Starts an online (sslcommerz/bkash) subscription checkout. 409 if an open request exists; 503 if the gateway is unconfigured. Builds a `pending` `PaymentRequest` and returns `{paymentRequestId, amount, gateway, customer*}`. |
| POST | `/` | `role: tenant_owner` | Create manual payment request. 409 if an open request already exists (`pending/submitted/pending_review/needs_info`). Validates plan/amount/coupon/transactionId (dedupe on trx). Writes `AuditLog` (`payment_request_created`) + super-admin alert. |
| POST | `/:id/resubmit` | `role: tenant_owner` | Resubmit a `rejected`/`needs_info` request; resets review fields to `pending`. Writes `AuditLog` (`payment_request_resubmitted`) + alert. |
| POST | `/:id/cancel` | `role: tenant_owner` | Cancel an open request (status → `cancelled`, `processedAt` set). Writes `AuditLog` (`payment_request_cancelled`). |

**Business logic (`buildPaymentRequestInput`):** normalizes plan/billing-cycle/status; resolves `requestType` (activate/renew/upgrade/downgrade); prices via `getPlanPrice`; applies coupon via `applyCouponToPaymentRequest` (discount/final amount); requires a `transactionId` for manual methods (bkash/nagad/rocket/bank_transfer); rejects duplicate transaction references against non-rejected requests.

## Cross-cutting financial data model (relevant fields)

- **Invoice** (`schema.prisma:623`): `invoiceNumber`, `bookingId`(req), `clientId`, `totalAmount`, `paidAmount`, `dueAmount`, `refundedAmount`, `status` (unpaid default), `dueDate`, `issuedDate`, `cancelReason`, `taxRuleId`, `taxRate`, `taxAmount`, `subTotal`; relations `payments`, `refunds`, `auditTrail`, `installments`.
- **InvoiceInstallment** (`:660`): `label`, `amount`, `dueDate`, `paidAmount`, `status` (pending), `sortOrder`.
- **Payment** (`:676`): `invoiceId`(req), `bookingId`(req), `amount`, `method`, `transactionRef`, `proofUrl`, `date`, `receivedBy`.
- **InvoiceRefund** (`:696`): `amount`, `reason`, `method`, `processedBy`.
- **InvoiceAuditEvent** (`:707`): `type`, `content`, `oldStatus`, `newStatus`, `amount`, `createdBy`.
- **Account** (`:768`): `name`, `type` (cash/bank/etc.), `balance`, `accountNumber`, `bankName`, `status` (active); relations `transactions`, `expenses`.
- **Transaction** (`:785`) — the ledger: `accountId`, `type` (income/expense), `category`, `description`, `amount`, `referenceId`, `referenceType` (payment/expense), `clientId`, `bookingId`, `vendorId`, `invoiceId`, `paymentMethod`, `status` (completed default), `date`, `createdBy`.
- **Expense** (`:808`): `category`, `description`, `amount`, `date`, `paymentMethod`, `vendorId`, `accountId`, `approvedBy`, `status` (pending default).
- **TaxRule** (`:1412`): `name`, `rate`, `type`, `appliesTo`, `isDefault`, `isActive`.

**Note — the `dueAmount`-based reports** (`/finance/balance-sheet`, `/finance/reminders`, `/accounts/summary`) rely on `invoice.dueAmount`/`status` being kept current, which is only updated on payment posting (`POST /invoices/:id/payments`) — NOT on payment deletion (`DELETE /:id/payments/:payId` does no recalculation), so a deleted payment leaves stale `paidAmount`/`dueAmount`/`status` and stale ledger `Transaction`.

Key files:
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/src/routes/invoices.js`
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/src/routes/payments.js`

## routes-products

## Travel-Product Routes

All routers in this area apply `router.use(authenticate)` globally (JWT auth + tenant resolution: populates `req.tenantId`, `req.userId`, `req.userRole`). Every query/mutation is tenant-scoped by `tenantId`. RBAC is enforced per-route via `requirePermission(module, action)` — except **Hajj**, which is authenticated-only with no permission gate. Mount paths are defined in `backend/src/app.js` (lines 148–159).

> **Note on assignment scope:** There is **no BSP-upload route** anywhere under `backend/src/routes/` (verified by grep across all route files and `app.js`). The air-ticket routers (`ticketRefunds.js`, `ticketVoids.js`, `ticketReissues.js`) contain no BSP/import/upload logic. If BSP reconciliation exists, it is not in this area.

### Mount Map (`backend/src/app.js`)

| Router file | Mount base path |
|---|---|
| `groupTours.js` | `/api/group-tours` |
| `mice.js` | `/api/mice` |
| `travelPolicy.js` | `/api/travel-policy` |
| `visa.js` | `/api/visa` |
| `visaStock.js` | `/api/visa-stock` |
| `inventory.js` | `/api/inventory` |
| `recruitment.js` | `/api/recruitment` |
| `ticketRefunds.js` | `/api/ticket-refunds` |
| `ticketVoids.js` | `/api/ticket-voids` |
| `ticketReissues.js` | `/api/ticket-reissues` |
| `flightReminders.js` | `/api/flight-reminders` |
| `hajj.js` | `/api/hajj` |

### Visa Applications (`visa.js` → `/api/visa`)

Model: `visaApplication`. Manages passport/embassy visa applications, optionally linked to a client and a booking.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/visa` | `bookings:view` | List visa applications (desc by createdAt). Filters: `status`; `search` matches applicantName / passportNumber / destination (case-insensitive OR). Includes client (id,name,phone) + booking (id,title). |
| POST | `/api/visa` | `bookings:create` | Create application. Required: `applicantName`. Accepts clientId, bookingId, passportNumber, nationality, visaType, destination, appliedDate, appointmentDate, decisionDate, expiryDate, status (default `not_applied`), referenceNo, embassyFee, serviceFee, notes. Sets `createdBy`. |
| GET | `/api/visa/:id` | `bookings:view` | Fetch one (tenant-scoped). Includes client (id,name,phone,email) + booking (id,title). 404 if not found. |
| PATCH | `/api/visa/:id` | `bookings:edit` | Partial update. String fields, float fields (embassyFee, serviceFee), and date fields each parsed conditionally on presence. 404 if not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/visa/:id` | `bookings:delete` | Delete after tenant-scoped existence check. 404 if not found. |
| GET | `/api/visa/stats/summary` | `bookings:view` | Returns per-status counts for statuses: `not_applied, applied, in_progress, approved, rejected, collected`. |

**Statuses:** not_applied, applied, in_progress, approved, rejected, collected.

### Visa Stock Inventory (`visaStock.js` → `/api/visa-stock`)

Model: `visaStock`. Tradeable visa inventory (buy/sell with computed profit). Allowed writable fields: visaType, country, duration, sponsorId, visaId, occupation, buyingPrice, sellingPrice, status, buyerName, agentId, notes.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/visa-stock` | `bookings:view` | List stock (desc by createdAt). Optional `status` filter. |
| GET | `/api/visa-stock/stats` | `bookings:view` | Aggregate: total, per-status counts (available/processing/sold/completed), `stockValue` (sum buyingPrice where available|processing), `soldProfit` (sum profit where sold|completed). |
| GET | `/api/visa-stock/:id` | `bookings:view` | Fetch one (tenant-scoped). 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/visa-stock` | `bookings:create` | Create. Required: `visaType`. `profit` computed = sellingPrice − buyingPrice. Sets createdBy. |
| PATCH | `/api/visa-stock/:id` | `bookings:edit` | Update via `updateMany` (tenant-scoped). Recomputes `profit` from effective buying/selling prices. Returns refreshed record. |
| POST | `/api/visa-stock/:id/sell` | `bookings:edit` | Mark sold: sets status=`sold`, buyerName, agentId, sellingPrice; recomputes profit = sellingPrice − buyingPrice. |
| DELETE | `/api/visa-stock/:id` | `bookings:delete` | Delete via `deleteMany` (tenant-scoped). 404 if count 0. |

**Statuses:** available, processing, sold, completed.

### Group Tours + Bookings (`groupTours.js` → `/api/group-tours`)

Models: `groupTour`, `groupTourBooking` (join to `booking`). Manages fixed-departure group tours and seat/room assignments of bookings.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/group-tours` | `bookings:view` | List tours (asc by departureDate). Includes `_count.bookings`. |
| POST | `/api/group-tours` | `bookings:create` | Create tour. Required: `name`. Accepts destination, departureDate, returnDate, capacity (int, default 0), status (default `upcoming`), notes. Sets createdBy. |
| GET | `/api/group-tours/:id` | `bookings:view` | Full manifest: tour + bookings (ordered by seatNumber), each with nested booking → client (id,name,phone,email) + travelers. 404 if not found. |
| PATCH | `/api/group-tours/:id` | `bookings:edit` | Partial update of name, destination, departureDate, returnDate, capacity (int), status, notes. 404 if not found. |

| DELETE | `/api/group-tours/:id/bookings/:bookingId` | `bookings:edit` | Remove a booking from the tour (`deleteMany` on groupTourId+bookingId). |

### MICE Events + Items (`mice.js` → `/api/mice`)

Models: `miceEvent`, `miceEventItem`. Meetings/Incentives/Conferences/Exhibitions events with cost line-items.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/mice` | `bookings:view` | List events (desc by createdAt). Filters: `status`; `search` on title (case-insensitive contains). Includes client (id,name,phone) + `_count.items`. |
| POST | `/api/mice` | `bookings:create` | Create event. Required: `title`. Accepts eventType (default `conference`), clientId, venue, city, country, startDate, endDate, pax (int, default 1), budget (float, default 0), status (default `inquiry`), notes. Sets createdBy. |
| GET | `/api/mice/:id` | `bookings:view` | Fetch one (tenant-scoped). Includes client (id,name,phone,email) + items (asc by createdAt). 404 if not found. |
| PATCH | `/api/mice/:id` | `bookings:edit` | Partial update of title, eventType, clientId, venue, city, country, startDate, endDate, pax, budget, status, notes. 404 if not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/mice/:id` | `bookings:delete` | Delete after tenant existence check. 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/mice/:id/items` | `bookings:edit` | Add cost line-item. Validates event belongs to tenant. itemType (default `venue`), description, quantity (int, default 1), unitCost (float, default 0), `totalCost` = qty×cost, vendorId, notes. |
| PATCH | `/api/mice/:id/items/:itemId` | `bookings:edit` | Update line-item. Event scoped to tenant first, then item scoped to event (guards cross-tenant IDOR). Recomputes `totalCost` from effective qty/cost so single-field updates don't zero the total. 404 if event/item not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/mice/:id/items/:itemId` | `bookings:edit` | Delete line-item (scoped to tenant's event via deleteMany). 404 if event or item not found. |

**Event statuses:** default `inquiry`. **Event types:** default `conference`. **Item types:** default `venue`.

### Hajj Packages / Groups / Pilgrims / Payments (`hajj.js` → `/api/hajj`)

Models: `hajjPackage`, `hajjGroup`, `hajjPilgrim`, `hajjPilgrimPayment`. **RBAC: authenticated only — no `requirePermission` gate on any Hajj route** (imports only `authenticate, prisma`; no `requireRole`/`requirePermission`). All mutations pass `req.body` through with minimal validation.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/hajj/packages` | auth only | List Hajj packages (desc by createdAt). |
| GET | `/api/hajj/packages/:id` | auth only | Fetch one package (tenant-scoped). 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/hajj/packages` | auth only | Create package (spreads req.body + tenantId). |
| PATCH | `/api/hajj/packages/:id` | auth only | Update package after tenant existence check. 404 if not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/hajj/packages/:id` | auth only | Delete package via deleteMany (tenant-scoped). Always returns `{success:true}`. |
| GET | `/api/hajj/groups` | auth only | List groups (desc by createdAt). Optional `packageId` filter. |
| POST | `/api/hajj/groups` | auth only | Create group (spreads req.body + tenantId). |
| PATCH | `/api/hajj/groups/:id` | auth only | Update group after tenant existence check. 404 if not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/hajj/groups/:id` | auth only | Delete group via deleteMany (tenant-scoped). |
| GET | `/api/hajj/pilgrims` | auth only | List pilgrims (desc by createdAt). Optional `packageId` filter. |
| POST | `/api/hajj/pilgrims` | auth only | Create pilgrim; then recounts pilgrims for the package and updates `hajjPackage.enrolled`. |
| PATCH | `/api/hajj/pilgrims/:id` | auth only | Update pilgrim after tenant existence check. 404 if not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/hajj/pilgrims/:id` | auth only | Delete pilgrim (tenant-scoped find first); recounts & updates package `enrolled`. 404 if not found. |
| GET | `/api/hajj/pilgrims/:id/payments` | auth only | List a pilgrim's payments (desc by createdAt). Validates pilgrim belongs to tenant. 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/hajj/pilgrims/:id/payments` | auth only | Record a payment (`receivedBy` = req.userId). Recomputes pilgrim `paidAmount` (sum), `dueAmount` (totalAmount − paid), and `paymentStatus` (paid / partial / unpaid). 404 if pilgrim not found. |

**Payment statuses (derived):** paid (paid ≥ total), partial (paid > 0), unpaid.
