### Travel Policy + Approvals (`travelPolicy.js` → `/api/travel-policy`)

Models: `travelPolicy`, `travelApprovalRequest`. Corporate travel policy definitions and per-trip approval workflow. Note the **split RBAC module**: policies use `settings:*`, approvals use `bookings:*`.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/travel-policy/policies` | `settings:view` | List policies (desc by createdAt). |
| POST | `/api/travel-policy/policies` | `settings:edit` | Create policy. Required: `name`. Fields: maxFlightBudget, maxHotelPerNight, maxMealPerDay (floats), requiresApproval (default true), approverRole (default `manager`), advanceNoticeDays (int, default 3), allowedClasses (array, default []), isActive (default true), notes. |
| PATCH | `/api/travel-policy/policies/:id` | `settings:edit` | Partial update (name, approverRole, notes; the three budget floats; requiresApproval; isActive; advanceNoticeDays; allowedClasses). 404 if not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/travel-policy/policies/:id` | `settings:edit` | Delete after tenant existence check. 404 if not found. |
| GET | `/api/travel-policy/approvals` | `bookings:view` | List approval requests (desc by createdAt). Optional `status` filter. Includes policy (id,name). |
| POST | `/api/travel-policy/approvals` | `bookings:create` | Create approval request. `requestedBy` = req.userId. Fields: policyId, destination, travelDate, returnDate, purpose, estimatedCost (float), bookingId. |
| PATCH | `/api/travel-policy/approvals/:id` | `bookings:edit` | Approve/reject. On status `approved`/`rejected` sets `approvedBy` = req.userId. Accepts rejectionNote. 404 if not found. |

**Approval statuses:** approved, rejected (plus initial/pending default from schema).

### Air-Ticket Refunds (`ticketRefunds.js` → `/api/ticket-refunds`)

Model: `ticketRefund`. Net refund = `airlineRefund + taxRefund − agencyFee` (helper `calcNet`).

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/ticket-refunds` | `bookings:view` | List refunds (desc by createdAt). Optional `status` filter. Includes booking (id,title). |
| GET | `/api/ticket-refunds/:id` | `bookings:view` | Fetch one (tenant-scoped). Includes booking (id,title). 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/ticket-refunds` | `bookings:create` | Create. Fields: bookingId, refundRef, pnr, ticketNumber, clientName, airline, originalFare, airlineRefund, taxRefund, agencyFee, reason, status (default `requested`), refundMethod, notes. `netRefundAmount` computed. |
| PATCH | `/api/ticket-refunds/:id` | `bookings:edit` | Partial update. Recomputes `netRefundAmount` from merged record if any numeric field changes. On status `completed`/`approved` sets `processedAt`. 404 if not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/ticket-refunds/:id` | `bookings:delete` | Delete via deleteMany (tenant-scoped). 404 if count 0. |

**Statuses:** requested (default), approved, completed.

### Air-Ticket Reissues (`ticketReissues.js` → `/api/ticket-reissues`)

Model: `ticketReissue`. `fareDifference = newFare − originalFare`; `totalCharge = fareDifference + reissueFee + agencyFee` (helpers `calcFareDiff`, `calcTotal`).

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/ticket-reissues` | `bookings:view` | List reissues (desc by createdAt). Optional `status` filter. Includes booking (id,title). |
| GET | `/api/ticket-reissues/:id` | `bookings:view` | Fetch one (tenant-scoped). Includes booking (id,title). 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/ticket-reissues` | `bookings:create` | Create. Fields: bookingId, reissueRef, pnr, ticketNumber, clientName, airline, originalRoute, newRoute, originalDate, newDate, originalFare, newFare, fareDifference (defaults to newFare−originalFare), reissueFee, agencyFee, reason, status (default `requested`), notes. `totalCharge` computed. |
| PATCH | `/api/ticket-reissues/:id` | `bookings:edit` | Partial update. Recomputes fareDifference + totalCharge from merged record when numeric fields change. On status `completed` sets `completedAt = now`. 404 if not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/ticket-reissues/:id` | `bookings:delete` | Delete via deleteMany (tenant-scoped). 404 if count 0. |

**Statuses:** requested (default), completed.

### Flight / Departure Reminders (`flightReminders.js` → `/api/flight-reminders`)

Reads from `booking` + `auditLog`; triggers notifications. Depends on services: `travelDepartureReminderService.processTravelDepartureReminders`, `notificationService.notifyEvent`, `lib/staffInAppNotification.notifyTenantStaff`. **No `requirePermission` gate** on the read/send routes (authenticated only); `run-now` is role-gated.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/flight-reminders/upcoming` | auth only | Upcoming departures within `days` (query, default 7, max 30). Selects bookings with `travelDateFrom` in [today, today+days] and status in confirmed/ticketed/traveling/pending. For each: computes `daysLeft` and `reminderSentToday` (checks auditLog for `departure_reminder_{daysLeft}` or `departure_reminder_manual` action today). Includes client (id,name,phone,email). Returns `{bookings, days}`. |
| POST | `/api/flight-reminders/send/:bookingId` | auth only | Manually send a departure reminder for one booking. Fires `notifyEvent("travel_departure_reminder", …)` (client name/phone/email, destination, travelDate, daysLeft, company name), notifies tenant staff in-app, and writes an `auditLog` with action `departure_reminder_manual`. 404 if booking not found. |
| POST | `/api/flight-reminders/run-now` | `requireRole("super_admin","tenant_owner")` | Manually trigger the batch departure-reminder job (`processTravelDepartureReminders`). Returns job result. |

---

### Cross-Cutting Notes

- **Tenant isolation:** every read/write is filtered by `tenantId`; single-record ops use `findFirst({ id, tenantId })` guards or `deleteMany/updateMany({ id, tenantId })` before/instead of an unscoped `update`/`delete` by id.
- **RBAC module mapping:** visa, visa-stock, group-tours, mice, inventory, ticket-refunds/voids/reissues, and travel-policy *approvals* all use the **`bookings`** permission module. Travel-policy *policies* use **`settings`**. Recruitment uses **`team`**. **Hajj has no permission gate** (authenticated users only). Flight-reminders `upcoming`/`send` have no permission gate; `run-now` requires role `super_admin` or `tenant_owner`.
- **Computed/denormalized fields:** visaStock `profit`; mice item `totalCost`; hajj pilgrim `paidAmount`/`dueAmount`/`paymentStatus` and package `enrolled`; ticketRefund `netRefundAmount`; ticketReissue `fareDifference`/`totalCharge`.
- **Error handling:** uniformly `try/catch` → HTTP 500. Product routes (visa, mice, group-tours, travel-policy, inventory, recruitment, flight-reminders) return `{message:"Server error"}` / a fixed string; visaStock, hajj, and the three ticket routers return `{message: err.message}` (leak the raw error text to the client).

Relevant file paths (all absolute):
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/src/routes/visa.js`
- `/var/www/hearth-core-app/.claude/worktrees/adoring-wiles-c6b642/backend/src/routes/visaStock.js`

## routes-crm-ops

# CRM & Operations Routes

All routes below share a common middleware chain applied at the application layer and within each router:

- **Global `/api` gate** (`app.js:113`): every `/api/*` request first passes `subscriptionAccessGate` (blocks tenants whose subscription is inactive/expired).
- **`authenticate`** (`router.use(authenticate)` at the top of every file): requires a valid `Bearer` JWT. Rejects `pending` (403 `PENDING_APPROVAL`), `rejected` (403 `REJECTED`), and non-`active` (403 `INACTIVE`) users. On success sets `req.userId`, `req.tenantId`, `req.userRole`. Every query is tenant-scoped by `req.tenantId`.
- **RBAC helpers** (`middleware/auth.js`):
  - `requirePermission(module, action)` — checks the `ROLE_PERMISSIONS` matrix. `super_admin` always bypasses. Returns 403 `Forbidden: {role} cannot {action} {module}` otherwise.
  - `requireRole(...roles)` — `super_admin` always passes; else the caller's role must be in the list. Returns 403 `Forbidden: requires role X or Y`.
  - `requireFeature(flag)` — gates by subscription-plan feature flag (`planHasFeature`); `super_admin` bypasses. Returns 403 `FEATURE_NOT_IN_PLAN`.

**Role → permission-module reference** (relevant modules only; `super_admin` bypasses all):

| Module used here | tenant_owner | manager | sales_agent | accountant | operations |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `clients` | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | v/c/e | view | view |
| `tasks` | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | v/c/e | view | v/c/e |
| `agents` | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | view | view | view |
| `bookings` | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | v/c/e | view | v/c/e/exp |
| `invoices` | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | v/c/e/appr/exp | view | v/c/e/appr/exp | view |
| `team` | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | view | — | — | — |
| `settings` | v/c/e/d/appr/exp | — | — | — | — |

(v=view, c=create, e=edit, d=delete, appr=approve, exp=export.) Note: `team` write actions (`create`/`edit`/`delete`) are held by **tenant_owner only** at the permission-matrix level; `manager` has `team: view` only. Where HRM/payroll endpoints use `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")` instead of `requirePermission("team", …)`, managers regain write access via the role check.

---

## Complaints — `/api/complaints`
File: `backend/src/routes/complaints.js`. Model: `Complaint`. Deliberately reuses the `clients` permission module (no dedicated key). Editable fields (`ALLOWED`): `subject`, `description`, `clientId`, `clientName`, `bookingId`, `category`, `priority`, `status`, `assignedTo`, `resolution`, `feedback`, `rating` (coerced to Number or null).

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/complaints` | `clients:view` | List complaints for tenant, newest first. Optional `?status=` filter. |
| GET | `/api/complaints/stats` | `clients:view` | `groupBy` status counts → `{ open, in_progress, resolved, closed, total }`. |
| GET | `/api/complaints/:id` | `clients:view` | Single complaint (tenant-scoped); 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/complaints` | `clients:create` | Create complaint (`subject` required → 400). Stamps `createdBy`. **Automation:** if `crmConfig.automations.complaintNotifyOwner` is on, creates a `Notification` (type `complaint`, link `/complaints`) for the tenant owner. |
| PATCH | `/api/complaints/:id` | `clients:edit` | Update fields. **Status workflow:** moving into `resolved`/`closed` stamps `resolvedAt` (if unset); moving back to `open`/`in_progress` clears `resolvedAt`. |
| DELETE | `/api/complaints/:id` | `clients:delete` | Hard delete (tenant-scoped); 404 if none. |

**Complaint status lifecycle:** `open` → `in_progress` → `resolved` → `closed` (reopenable to `open`/`in_progress`). `resolvedAt` auto-managed by the PATCH handler.

---

## Campaigns — `/api/campaigns`
File: `backend/src/routes/campaigns.js`. Model: `Campaign`. **Entire router feature-gated by `requireFeature("hasMarketingTools")`** (Business/Ultimate plans) — applied via `router.use(...)` after `authenticate`, so all endpoints below require the feature *and* the listed permission. Editable fields (`ALLOWED`): `name`, `channel`, `subject`, `body`, `audienceType`, `audienceValue`, `status`. `MAX_RECIPIENTS = 1000`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/campaigns` | feature + `clients:view` | List campaigns, newest first. |
| GET | `/api/campaigns/audience-preview` | feature + `clients:view` | Resolve segment size. Query: `type`, `value`, `channel` (default `sms`) → `{ count }`. |
| GET | `/api/campaigns/:id` | feature + `clients:view` | Single campaign; 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/campaigns` | feature + `clients:create` | Create campaign (`name` required → 400). Forced `status: "draft"`, stamps `createdBy`. |
| PATCH | `/api/campaigns/:id` | feature + `clients:edit` | Update campaign. **Blocked (400) if `status === "sent"`.** |
| DELETE | `/api/campaigns/:id` | feature + `clients:delete` | Delete campaign; 404 if none. |
| POST | `/api/campaigns/:id/send` | feature + `clients:edit` | **Execute campaign.** Rejects if already `sent` (400) or empty body (400). Resolves audience, caps at 1000, sends per-channel, records counts. |

**Audience resolution (`resolveAudience`):** filters `Client` by tenant; `audienceType` `tag` → `tags has value`, `clientType` → `clientType = value`; then keeps only recipients contactable on the channel (`email` requires `email`, else requires `phone`).

**Channels & delivery:** `sms` (`smsService.sendSms`), `whatsapp` (`whatsappService.sendWhatsApp`, tenant-scoped), `email` (`emailService.sendEmail`, subject falls back to campaign name, body newlines → `<br/>`). Body templating: `{{name}}` (case-insensitive) → client name.

**Send result:** on completion sets `status: "sent"`, `recipientCount`, `sentCount`, `failedCount`, `sentAt`. Per-recipient failures are counted (`failed++`) but do not abort the run.

---

## CRM Analytics — `/api/crm-analytics`
File: `backend/src/routes/crmAnalytics.js`. Read-only aggregation endpoint (in-JS aggregation over per-tenant data).

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/crm-analytics` | `clients:view` | CRM analytics dashboard. Optional `?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD` (applied to lead `createdAt` only). |

**Data sources:** `Lead` (status, source, budget, assignedTo — date-filtered), `User`, `Booking` (clientId), `Complaint` (status, rating), `Campaign` (sentCount).

**Metrics returned:**
- **`leads`** — `total`, `won`, `lost`, `conversionRate` (% won of total), `lostRate` (% lost of total), and a `funnel` array over statuses `[new, contacted, qualified, quoted, won, lost]` each with `count` and `pct`.
- **`bySource`** — per lead source: `total`, `won`, `value` (sum of won budgets), `conversion` %; sorted by total desc.
- **`byAssignee`** — per executive (User name; `Unassigned` fallback): `total`, `won`, `conversion` %; sorted by won desc then total.
- **`retention`** — `totalCustomers` (distinct clients with bookings), `repeatCustomers` (>1 booking), `repeatRate` %.
- **`complaints`** — `total`, `open` (open+in_progress), `resolved` (resolved+closed), `avgRating` (mean of rated, 1-dp; null if none).
- **`campaigns`** — `total`, `messagesSent` (sum of `sentCount`).

---

## CRM Settings / Config — `/api/crm-settings`
File: `backend/src/routes/crmSettings.js`. Model: `CrmConfig` (one per tenant, auto-created with defaults on first access). Exports `getOrCreate` for reuse (consumed by complaints automation).

**Defaults:** `leadSources` = [website, facebook, referral, walk_in, phone, whatsapp]; `followUpTypes` = [call, whatsapp, email, meeting, site_visit]; `complaintCategories` = [general, booking, payment, service, refund, staff]; `customFields` = []; `automations` = `{ leadWonCreateTask: false, complaintNotifyOwner: false }`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/crm-settings` | `clients:view` | Get (or lazily create) the tenant CRM config. |
| PATCH | `/api/crm-settings` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager","owner")` | Update CRM config. Owner/manager only. |

**PATCH validation:** `leadSources` / `followUpTypes` / `complaintCategories` are de-duplicated, trimmed, non-empty string lists. `customFields` entries require a `label`; each normalized to `{ key (slugified label), label, type ∈ {text,number,date,select} (default text), appliesTo ∈ {lead,client} (default client), options[] }`. `automations` coerced to booleans `{ leadWonCreateTask, complaintNotifyOwner }`.

## Support Tickets — `/api/support-tickets`
File: `backend/src/routes/supportTickets.js`. Model: `SupportTicket`. Uses the **`tasks`** permission module. Auto-numbered `ST-0001`… (`nextTicketNumber` = count+1, zero-padded to 4). Responses enriched with `assigneeName`, `creatorName`, `clientName`, `clientPhone` (via `enrichTickets`), plus included `client` and `booking` relations.

Valid statuses: `open`, `assigned`, `in_progress`, `resolved`, `closed`. Valid priorities: `low`, `medium`, `high`, `urgent`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/support-tickets` | `tasks:view` | List tickets, newest first. Filters: `?status=`, `?assignedTo=`, `?clientId=`. Includes client+booking. |
| GET | `/api/support-tickets/:id` | `tasks:view` | Single enriched ticket; 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/support-tickets` | `tasks:create` | Create ticket (`subject` required → 400). Priority defaults `medium`; `category` default `general`; `source` default `internal`. Status = `assigned` if `assignedTo` provided else `open`. Auto ticketNumber; stamps `createdBy`. |
| PATCH | `/api/support-tickets/:id` | `tasks:edit` | Partial update (subject/description/priority/category/source/clientId/bookingId/status/resolutionNotes). Invalid priority/status silently ignored. Setting status `resolved`/`closed` stamps `resolvedAt`. |
| POST | `/api/support-tickets/:id/assign` | `tasks:edit` | Assign/unassign. Sets `assignedTo`; status → `assigned` (or `open` if cleared). |
| POST | `/api/support-tickets/:id/resolve` | `tasks:edit` | Mark `resolved`, store `resolutionNotes` (keeps existing if blank), stamp `resolvedAt`. |
| DELETE | `/api/support-tickets/:id` | `tasks:delete` | Hard delete; 404 if none. |

---

## Client / Booking Documents — `/api/documents`
File: `backend/src/routes/documents.js`. Read-only, aggregates two models.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/documents` | `clients:view` | Merged, unified list of all `ClientDocument` + `BookingDocument` for the tenant, sorted by `uploadedAt` desc. |

**Unified item shape:** `{ id, source ("client"|"booking"), sourceId, sourceLabel, name, type, url, uploadedAt }`. `sourceLabel` for client docs = client name; for booking docs = booking title → package title snapshot → client name → "Booking". (This route only reads/lists documents; upload/delete of the underlying docs lives on the clients/bookings routers.)

---

## Loyalty — `/api/loyalty`
File: `backend/src/routes/loyalty.js`. Models: `LoyaltyRule` (one/tenant), `LoyaltyAccount` (one/client), `LoyaltyTransaction`. Tiers: `standard` → `silver` → `gold` → `platinum` (by `totalPoints` vs thresholds).

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/loyalty/rules` | `settings:view` | Get tenant loyalty rule (or null). |
| PUT | `/api/loyalty/rules` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")` | Upsert loyalty rule. Fields: `pointsPerUnit`(dflt 1), `unitAmount`(100), `redemptionValue`(1), `expiryDays`(365), `silverThreshold`(1000), `goldThreshold`(5000), `platinumThreshold`(15000), `isActive`(≠false). |
| GET | `/api/loyalty/accounts/:clientId` | `clients:view` | Get/create account for client; returns account + `availablePoints` (`total−used`) + last 50 transactions + rule. 404 if client not in tenant. |
| POST | `/api/loyalty/accounts/:clientId/award` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")` | Manually award points (must be >0 → 400). Creates `earned` txn (with `expiresAt` from rule), increments `totalPoints`, recomputes tier. |
| POST | `/api/loyalty/accounts/:clientId/redeem` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")` | Redeem points as invoice discount (>0, ≤ available → else 400). Creates `redeemed` txn (negative points), increments `usedPoints`. Returns `discountAmount` = `points × redemptionValue`. |
| POST | `/api/loyalty/award-on-payment` | `invoices:edit` | Auto-award on payment (called internally). Skips if no/inactive rule or points≤0. `points = floor(paymentAmount / unitAmount × pointsPerUnit)`. Creates `earned` txn (ref invoice), increments totals, recomputes tier. |
| GET | `/api/loyalty/leaderboard` | `clients:view` | Top 20 accounts by `totalPoints` desc, with client contact info. |

**Account fields:** `totalPoints`, `usedPoints`, `expiredPoints`, `tier`. **Transaction types:** `earned`, `redeemed` (with `points`, `description`, `referenceId`, `referenceType`, `expiresAt`). Award/redeem/auto-award all run inside a `prisma.$transaction`.

---

## Referrals — `/api/referrals`
File: `backend/src/routes/referrals.js`. Models: `ReferralCode`, `ReferralConversion`. Uses the **`agents`** permission module (except convert = `bookings`, pay = role-based). Code format `{PREFIX}-{6char}`; prefix `CLI` for `ownerType==="client"`, else `AGT` (default owner type `agent`).

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/referrals` | `agents:view` | List all referral codes, newest first, with conversion counts. |
| POST | `/api/referrals` | `agents:create` | Generate a code (`ownerId` required → 400). Returns existing active code if owner already has one (idempotent). Unique-code retry ≤5. Default `commissionRate` 5%. |
| GET | `/api/referrals/owner/:ownerId` | `agents:view` | Codes for a specific owner, with last 20 conversions + counts. |
| POST | `/api/referrals/convert` | `bookings:create` | Record a conversion for a booking (`code` required → 400; 404 if code missing/inactive). Idempotent per `(code, bookingId)` — returns existing conversion if duplicate. Creates `pending` conversion + increments `usageCount`. |
| PATCH | `/api/referrals/conversions/:id/pay` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")` | Mark conversion commission `paid`, stamp `paidAt`. |
| GET | `/api/referrals/summary` | `agents:view` | Per-code summary: `usageCount`, `totalEarned`, `paidEarned`, `pendingEarned`, `isActive`. |

**ReferralCode fields:** `code`, `ownerType` (agent/client), `ownerId`, `commissionRate`, `isActive`, `usageCount`. **ReferralConversion fields:** `referralCodeId`, `referredClientId`, `bookingId`, `commissionEarned`, `status` (`pending`→`paid`), `paidAt`.

---

## HRM — `/api/hrm`
File: `backend/src/routes/hrm.js`. Models: `StaffProfile` (1:1 with User), `StaffAttendance`, `StaffLeaveRequest`. Uses the **`team`** permission module; some writes additionally scoped by an in-handler `isManager` role check. `MANAGER_ROLES` = {super_admin, tenant_owner, owner, manager}.

Enumerations: attendance statuses {`present`, `absent`, `half_day`, `remote`, `on_leave`}; leave types {`annual`, `sick`, `unpaid`, `other`}; leave statuses {`pending`, `approved`, `rejected`}.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/hrm/profiles` | `team:view` | List all non-rejected tenant users, each enriched with their `StaffProfile` (or null). Sorted by name. |
| PUT | `/api/hrm/profiles/:userId` | `team:edit` | Upsert a staff profile: `jobTitle`, `department`, `joinDate`, `emergencyContact`, `emergencyPhone`, `notes`. 404 if user not in tenant. |
| GET | `/api/hrm/attendance` | `team:view` | Attendance for a `?date=` (default today), optional `?userId=`. Enriched with `userName`. |
| POST | `/api/hrm/attendance` | `team:edit` | Upsert attendance for `(tenant,user,date)`. Status validated (default `present`). **Non-managers can only mark their own attendance** (else 403). Fields: `checkIn`, `checkOut`, `notes`; stamps `markedBy`. |
| GET | `/api/hrm/leave` | `team:view` | List leave requests, newest first, enriched with `userName` + `reviewerName`. Optional `?status=`. **Non-managers see only their own** requests. |
| POST | `/api/hrm/leave` | `team:create` | Submit leave request (`startDate`+`endDate` required → 400). Type validated (default `annual`). Managers may submit on behalf of another user via `userId`; others self-only. Status `pending`. |
| PATCH | `/api/hrm/leave/:id/review` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager","owner")` | Approve/reject a leave request (`status` must be `approved`/`rejected` → else 400). Stamps `reviewedBy`, `reviewedAt`, optional `reviewNotes`. |

**Note on `team` permissions:** since the matrix grants `team:create/edit` to `tenant_owner` only, the profile/attendance/leave-create endpoints are effectively tenant_owner-only at the permission layer (managers get `team:view`); managers regain review power via the explicit `requireRole` on `/leave/:id/review`. The `isManager` in-handler checks additionally rely on runtime role for own-vs-others scoping.

---

## Payroll — `/api/payroll`
File: `backend/src/routes/payroll.js`. Models: `SalaryStructure` (1:1 with StaffProfile), `PayrollRun`, `PayslipEntry`. Uses **`team`** permission for reads; writes use `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/payroll/salary-structures` | `team:view` | List all salary structures with staff profile + user (id/name/email). |
| GET | `/api/payroll/salary-structures/:staffProfileId` | `team:view` | Single structure (or null). |
| PUT | `/api/payroll/salary-structures/:staffProfileId` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")` | Upsert structure. 404 if staff profile not in tenant. |
| GET | `/api/payroll/runs` | `team:view` | List payroll runs (year desc, month desc) with payslip counts. |
| POST | `/api/payroll/runs` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")` | Generate a run for `{month, year}` (both required → 400). 409 if run already exists for that month/year. Builds payslips from active staff + salary structures. |
| GET | `/api/payroll/runs/:id` | `team:view` | Single run with all payslips (+ staff user name/email), sorted by staff name. 404 if not found. |
| PATCH | `/api/payroll/runs/:id` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")` | Update run `status`; if `approved`, stamps `processedBy` + `processedAt`. |
| PATCH | `/api/payroll/payslips/:id/pay` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager")` | Mark a payslip `paid` (stamps `paidAt`, `paymentMethod`, `paymentReference`), then recomputes and updates run `paidCount`. |

**Salary-structure fields:** `basicSalary`, `houseAllowance`, `transportAllowance`, `medicalAllowance`, `otherAllowance`, `taxDeduction`, `providentFund`, `otherDeduction`, `currency` (default `BDT`), `effectiveFrom`, `notes` (all amounts `parseFloat || 0`).

**Payroll-run generation logic (`POST /runs`):**
- `month` coerced to zero-padded string (matches the `@@unique([tenantId, month, year])` stored format and avoids Prisma type errors).
- Includes only staff whose `user.status === "active"` **and** who have a `salaryStructure`; 400 if none.
- Pulls attendance for `${year}-${MM}` prefix; tallies `present`/`absent`/`leave` per user (note: attendance uses `on_leave` but the tally checks `leave`, so `leaveDays` typically stays 0).
- Per payslip: `grossSalary = basic + all allowances`; `totalDeductions = tax + PF + other`; `netSalary = gross − deductions`. Run `totalAmount` = Σ netSalary. New run created with `status: "draft"` and nested payslip creation; each payslip `status: "pending"`.

**Payslip lifecycle:** `pending` → `paid`. **Run lifecycle:** `draft` → `approved`/`finalized` (via PATCH; `approved` stamps processor).

---

## Tasks — `/api/tasks` (generic CRUD factory)
Mounted as `require("./routes/crud")("task")` (`app.js:131`). File: `backend/src/routes/crud.js`. Model: `Task`; permission module resolved via `PERMISSION_MODULE_MAP.task = "tasks"`. Not a travel-package model, so only the base CRUD verbs apply (no child sub-routes).

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/tasks` | `tasks:view` | List tenant tasks, newest first (`createdAt` desc). |
| GET | `/api/tasks/:id` | `tasks:view` | Single task (tenant-scoped); 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/api/tasks` | `tasks:create` | Create task from raw `req.body` + `tenantId` (no field whitelist in the generic factory). |
| PATCH | `/api/tasks/:id` | `tasks:edit` | Update via `updateMany` (tenant-scoped) with raw `req.body`; 404 if none matched; returns refreshed record. |
| DELETE | `/api/tasks/:id` | `tasks:delete` | Hard delete (tenant-scoped); 404 if none. |

Task permission holders: tenant_owner (full), manager (full), sales_agent (view/create/edit), accountant (view), operations (view/create/edit).

---

### Cross-cutting notes
- **Tenant isolation:** every read/write is filtered by `req.tenantId`; updates/deletes use `updateMany`/`deleteMany` with tenant filter or `findFirst` tenant-scoped guards to prevent cross-tenant access.
- **Feature gating:** only **campaigns** is subscription-feature gated (`hasMarketingTools` → Business/Ultimate). Complaints, support tickets, documents, loyalty, referrals, HRM, payroll, CRM analytics, and CRM settings are permission/role gated only (no plan-feature flag on the router).
- **Currency:** payroll and loyalty examples use BDT (`৳`), consistent with the Bangladesh-market tenant base.
- **Automations tie-in:** `crmSettings.getOrCreate` is consumed by `complaints.js` (`complaintNotifyOwner` → owner notification). `automations.leadWonCreateTask` is defined in CRM config here but consumed elsewhere (leads route), not in these files.


## routes-platform

# Platform / Admin Routes

Backend super-admin (platform owner) and tenant-subscription management routes. All handlers live under `backend/src/routes/`. Mount points are defined in `backend/src/app.js` (lines 118-185). A global `subscriptionAccessGate` runs on every `/api` request (`app.js:115`).

## RBAC primitives (`backend/src/middleware/auth.js`)

| Middleware | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| `requireSuperAdmin` | Rejects with `403 Forbidden` unless `req.userRole === "super_admin"`. Used to lock a route to the platform owner. (`auth.js:45`) |
| `requireRole(...roles)` | `super_admin` always passes; otherwise `req.userRole` must be in the list, else `403`. (`auth.js:52`) |
| `requirePermission(module, action)` | Role→module→action matrix (mirrors frontend `permissions.ts`). (`auth.js:149`) |
| `requireFeature(flag)` | Plan feature-gate; blocks if tenant's plan lacks the flag. (`auth.js:207`) |

**Super-admin only** = the router calls `router.use(requireSuperAdmin)` (or the individual route does), so only the platform owner can call it. These are marked **SA-only** below.

---

## 1. Platform Admin Core — `admin.js`
**Mount:** `/api/admin` (`app.js:167`). Router-level `router.use(authenticate); router.use(requireSuperAdmin)` — **every endpoint is SA-only.** Actor is resolved from `req.userId`; most mutations write an `auditLog` row.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/admin/tenants` | SA-only | Manually create a tenant + owner user. Generates unique slug, validates password policy (`validatePassword`), checks email uniqueness, sets `subscriptionPlan`/`status`/expiry (months), normalizes `enabledSubcategories`/`enabledServiceTypes`, creates `tenant_owner` user (active, approvedBy=actor), links `ownerId`. Audit: `tenant_created`. |
| GET | `/api/admin/tenants/health` | SA-only | Tenant health dashboard: per-tenant health score (active sub +40, recent login +30, bookings-30d +20, has-clients +10) → label `healthy`/`moderate`/`at_risk`. Excludes the platform tenant (`isPlatformTenant`). Aggregates last-30d bookings via `groupBy`. |
| GET | `/api/admin/tenants` | SA-only | List all tenants (desc), with `_count` of users/bookings and user summaries. Query `?excludePlatform=true|1` filters out the platform tenant. |
| GET | `/api/admin/tenants/:id` | SA-only | Single tenant with users + `_count` (users/bookings/clients/invoices). 404 if missing. |
| PATCH | `/api/admin/tenants/:id` | SA-only | Update tenant. Whitelist only (`ADMIN_ALLOWED_TENANT_FIELDS`): name, subscriptionPlan/Status/Expiry, phone, whatsapp, address, city, country, website, notes, enabledServiceTypes, enabledSubcategories, enableHajjUmrahModule, enableBdOperationsModule. Rejects modifying the platform tenant. Audit: `tenant_updated`. |
| PATCH | `/api/admin/tenants/:id/owner` | SA-only | Update tenant owner account (name/email/password/phone/whatsapp). Resolves owner via `ownerId` then falls back to first `tenant_owner`/`owner`. Email-uniqueness check; password ≥6 chars, bcrypt-hashed. Audit: `owner_password_reset` or `owner_updated`. |
| DELETE | `/api/admin/tenants/:id` | SA-only | Delete tenant with best-effort cascade (deletes payment, invoice, quotation, booking, lead, client, vendor, expense, task, agent, account, tenantDomain, smsLog, notification, subscription, paymentRequest, auditLog rows, then users, then tenant). Rejects deleting the platform tenant. Audit: `tenant_deleted`. |
| GET | `/api/admin/stats` | SA-only | Platform dashboard totals: totalTenants, totalUsers, totalBookings, totalRevenue (sum of all payments). |
| GET | `/api/admin/payment-requests` | SA-only | List all subscription payment requests (desc) with tenant name. |
| PATCH | `/api/admin/payment-requests/:id` | SA-only | Simple approve/reject of a payment request. Sets status/reviewerComment/processedAt; on `approved` activates tenant (plan + `active` + expiry by plan tier: enterprise/business=12mo, pro=6mo, else 1mo). Audit: `payment_approved`/`payment_rejected`. *(Note: `adminSubscriptionWorkflow.js` provides a richer version of this endpoint — see §2.)* |
| GET | `/api/admin/pending-users` | SA-only | Signup approval queue: users with `status:"pending"`, passwords/reset tokens stripped. |
| GET | `/api/admin/users/status/:status` | SA-only | List users by arbitrary `status` (e.g. active/rejected), tokens stripped. |
| POST | `/api/admin/users/:id/approve` | SA-only | Approve a pending signup: sets user active + approvedBy, sets tenant to `trial` with trial expiry (`addTrialExpiry`), clears rejectionReason, fires Telegram `notifyUserApproved`. Audit: `user_approved`. |
| POST | `/api/admin/users/:id/reject` | SA-only | Reject signup with optional `reason`. Audit: `user_rejected`. |
| GET | `/api/admin/trial-expiry-alerts` | SA-only | Expired trials/subscriptions needing follow-up (up to 200 `expired` tenants, non-platform). Derives per-tenant renewal-notify history (auto/manual email/SMS/WhatsApp) from audit logs; returns owner contact + notify flags. |
| POST | `/api/admin/tenants/:id/trial-expiry-notify` | SA-only | Manually send renewal notice to one agency owner over `channels` (default sms/whatsapp/email) via `sendManualRenewalNotify`. Returns per-channel results + warnings. |
| POST | `/api/admin/trial-expiry-notify-bulk` | SA-only | Bulk renewal reminders. `tenantIds` (deduped, 1-100), `channels` (default email/sms/whatsapp). Returns sent/failed/total. |
| GET | `/api/admin/gateway-status` | SA-only | Detailed payment-gateway configuration status (`getGatewayStatusDetailed`). |

---

## 2. Subscription Approval Workflow — `adminSubscriptionWorkflow.js`
**Mounted twice:** `/api/admin` (`app.js:165`) **and** `/api/admin/subscription-workflow` (`app.js:174`) — identical endpoints reachable under both prefixes. Router-level `authenticate` + `requireSuperAdmin` — **all SA-only.** Every mutation writes `auditLog`, `subscriptionHistory`, a `subscription` record, and (for tenant-facing changes) sends a WhatsApp notice to the agency owner (`notifySubscriptionChangeWhatsApp` / `notifySubscriptionPaymentApproved`).

| METHOD | Path (relative to both prefixes) | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/payment-methods` | SA-only | Manual payment methods, merged with `DEFAULT_PAYMENT_METHODS`; seeds defaults if the table is empty. |
| PUT | `/payment-methods` | SA-only | Upsert manual payment-method configs (`methods[]`: methodCode, label, enabled, accountName/Number, bankName, branchName, instructions, sortOrder). Returns merged list. |
| GET | `/payment-requests` | SA-only | Filterable list. Query: `status`, `tenantId`, `plan`, `method`, `q` (searches trxId/transactionId/sender/tenant name). Includes tenant subscription summary. |
| GET | `/payment-requests/:id` | SA-only | Single request + last 10 `subscriptionHistory` rows. |
| PATCH | `/payment-requests/:id` | SA-only | **Full approval engine.** `action`/`status` = approve/approved → computes plan, billingCycle (monthly/yearly), requestType (activate/renew/upgrade/downgrade via `resolveType`) and activation timing (`subscriptionOutcome`: immediate vs scheduled `activate_now`/`extend_from_current_expiry`/`after_current_expiry`); updates request + tenant (if immediate), records history/subscription, cancels other pending requests, notifies, increments coupon usage if `couponCode`. `reject/rejected/request_more_info/needs_info/duplicate/cancelled/cancel` → sets normalized status + rejectionReason, WhatsApp notice. |
| GET | `/tenants/:id/subscription-history` | SA-only | Full subscription history for a tenant (desc). |
| POST | `/tenants/:id/subscription/manual-activate` | SA-only | Manually activate/set a plan (plan, billingCycle, activationDate, expiryDate, subscriptionStatus, actionType, note). Records history + audit + WhatsApp. |
| PATCH | `/tenants/:id/subscription/skip-trial` | SA-only | For trial tenants only. With `plan`/`targetPlan` → skip trial and activate paid plan (`trial_skipped`). Without a plan → expire immediately. |
| PATCH | `/tenants/:id/subscription/extend` | SA-only | Extend subscription by `months` (>0); base is current expiry if future, else now. actionType `extended`. |
| PATCH | `/tenants/:id/subscription/suspend` | SA-only | Set subscription status to `suspended`; actionType `suspended`. |

**Action labels** emitted to WhatsApp: activated, renewed, upgraded, upgrade_scheduled, extended, suspended, cancelled, trial_skipped, trial_ended_paid.

---

## 3. Platform Staff — `adminPlatformStaff.js`
**Mount:** `/api/admin/platform-staff` (`app.js:166`). Internal platform-team accounts stored in a separate `PlatformStaff` table (not tenant `User`). The **`POST /login` is public** (declared before `router.use(authenticate); router.use(requireSuperAdmin)`); every other route is **SA-only.**

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/admin/platform-staff/login` | **Public** | Platform-staff login. Validates email/password against `PlatformStaff`, requires `status:"active"`, bcrypt-compares, issues a 12h JWT `{ staffId, role:"platform_staff", permissions }`. Returns token + safe staff record. |
| GET | `/api/admin/platform-staff/meta/permissions` | SA-only | Permission catalogue (`PERMISSION_OPTIONS`): agencies, payments, subscriptions, reports, audit_log, whatsapp, sms, domains, master_data. |
| GET | `/api/admin/platform-staff` | SA-only | List all platform staff (no passwords). |
| POST | `/api/admin/platform-staff` | SA-only | Create staff (name, email, phone, password ≥8, permissions[]). Email-uniqueness check; bcrypt hash; status active. |
| PATCH | `/api/admin/platform-staff/:id` | SA-only | Update staff (name, phone, status, permissions, password ≥8). |
| DELETE | `/api/admin/platform-staff/:id` | SA-only | Delete a platform-staff account. |

| GET | `/api/admin/master-data/categories` | SA-only | Valid category list. |
| POST | `/api/admin/master-data/` | SA-only | Create reference (category+name required; code uppercased, nameBn, parentId, meta, sortOrder, isActive). |
| PATCH | `/api/admin/master-data/:id` | SA-only | Update reference (partial). |

## 7. Audit Logs — `auditLogs.js`
**Mount:** `/api/audit-logs` (`app.js:162`). Router-level `authenticate`. Not SA-only, but SA sees platform-wide logs.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/audit-logs/` | `requireAuditViewer` = super_admin, tenant_owner, owner, manager | Activity log, newest first, capped at 500. **super_admin** sees all logs; other roles are scoped to their own `tenantId`. |
| POST | `/api/audit-logs/` | Write-roles: super_admin, tenant_owner, manager | Manually create a tenant-scoped audit entry (module, action, targetType/Id/Label, old/newValue, metadata). Captures actor + IP (`x-forwarded-for`). Server-side actions are the primary source; this is for privileged manual events. |

---

## 8. Custom Domains — Platform `domains.js` & Tenant `tenantDomains.js`
Both operate on the `TenantDomain` model. Domain plan limits: free 0, basic 0, pro 1, business 2, enterprise/unlimited -1 (unlimited). Active statuses required: `active`/`trial`.

### 8a. `domains.js` — **Mounted twice:** `/api/admin/domains` (`app.js:170`) and legacy `/api/domains` (`app.js:172`, same router). `authenticate` + `requireSuperAdmin` — **all SA-only.** Every mutation writes a `domains`-module audit log.

| METHOD | Path (both prefixes) | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | SA-only | List all tenant domains (desc) with tenant summary. |
| POST | `/` | SA-only | Register a domain for a tenant (`tenantId`, `domain`, `wwwRedirect`). Normalizes/validates domain, enforces uniqueness (409), active-subscription + plan domain-limit checks, generates verification token, first domain becomes primary. Audit: `create`. |
| POST | `/:id/verify` | SA-only | Verify DNS TXT at `_verify.<domain>` against the token via `dns.google`. Sets `verified`/`unverified` + `lastDnsCheck`. Audit: `verify`/`verify_failed`. |
| PATCH | `/:id/ssl` | SA-only | Set `sslStatus` (active/pending/none). Audit: `update`. |
| PATCH | `/:id/status` | SA-only | Set operational `status` (active/pending/error). Activation requires verified + plan-eligible + active subscription. Audit: `activate`/`deactivate`. |
| PATCH | `/:id/primary` | SA-only | Make domain primary (clears others for the tenant). Audit: `set_primary`. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | SA-only | Remove a domain. Audit: `delete`. |

### 8b. `tenantDomains.js` — **Mount:** `/api/tenant-domains` (`app.js:120`). `authenticate` + `requireRole("tenant_owner")` (SA passes too). Tenant-owner self-service, scoped to `req.tenantId`. Audits under `domains` module with `tenant_*` actions.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/tenant-domains/summary` | tenant_owner (SA) | Domain quota summary: plan, status, domainLimit, usedDomains, canAddDomain, defaultWebsiteUrl. |
| GET | `/api/tenant-domains/` | tenant_owner (SA) | List this tenant's domains (primary first). |
| POST | `/api/tenant-domains/` | tenant_owner (SA) | Add a custom domain (self-service): active-subscription + plan-limit enforcement, uniqueness (409 with cross-tenant detail), token generated, first domain primary. Audit: `tenant_create`. |
| POST | `/api/tenant-domains/:id/verify` | tenant_owner (SA) | DNS TXT verification (scoped to own tenant). Audit: `tenant_verify`/`tenant_verify_failed`. |
| PATCH | `/api/tenant-domains/:id/primary` | tenant_owner (SA) | Set a verified domain as primary. Audit: `tenant_set_primary`. |
| DELETE | `/api/tenant-domains/:id` | tenant_owner (SA) | Delete own domain; promotes oldest remaining to primary if the deleted one was primary. Audit: `tenant_delete`. |

---

## 9. Blog / Website Posts — `blogs.js`
**Mount:** `/api/blogs` (`app.js:134`). `authenticate` + `requireFeature("hasWebsiteTemplates")` (plan-gated, not SA). Per-route `requirePermission("website", ...)`. Tenant-scoped to `req.tenantId` on the `WebsitePost` model. **Not a platform-admin route** — it is tenant-facing content management (included here because it manages the tenant's public website blog).

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/blogs/` | `website:view` + feature `hasWebsiteTemplates` | List tenant's posts (optional `?status=`), published-first. |
| GET | `/api/blogs/:id` | `website:view` | Single post (tenant-scoped, 404 if missing). |
| POST | `/api/blogs/` | `website:create` | Create post (title required; unique slug per tenant; draft/published; publishedAt set on publish; excerpt, body, coverImage, authorName). |
| PATCH | `/api/blogs/:id` | `website:edit` | Update post (partial; slug re-uniqued; publish/unpublish toggles publishedAt). |
| DELETE | `/api/blogs/:id` | `website:delete` | Delete post (tenant-scoped). |

---

## 10. Subscriptions CRUD — `/api/subscriptions` = `crud.js("subscription")`
**Mount:** `/api/subscriptions` (`app.js:160`) via the generic CRUD factory `crud.js` with modelKey `subscription`. `authenticate` at router level; per-route `requirePermission("subscription", action)` (permission module mapped from `subscription`). **Tenant-scoped** to `req.tenantId` on the `Subscription` model — this is *not* the platform approval workflow (that is §2); it is generic per-tenant CRUD over subscription records.

| METHOD | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/subscriptions/` | `subscription:view` | List tenant's subscription records (desc by createdAt). |
| GET | `/api/subscriptions/:id` | `subscription:view` | Single record (tenant-scoped, 404 if missing). |
| POST | `/api/subscriptions/` | `subscription:create` | Create record (body + `tenantId` injected). |
| PATCH | `/api/subscriptions/:id` | `subscription:edit` | Update record (tenant-scoped `updateMany`). |
| DELETE | `/api/subscriptions/:id` | `subscription:delete` | Delete record (tenant-scoped). |

Per the `ROLE_PERMISSIONS` matrix (`auth.js`): `tenant_owner` has full subscription view/create/edit/delete; `manager` has `subscription:view` only; `sales_agent`/`accountant` have no `subscription` module access. `super_admin` bypasses `requirePermission` implicitly.

---

## Cross-cutting notes
- **Global subscription gate:** `subscriptionAccessGate` is applied to all `/api/*` requests (`app.js:115`) before any route — it can block tenants whose subscription is inactive/expired regardless of the per-route RBAC above.
- **Duplicate mounts to be aware of:** `adminSubscriptionWorkflow` is reachable under both `/api/admin/*` and `/api/admin/subscription-workflow/*`; `domains.js` under both `/api/admin/domains/*` and legacy `/api/domains/*`. `admin.js` and `adminSubscriptionWorkflow.js` both define `GET /api/admin/payment-requests` and `PATCH /api/admin/payment-requests/:id` — because `adminSubscriptionWorkflow` is mounted first (`app.js:165` before `:167`), **its richer versions win** at `/api/admin/payment-requests`.
- **Platform tenant protection:** `isPlatformTenant()` guards prevent the platform/admin tenant from being edited or deleted through the Agencies endpoints (`admin.js`).
- **Platform-staff JWTs** (`role: "platform_staff"`) are issued by `adminPlatformStaff.js` login but are **not** `super_admin`; they will fail `requireSuperAdmin` on the very routes in this file unless the platform separately elevates them — the permission catalogue (`meta/permissions`) is the intended enforcement surface for that role elsewhere.


## routes-integrations

# Integrations, Comms, Portal, CMS & Dashboard Routes

All route files below are under `backend/src/routes/`. Mount points (from `backend/src/app.js`):

| Router file | Mount base | Global auth |
|---|---|---|
| `email.js` | `/api/email` | `authenticate` (all routes) |
| `sms.js` | `/api/sms` | `authenticate` + `requireFeature("hasSmsIntegration")` |
| `whatsapp.js` | `/api/whatsapp` | `authenticate` + `requireFeature("hasWhatsApp")` |
| `bkash.js` | `/api/payments/bkash` (mounted inside `payments.js`) | per-route |
| `sslcommerz.js` | `/api/payments/sslcommerz` (mounted inside `payments.js`) | per-route |
| `notifications.js` | `/api/notifications` | `authenticate` (all routes) |
| `notificationAutomation.js` | `/api/notifications/automation` (sub-mounted) | inherits `authenticate` |
| `cron.js` | `/api/cron` | `verifyCronSecret` (NOT JWT) |
| `contact.js` | `/api/contact` | Public (no auth) |
| `demo.js` | `/api/demo-requests` | Public (no auth) |
| `public.js` | `/api/public` | Public (no auth) |
| `portal.js` | `/api/portal` | Separate `portal` JWT audience |
| `website.js` | `/api/website` | `authenticate` + `requireFeature("hasWebsiteTemplates")` |
| `landingCms.js` | `/api/landing-cms` | Mixed (public GET, super-admin write) |
| `dashboard.js` | `/api/dashboard` | `authenticate` (all routes) |
| `dataExport.js` | `/api/export` | `authenticate` (all routes) |

**RBAC middleware semantics** (from `middleware/auth.js`):
- `requireSuperAdmin` — only `super_admin` role; all others get 403.
- `requireRole(...roles)` — `super_admin` always passes; otherwise role must be in the list.
- `requirePermission(module, action)` — `super_admin` bypasses; otherwise checks `ROLE_PERMISSIONS[role][module]` includes the action.
- `requireFeature(flag)` — `super_admin` bypasses; otherwise tenant's `subscriptionPlan` must include the plan feature flag (else 403 `FEATURE_NOT_IN_PLAN`).

---

## Email — `/api/email` (all routes: `authenticate`)

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/config` | authenticated | Returns current SMTP config derived from env (`SMTP_HOST/PORT/SECURE/USER/FROM_NAME/FROM`). Password never returned. |
| POST | `/test` | authenticated | Tests SMTP connection via `testSmtpConnection()`. Returns `{success:false}` if `SMTP_HOST` unset. Body: `{ to }`. |
| POST | `/send/booking-confirmation` | authenticated | Sends booking confirmation email. Body `{ bookingId }`; loads tenant-scoped booking + client; 404 if not found, 400 if client has no email. |
| POST | `/send/invoice` | authenticated | Sends invoice email. Body `{ invoiceId }`; tenant-scoped; falls back to `invoice.clientName` if no client email; 400 if no valid `@` email. |

- Backed by `services/emailService` (`sendBookingConfirmation`, `sendInvoiceEmail`, `testSmtpConnection`). SMTP is environment-managed (no DB template storage in this router).

---

## SMS — `/api/sms` (all routes: `authenticate` + `requireFeature("hasSmsIntegration")`)

Provider mapping: env `SMS_PROVIDER` (`console` default) → normalized (`bulksmsbd`→`bulksms`, `twilio`→`sslwireless`). Send flow: `dispatchSms()` writes an `smsLog` (status `pending`), calls `sendSms()`, then updates the log to `sent`/`failed`. If `SMS_ENABLED === "false"`, marks log `failed` with "SMS is disabled".

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/config` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Public (non-secret) SMS config: provider, senderId, baseUrl, enabled, apiKeyConfigured. |
| GET | `/balance` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Provider SMS balance via `getSmsBalance()`; 502 on failure. |
| PUT | `/config` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Config is env-managed; rejects `apiKey`/`apiSecret` with 400; only returns advisory message. |
| POST | `/send` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","manager","super_admin")` | Send one SMS. Body `{ phone, message, templateId? }`. Optional template lookup (tenant-null or tenant-scoped, active). 502 on send failure. |
| POST | `/send-bulk` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Send to array of phones. Body `{ phones[], message, templateId? }`. Returns `{ total, sent, failed, results }`. |
| POST | `/test` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Sends fixed test SMS to `{ phone }`. |
| GET | `/logs/stats` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Counts: total / sent / failed / pending (global, all tenants). |
| GET | `/logs` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Paginated SMS logs (page/limit ≤100). Filters: `status`, `phone` (contains), `from`/`to` date. Message returned as truncated `messagePreview`. |
| GET | `/logs/:id` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Single SMS log by id (full message). |
| GET | `/templates` | authenticated | Global (tenant-null) active SMS templates. |
| GET | `/templates/:id` | authenticated | One template (tenant-null OR own tenant). |
| POST | `/templates` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Create global template `{ name, type, message, variables?, isActive? }`. Auto-extracts `{{var}}` variables if not provided. |
| PATCH | `/templates/:id` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Update global template (name/type/message/isActive/variables). Re-extracts variables if message changes. |
| DELETE | `/templates/:id` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Delete global template. |

---

## WhatsApp — `/api/whatsapp` (all routes: `authenticate` + `requireFeature("hasWhatsApp")`)

Send backed by `services/whatsappService` (`sendWhatsApp`, `getWhatsAppConfig`). Templates use `whatsappTemplateService.extractVariables`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/config` | authenticated (feature-gated) | Provider config (no secrets) plus this-month WhatsApp quota: `{ limit, used, unlimited }`. `used` = count of `sent` `whatsAppLog` rows since UTC month start; limit via `getPlanLimit(plan,"whatsapp")` (-1 = unlimited). Falls back to bare config on error. |
| POST | `/test` | authenticated (feature-gated) | Send test WhatsApp message. Body `{ to, message? }`; 400 if no `to`. |
| GET | `/templates` | authenticated | Global (tenant-null) WhatsApp templates. |
| GET | `/templates/:id` | authenticated | One global template. |
| POST | `/templates` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Create global template `{ name, type, message, metaTemplateName?, variables?, isActive? }`. Auto-extracts variables. |
| PATCH | `/templates/:id` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Update global template (name/type/message/metaTemplateName/isActive/variables). |
| DELETE | `/templates/:id` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Delete global template. |
| GET | `/logs` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Paginated `whatsAppLog` list (page/limit ≤100). Filters: `status` (or `all`), `phone` (contains), `tenantId`. Returns global stats block `{ total, sent, failed, pending }`. |
| POST | `/send` | `requireSuperAdmin` | Send WhatsApp message. Body `{ to, message }`; logs via service. |

---

## bKash Gateway — `/api/payments/bkash`

Tokenized checkout (v1.2.0-beta). Env: `BKASH_APP_KEY/APP_SECRET/USERNAME/PASSWORD`, `BKASH_SANDBOX`, `API_BASE_URL`. In-memory token cache (`getToken()`, 1h TTL). On success delegates to `paymentGateway.handlePaymentSuccess` + `auditPaymentEvent`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/create` | `authenticate` | Create bKash payment. Body `{ invoiceId, paymentRequestId, amount, customerPhone }`. 503 if not configured. Calls bKash `/create` with `callbackURL=<API_BASE>/payments/bkash/callback`; writes `auditLog` (`module=payment_gateway, action=bkash_created`) storing invoiceId/paymentRequestId/tenantId/amount; returns `{ transactionId, redirectUrl, paymentID }`. |
| GET | `/callback` | Public (bKash redirect) | bKash redirects here with `paymentID` + `status`. On `success`: executes payment (`/execute`); if `Completed`, recovers context from the `bkash_created` audit log, records payment via `handlePaymentSuccess` (method `online`, gateway `bkash`), audits `payment_success`, then 302-redirects to `${FRONTEND_URL}/payment/callback?status=success&tran_id=…&gateway=bkash`. Handles failed/cancel/unknown with corresponding redirect + audit (`payment_execution_failed`, `payment_<status>`). |

---

## SSLCommerz Gateway — `/api/payments/sslcommerz`

Env: `SSLCOMMERZ_STORE_ID/STORE_PASSWORD`, `SSLCOMMERZ_SANDBOX`, `API_BASE_URL`. Callback URLs via `getCallbackUrls("sslcommerz")`. Custom fields `value_a..d` carry invoiceId / paymentRequestId / tenantId / amount.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/initiate` | `authenticate` | Create SSLCommerz session (`/gwprocess/v4`). Body `{ invoiceId, paymentRequestId, amount, customerName, customerEmail, customerPhone }`. 503 if not configured. Audits `payment_initiated`; returns `{ transactionId, redirectUrl(GatewayPageURL), sessionKey }`. |
| POST | `/success` | Public (gateway form-post) | Success callback. Server-side validation via `validationserverAPI.php` (`val_id`); if invalid → audit `payment_validation_failed` + redirect failed. On valid → `handlePaymentSuccess` (gateway `sslcommerz`), audit `payment_success`, redirect `…status=success`. |
| POST | `/fail` | Public | Audit `payment_failed`; redirect `…status=failed`. |
| POST | `/cancel` | Public | Audit `payment_cancelled`; redirect `…status=cancelled`. |
| POST | `/ipn` | Public (server-to-server) | IPN validation. If `status` VALID/VALIDATED → `handlePaymentSuccess` (metadata `source:ipn`) + audit `ipn_validated`. Returns JSON `{status:"ok"}`. |

**Parent unified router — `/api/payments`** (`payments.js`, relevant to gateways):

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/gateway-status` | `authenticate` | Which online gateways are configured (`getGatewayStatus()`). |
| GET | `/` | `authenticate` | List tenant payments (with invoice select). |
| POST | `/initiate` | `authenticate` | Universal initiation. `gateway=cod` → instant confirm + audit `payment_cod_confirmed`; `sslcommerz`/`bkash` → internally proxies to the gateway sub-route. |
| GET | `/status/:transactionId` | `authenticate` | Resolves gateway status from `payment_gateway` audit logs (tenant-scoped). |
| POST | `/callback/:gateway` | Public | Frontend-facing status resolver from audit logs by `tran_id`/`paymentID`. |

---

## Notifications — `/api/notifications` (all routes: `authenticate`)

Visibility scope: `tenantId = req.tenantId` AND (`userId = null` OR `userId = req.userId`) — i.e. tenant-broadcast + user-targeted notifications.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | authenticated | Paginated notifications (page, limit ≤100 default 50). Returns `{ items, total, page, limit, unreadCount }`. |
| GET | `/unread-count` | authenticated | Count of unread visible notifications. |
| PATCH | `/read-all` | authenticated | Mark all visible unread as read. |
| PATCH | `/:id/read` | authenticated | Mark one notification read (scoped); 404 if not owned/visible. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | authenticated | Delete one notification (scoped); 404 if not found. |

---

## Notification Automation — `/api/notifications/automation` (sub-mounted; inherits `authenticate`)

Governs per-tenant automation for 4 events (from `tenantAutomationService.AUTOMATION_EVENTS`): `lead_created`, `booking_created`, `payment_received`, `quotation_sent`. Each event has `sms` and `inApp` toggles (default `true`). Settings map to `smsSettings` flags: `smsEnabled`, `notifyOnBooking`, `notifyOnPayment`, `notifyOnLead`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | authenticated | Returns `{ settings, events[] }` with labels (Lead Created / Booking Created / Payment Received / Quotation Sent). Ensures tenant settings exist. |
| PATCH | `/` | `requireRole("tenant_owner")` | Update `settings` (whitelist: smsEnabled/notifyOnBooking/notifyOnPayment/notifyOnLead) and/or `events[]` (per-event `sms`/`inApp`). Rejects unknown `eventType` with 400. Returns refreshed state. |
| GET | `/logs/stats` | authenticated | Tenant delivery counts: total / sent / failed / pending / bySms / byInApp (from `notificationDelivery`). |
| GET | `/logs` | authenticated | Paginated delivery log (limit ≤100 default 20). Filters: `status`, `channel`, `eventType`, `search` (recipient/recipientName/message contains). |
| GET | `/logs/:id` | authenticated | Single tenant-scoped delivery record. |

---

## Cron — `/api/cron` (protected by `verifyCronSecret`, NOT JWT)

Guard: header `x-cron-secret` or query `secret` must equal env `CRON_SECRET` (must be ≥8 chars, else 500). No internal node-cron scheduler exists — these are triggered by an external scheduler.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/process-expiry` | cron secret | Master daily job. (1) Sends expiry reminders for tenants expiring within 3 days (dedup via daily audit log `subscription_expiring_reminder_sent`, `notifyEvent("subscription_expiring")`). (2) Expires overdue tenants via `expireTenantAndNotify`. (3) Runs `processTrialDrips`, `processPassportExpiryAlerts`, `processTravelDepartureReminders`. (4) Activates `scheduled` subscriptions whose `startDate ≤ now` (updates tenant plan/status/expiry, writes `subscriptionHistory` + audit `scheduled_subscription_activated`, `notifyEvent("subscription_activated")`). Returns counts (`processed`, `remindersSent`, `scheduledActivated`, plus drip/passport/travel sub-results). |
| POST | `/nightly-report` | cron secret | End-of-day WhatsApp summary (11 PM BD / UTC+6). For each active/trial tenant with WhatsApp contact AND `hasWhatsApp` plan feature: aggregates today's bookings (vs yesterday trend arrow), new/total clients, payments in, outstanding due, open inquiries, overdue follow-ups, unpaid invoices, departures in next 3 days; adds subscription-expiry warning (≤7 days / expired) and an "Action Needed" alert block; sends formatted WhatsApp message via `sendWhatsApp`. Returns `{ sent, total, results, date }`. |

---

## Contact — `/api/contact` (Public)

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/` | Public | Creates a `contactSubmission` `{ name, email, phone?, subject?, message, tenantSlug? }`. Requires name/email/message (400 otherwise). Fires non-blocking `sendContactConfirmation` (to submitter) + `sendContactNotification` (to admin). Returns `{ success, id }`. |

## Demo Requests — `/api/demo-requests` (Public)

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/` | Public | Creates a `demoRequest` `{ name, email, phone?, company?, teamSize?, message? }`. Requires name/email. Fires non-blocking `sendDemoRequestConfirmation` + `sendDemoRequestNotification`. Returns `{ success, id }`. |

## Public Marketing API — `/api/public` (Public)

Serves tenant marketing sites (by slug or custom domain), packages, blog posts, website config, SEO files, and public booking capture. Custom-domain access requires plan in `{pro, business, enterprise, unlimited}`, active/trial status, and a `tenantDomain` with `status=active` + `verificationStatus=verified`. Website config lives inside `Tenant.notes` JSON (`websiteConfig`), normalized to a CMS `sections` shape.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/:slug` | Public | Tenant public profile by slug (public-safe fields: name, slug, logo, description, phone, address, website, socialLinks). |
| GET | `/:slug/packages` | Public | Merged published packages by slug: `travelPackage` (published) + `hajjPackage` (non-archived). `?featured=true` filters featured travel packages. |
| GET | `/:slug/blog-posts` | Public | Published `websitePost` list (id, slug, title, excerpt, coverImage, publishedAt, authorName). |
| GET | `/:slug/blog-posts/:postSlug` | Public | Single published blog post. |
| GET | `/:slug/website` | Public | Saved website config by slug; 404 if none. |
| GET | `/domain/:domain` | Public | Tenant profile by verified custom domain (domain normalized). |
| GET | `/domain/:domain/packages` | Public | Packages by custom domain (`?featured=true` supported). |
| GET | `/domain/:domain/website` | Public | Website config by custom domain. |
| GET | `/sitemap/:slug` | Public | Generates `sitemap.xml` (home/packages/about/contact + per-package + per-post URLs) at `https://<slug>.travelagencyweb.com`. |
| GET | `/robots/:slug` | Public | Generates `robots.txt` (allow-all + sitemap ref). |
| POST | `/book` | Public | Public booking/inquiry capture. Resolves tenant by `slug` or `domain`; requires name+phone; rejects non-active/trial tenants (403). Creates a `lead` (source `website`, tag `web-inquiry`) + `leadActivity` note; fire-and-forget owner notification via WhatsApp + email. Returns `{ success, leadId }`. |

---

## Customer / Agent / Supplier Portal — `/api/portal`

Separate JWT audience `"portal"` (cannot be used against agency endpoints). Identity is email-based; a user may hold roles `customer` (matches `Client.email`), `supplier` (matches `Vendor.email`), `agent` (matches `Agent.email`, case-insensitive). Magic-link flow uses audience `"portal-magic"` (15-min) exchanged for a 7-day session. Auth endpoints rate-limited by `portalAuthLimiter`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/auth/request-link` | Public (rate-limited) | Emails a magic sign-in link if the email matches any role. Always returns `{ok:true}` (anti-enumeration). |
| POST | `/auth/verify` | Public (rate-limited) | Exchanges magic token for a 7-day portal session JWT; re-classifies roles; 403 if no access, 401 if invalid/expired. Returns `{ token, email, roles }`. |
| GET | `/auth/me` | `portalAuthenticate` | Returns current portal identity `{ email, roles }`. |
| GET | `/bookings` | portal (customer) | Customer's bookings (by matching client email), with tenantName; `[]` if not customer. |
| GET | `/bookings/:id` | portal (customer) | Booking detail with travelers, invoices+installments, timeline (last 20); sanitized via `sanitizePortalBookingDetail`; 403 if not customer, 404 if not owned. |
| GET | `/agent/bookings` | portal (agent) | B2B sub-agent's bookings with commission summary fields; `[]` if not agent. |
| GET | `/agent/commissions` | portal (agent) | Commission wallet summary `{ pendingTotal, paidTotal, bookingCount, items }`; empty summary if not agent. |
| GET | `/purchase-orders` | portal (supplier) | Supplier's `vendorBill` records (mapped as purchase orders) with tenantName; `[]` if not supplier. |

---

## Website Builder / CMS — `/api/website` (`authenticate` + `requireFeature("hasWebsiteTemplates")`, Pro+)

Config persisted inside `Tenant.notes` JSON. Section model (`cms.sections[]`) with legacy `content.*` two-way sync (`applyCmsToLegacy` / `syncLegacyToSections`). Section types: hero, about, services, featured-packages, why-choose-us, testimonials, faq, team, cta, contact. Asset uploads allow PNG/JPG/JPEG/WEBP (SVG excluded — stored-XSS risk), ≤8 MB, saved to `uploads/website-assets/<tenantId>/`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/upload-asset` | `requirePermission("website","edit")` | Upload base64/dataUrl image. `assetType` ∈ {logo, hero, about, gallery}. Validates MIME + size; returns `{ assetUrl, fileName }`. |
| GET | `/config` | `requirePermission("website","view")` | Returns normalized website config, or `null` if unconfigured (not an error). |
| POST | `/config` | `requirePermission("website","edit")` | Save full config (validates template/colors/content present); normalizes + persists; also syncs `socialLinks` into notes. |
| GET | `/sections` | `requirePermission("website","view")` | Returns `cms.sections[]` (normalized). |
| POST | `/sections` | `requirePermission("website","edit")` | Append a new section (requires `type`); re-indexes orders; returns full sections list (201). |
| PATCH | `/sections/:sectionId` | `requirePermission("website","edit")` | Update a section (merges fields, replaces items if array); 404 if not found; returns updated section. |
| POST | `/sections/reorder` | `requirePermission("website","edit")` | Reorder by `sectionIds[]`; unlisted sections appended; re-indexes; returns full sections list. |
| DELETE | `/sections/:sectionId` | `requirePermission("website","edit")` | Remove a section; re-indexes; returns remaining sections. |

---

## Landing CMS — `/api/landing-cms` (platform marketing site)

File-backed JSON at `backend/data/landing-cms.json`; image uploads to `backend/uploads/landing/`.

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | Public | Returns the landing-page CMS JSON (empty object if none). |
| POST | `/` | `authenticate` + `requireSuperAdmin` | Overwrites the CMS JSON (body must be an object). |
| POST | `/upload` | `authenticate` + `requireSuperAdmin` | Uploads a base64 hero/about image `{ dataUrl, fileName }`; returns `{ url }` (`/uploads/landing/<file>`). |

---

## Dashboard — `/api/dashboard` (all routes: `authenticate`)

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/stats` | `requirePermission("dashboard","view")` | Tenant-scoped aggregation. **Base metrics (all roles):** totalUsers, totalClients, totalBookings, recentBookings (5, with clientName), activeLeads, followUpsDueToday, quotationsSentThisMonth, quotationsAwaitingApproval, confirmedBookings, upcomingDepartures (next 7 days), topDestinations (top 5), bookingsByType, openSupportTickets, passportsExpiringSoon (≤30 days), passportsExpired. **Finance-only add-ons** (roles: super_admin, tenant_owner, manager, accountant): totalRevenue (Σ invoice paidAmount), recentPayments (10), overdueInvoices count + amount, vendorDues, salesThisMonth. |

---

## Data Export — `/api/export` (all routes: `authenticate`)

| Method | Path | RBAC | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/csv` | authenticated | Multi-section CSV (`### SECTION` headers) for Clients, Bookings, Invoices, Payments, Leads. Tenant-scoped; optional `from`/`to` `createdAt` range. Downloads `hearth_export_<label>.csv`. |
| GET | `/workbook` | authenticated | Full ExcelJS workbook (via `exceljs`). Sheet 1 "Summary" dashboard (Sales & operations, Money, Catalog & operations, Contacts & CRM, plus a "Sheets in this file" index). Then one sheet per non-empty module across **33 modules** (see below), every scalar field included (dates ISO-sliced, objects JSON-stringified; `tenantId`/`updatedAt` dropped; money columns formatted; frozen header + autofilter). Enriches `assignedTo`/`receivedBy`→user name, `clientId`→client name. Optional `from`/`to` range. Per-model fetch is strictly tenant-scoped (never falls back to unscoped). Downloads `hearth_full_export_<label>.xlsx`. |
| POST | `/db-backup` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","super_admin")` | Full PostgreSQL `pg_dump` (plain SQL) of the DB parsed from `DATABASE_URL`; streams `hearth_backup_<timestamp>.sql`; deletes temp file after send. 120s timeout. |
| GET | `/backup-info` | `requireRole("tenant_owner","super_admin")` | Reads `/var/backups/hearth-core` for `.dump`/`.sql` files; returns `{ lastBackup, count, dir }`. |

**Workbook `EXPORT_MODULES` (33):** Clients (`client`), Client Family (`clientFamilyMember`), Wallet Ledger (`walletTransaction`), Leads (`lead`), Vendors (`vendor`), Vendor Bills (`vendorBill`), Agents (`agent`), Agent Ledger (`agentTransaction`), Visa Stock (`visaStock`), Quotations (`quotation`), Bookings (`booking`), Invoices (`invoice`), Invoice Installments (`invoiceInstallment`), Payments (`payment`), Expenses (`expense`), Ledger (`transaction`), Accounts (`account`), Tasks (`task`), Complaints (`complaint`), Campaigns (`campaign`), Travel Packages (`travelPackage`), Hajj Packages (`hajjPackage`), Hajj Groups (`hajjGroup`), Hajj Pilgrims (`hajjPilgrim`), Group Tours (`groupTour`), MICE Events (`miceEvent`), Visa Applications (`visaApplication`), Support Tickets (`supportTicket`), Loyalty Accounts (`loyaltyAccount`), Loyalty Ledger (`loyaltyTransaction`), Referrals (`referralCode`), Staff Profiles (`staffProfile`).

---

### Cross-cutting notes
- **Comms logging models:** `smsLog`, `whatsAppLog`, `notificationDelivery`, `notification`; templates in `smsTemplate` / `whatsAppTemplate` (global templates use `tenantId=null`).
- **Payment gateway audit trail:** all gateway lifecycle events recorded in `auditLog` with `module="payment_gateway"` (actions: `payment_initiated`, `bkash_created`, `payment_success`, `payment_failed`, `payment_cancelled`, `payment_cod_confirmed`, `ipn_validated`, `payment_validation_failed`, `payment_execution_failed`).
- **Frontend payment return URL:** all gateways redirect to `${FRONTEND_URL}/payment/callback?status=…&tran_id=…&gateway=…` (default `https://travelagencyweb.com`).
- **Website config storage:** the tenant marketing website is stored inside `Tenant.notes` (JSON), shared by both authed `/api/website` (edit) and public `/api/public` (read) routers, which each carry their own copy of the section-normalization helpers.

Relevant source files (all absolute):
`/var/www/hearth-core-app/backend/src/routes/{email,sms,whatsapp,bkash,sslcommerz,payments,notifications,notificationAutomation,cron,contact,demo,public,portal,website,landingCms,dashboard,dataExport}.js`, `/var/www/hearth-core-app/backend/src/middleware/{auth,portalAuth}.js`, `/var/www/hearth-core-app/backend/src/services/tenantAutomationService.js`, `/var/www/hearth-core-app/backend/src/app.js`.
