# APPENDIX B — SIDEBAR, NAVIGATION & GATING PIPELINE

# Sidebar & Navigation

The sidebar is a static tree defined in `src/config/navigation.ts` (via `getNavigationGroups()`), rendered by `src/components/AppSidebar.tsx`, and filtered per-group by `src/components/AppSidebarNav.tsx`. The tree is intentionally the **full** menu; three orthogonal gates decide what each user actually sees: **RBAC module access**, the **advanced-module gate** (`src/lib/moduleAccess.ts`), and a **plan floor** (`minPlan`).

## Navigation source & rendering

| Concern | File |
|---|---|
| Nav tree definition (`getNavigationGroups`, `navigationGroups`, `packagesDefaultPath`) | `src/config/navigation.ts` |
| Sidebar shell (brand, groups loop, admin panel, footer, logout) | `src/components/AppSidebar.tsx` |
| Per-group filter + item/locked-row rendering | `src/components/AppSidebarNav.tsx` |
| Advanced-module gate (`isNavItemModuleEnabled`, plan ranks) | `src/lib/moduleAccess.ts` |
| RBAC matrix (`canAccessModule`, `hasPermission`) | `src/lib/permissions.ts` |
| RBAC hook (`usePermissions().canAccess`) | `src/hooks/usePermissions.ts` |
| Service types & derivation to module flags | `src/lib/serviceTypes.ts`, `src/lib/enabledServiceTypes.ts` |
| Active-route matching | `src/lib/navActive.ts` |

`AppSidebar.tsx` (lines 31-35) calls `getNavigationGroups({ enabledServiceTypes, enabledSubcategories, showActivityLog })`. `showActivityLog` is `true` only when `appRole` is `tenant_owner`, `owner`, or `manager`. Each group is rendered via `<AppSidebarNavGroup>` with `currentPlan` and `tenant?.enabledModules`. Below the groups, an **Admin Panel** link (`/admin`, red styling) renders only when `canAccessAdmin` (role `super_admin`).

## NavItemConfig shape (`navigation.ts` lines 43-54)

`id`, `titleKey`, `url?`, `icon`, `module` (required — RBAC module), `requiredFeature?`, `minPlan?`, `requiredServiceTypes?`, `children?`.

## Full sidebar tree (grouped by section)

Legend: **module** = RBAC module for `canAccess`; **minPlan** = plan floor for a locked row; **svcTypes** = declared `requiredServiceTypes`; **feat** = `requiredFeature`; **bundle** = advanced-module bundle that gates the item (from `moduleAccess.ts`; blank = core, always-on).

- **1. Overview** — group `overview`, label `sidebar.overview`
  - `dashboard` — `sidebar.dashboard` → `/dashboard` — module `dashboard` — bundle: core
- **2. CRM** — group `crm`, label `sidebar.crm`
  - `crm-hub` — `sidebar.crmWorkspace` → `/crm` — module `clients` — bundle `crm` (default floor Business)
  - `clients` — `sidebar.clients` → `/clients` — module `clients` — core
  - `corporate` — `sidebar.corporateTravel` → `/corporate` — module `clients` — svcTypes `corporate_travel`, `mice_event` — bundle `corporate` (Business)
  - `agents` — `sidebar.agents` → `/agents` — module `agents` — core
  - `vendors` — `sidebar.vendors` → `/vendors` — module `vendors` — core
- **3. Sales & Bookings** — group `salesBookings`, label `sidebar.salesBookings`
  - `quotations` — `sidebar.quotations` → `/quotations` — module `quotations` — core
  - `bookings` — `sidebar.bookings` → `/bookings` — module `bookings` — core
  - `visa-stock` — `sidebar.visaStock` → `/visa-stock` — module `bookings` — core
  - `service-catalog` — `sidebar.serviceCatalogItem` → `/packages/all` — module `packages` — core
  - `ticket-transactions` — `sidebar.ticketTransactions` → `/ticket-transactions` — module `bookings` — svcTypes `air_ticket` — bundle `ticketing` (Business)
  - `flight-reminders` — `sidebar.flightReminders` → `/flight-reminders` — module `bookings` — svcTypes `air_ticket` — bundle `ticketing` (Business)
- **4. Tour & Group Travel** — group `tourGroupTravel`, label `sidebar.tourGroupTravel`
  - `group-tours` — `sidebar.groupTours` → `/group-tours` — module `bookings` — minPlan `basic` — svcTypes `tour_domestic`, `tour_international`, `mice_event` — bundle `tourGroups` (Business)
  - `mice` — `sidebar.mice` → `/mice` — module `bookings` — minPlan `basic` — svcTypes `mice_event` — bundle `tourGroups` (Business)
  - `travel-approvals` — `sidebar.travelApprovals` → `/travel-approvals` — module `bookings` — minPlan `basic` — svcTypes `corporate_travel`, `mice_event` — bundle `corporate` (Business)
  - `visa-tracker` — `sidebar.visaTracker` → `/visa-tracker` — module `bookings` — minPlan `basic` — svcTypes `visa`, `study_abroad` — bundle `visa` (Business)
- **5. Operations** — group `operations`, label `sidebar.operations`
  - `documents` — `sidebar.documents` → `/documents` — module `clients` — bundle `documentsDesk` (Business)
  - `tasks` — `sidebar.tasks` → `/tasks` — module `tasks` — core
  - `service-operations` — `sidebar.serviceOperations` → `/operations/services` — module `bookings` — bundle `documentsDesk` (Business)
  - `hajj-operations` — `sidebar.hajjUmrahOperations` → `/hajj-umrah` — module `hajj_umrah` — svcTypes `hajj_umrah` — bundle `hajj` (Business)
  - `bd-operations` — `sidebar.bdOperations` → `/operations/bd` — module `bookings` — svcTypes `study_abroad`, `b2b_agent` — bundle `studentManpower` (Business)
- **6. Finance & Accounts** — group `financeAccounts`, label `sidebar.financeAccounts`
  - `invoices` — `sidebar.invoices` → `/invoices` — module `invoices` — core
  - `payments` — `sidebar.payments` → `/payments` — module `invoices` — core
  - `expenses` — `sidebar.expenses` → `/expenses` — module `accounts` — minPlan `basic` — core
  - `commissions` — `sidebar.commissions` → `/commissions` — module `agents` — svcTypes `air_ticket`, `b2b_agent`, `tour_domestic`, `tour_international`, `hajj_umrah` — bundle `subAgents` (Business)
  - `accounts` — `sidebar.accountsLedger` → `/accounts` — module `accounts` — minPlan `basic` — core
  - `reports` — `sidebar.financialReports` → `/reports` — module `reports` — minPlan `basic` — core
- **7. HR & Payroll** — group `hrPayroll`, label `sidebar.hrPayroll`
  - `team` — `sidebar.team` → `/team` — module `team` — core
  - `hrm` — `sidebar.hrm` → `/hrm` — module `team` — bundle `hrPayroll` (Business)
  - `roles` — `sidebar.roles` → `/roles` — module `team` — core
  - `activity-log` — `sidebar.activityLog` → `/activity-log` — module `team` — **conditionally injected** only when `showActivityLog` (owner/manager) — bundle `hrPayroll` (Business)
  - `payroll` — `sidebar.payroll` → `/payroll` — module `team` — minPlan `basic` — bundle `hrPayroll` (Business)
- **8. Marketing & Loyalty** — group `marketingLoyalty`, label `sidebar.marketingLoyalty`
  - `loyalty` — `sidebar.loyalty` → `/loyalty` — module `clients` — minPlan `enterprise` — bundle `marketing` (Business)
  - `referrals` — `sidebar.referrals` → `/referrals` — module `clients` — minPlan `enterprise` — bundle `marketing` (Business)
- **9. Website & CMS** — group `websiteCms`, label `sidebar.websiteCms`
  - `website-home` — `sidebar.websiteHome` → `/website` — module `website` — feat `hasWebsiteTemplates` — minPlan `pro` — bundle `website` (floor Pro, **autoOn**)
  - `website-builder` — `sidebar.themeBuilder` → `/website/builder` — module `website` — feat `hasWebsiteTemplates` — minPlan `pro` — bundle `website` (autoOn)
  - `website-blog` — `sidebar.websiteBlog` → `/website/blog` — module `website` — feat `hasWebsiteTemplates` — minPlan `pro` — bundle `website` (autoOn)
  - `website-publish` — `sidebar.publishDomain` → `/website/publish` — module `website` — feat `hasWebsiteTemplates` — minPlan `pro` — bundle `website` (autoOn)
  - `website-seo` — `sidebar.websiteSeo` → `/website/seo` — module `website` — feat `hasWebsiteTemplates` — minPlan `pro` — bundle `website` (autoOn)
- **10. Administration** — group `administration`, label `sidebar.administration`
  - `notifications` — `sidebar.notifications` → `/notifications` — module `reports` — core
  - `settings` — `sidebar.settings` → `/settings` — module `settings` — core
  - `organization` — `sidebar.organization` → `/organization` — module `organization` — core
  - `subscription` — `sidebar.subscription` → `/subscription` — module `subscription` — core
  - `userGuide` — `sidebar.userGuide` → `/user-guide` — module `dashboard` — core
- **(outside groups) Admin Panel** — `/admin` — rendered directly in `AppSidebar.tsx`, gated by `canAccessAdmin` (`super_admin` only), not part of `getNavigationGroups`.

## Advanced-module bundles (`moduleAccess.ts` lines 23-36)

Each bundle owns a set of nav-item ids and a `minPlan` floor (default **business**). All are OFF by default and toggled per-tenant in Settings, except `website` which is `autoOn` (a plan feature shown automatically to eligible plans, floor **pro**).

| Bundle id | labelKey | Nav items owned | minPlan | autoOn |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `crm` | modules.crm | crm-hub | business | no |
| `subAgents` | modules.subAgents | commissions | business | no |
| `corporate` | modules.corporate | corporate, travel-approvals | business | no |
| `ticketing` | modules.ticketing | ticket-transactions, flight-reminders | business | no |
| `tourGroups` | modules.tourGroups | group-tours, mice | business | no |
| `visa` | modules.visa | visa-tracker | business | no |
| `hajj` | modules.hajj | hajj-operations | business | no |
| `studentManpower` | modules.studentManpower | bd-operations | business | no |
| `documentsDesk` | modules.documentsDesk | documents, service-operations | business | no |
| `hrPayroll` | modules.hrPayroll | hrm, activity-log, payroll | business | no |
| `marketing` | modules.marketing | loyalty, referrals | business | no |
| `website` | modules.website | website-home, website-builder, website-blog, website-publish, website-seo | **pro** | **yes** |

Any nav item **not** listed above (dashboard, clients, agents, vendors, quotations, bookings, visa-stock, service-catalog, tasks, invoices, payments, expenses, accounts, reports, team, roles, notifications, settings, organization, subscription, userGuide) is a **core** item — `isNavItemModuleEnabled` returns `true` for it regardless of the enabled-module set.

## Filtering pipeline (how visibility is decided)

The sidebar applies filters at two levels. **`requiredServiceTypes` and `requiredFeature` are declarative metadata on the nav config but are NOT read by the live sidebar filter** — the actual gate is `canAccess(module)` + `isNavItemModuleEnabled(id, plan, enabledModules)` + `minPlan`. Service types influence the sidebar only *indirectly*, by populating the tenant's `enabledModules`.

### Item-level filter — `AppSidebarNav.tsx` `filterVisibleItems` (lines 84-93)
An item survives when **both** hold:
1. **RBAC gate** — `canAccess(item.module)` via `usePermissions()` → `canAccessModule(role, module)` (`permissions.ts` lines 228-239): true if the role's `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS[role][module]` has any truthy action. `super_admin` is always allowed. Legacy roles `owner`/`admin` → `tenant_owner`, `member` → `sales_agent` (`mapLegacyRole`).
2. **Module gate** — `isNavItemModuleEnabled(item.id, currentPlan, enabledModules)` (`moduleAccess.ts` lines 88-94):
   - Core item (no owning bundle) → `true`.
   - Below the bundle's plan floor (`planRank(plan) < planRank(moduleMinPlan(bundle))`) → `false`. Ranks: free 0, basic 1, pro 2, business 3, enterprise 4; `"unlimited"` normalizes to enterprise (`planRank`, lines 66-70).
   - `autoOn` bundle (only `website`) and plan is eligible → `true` (shown automatically, no toggle).
   - Otherwise visible only when `enabledModules` array includes the bundle id (the tenant opted in via Settings).

### Plan-floor rendering — `AppSidebarNav.tsx` `SidebarNavItem` (lines 75-82)
For items that pass the filter, `isPlanSufficient(item.minPlan, currentPlan)` is checked against `planOrder = ["free","basic","pro","business","enterprise"]` (lines 20-25). If the plan floor is **not** met, the item is not hidden — it renders as a **`LockedNavRow`**: greyed-out, non-clickable, with a lock icon and an "Upgrade to {Plan}" tooltip. If sufficient, it renders as an active `SidebarNavLeaf` (`NavLink`).

### Group-level visibility — `AppSidebarNavGroup` (lines 96-133)
A whole section is hidden (`return null`) when `visibleItems.length === 0`, i.e. every item in it was removed by RBAC or the module gate. Otherwise the group renders its label (a non-clickable header) and the surviving items. Note: locked (plan-insufficient) items still count as visible, so a group with only locked items is still shown.

### Service-types: indirect, not a sidebar filter
`requiredServiceTypes` is declared on items (e.g. hajj-operations → `hajj_umrah`, ticket-transactions → `air_ticket`) but `filterVisibleItems` never reads it. Instead, when a tenant picks service subcategories (Onboarding / Register / `ModuleSettings`), `buildServiceSelectionPayload` (`enabledServiceTypes.ts` lines 100-108) and `deriveModuleFlagsFromServiceTypes` (lines 76-88) translate the chosen services into the persisted set that drives `enabledModules` (plus legacy `enableHajjUmrahModule` / `enableBdOperationsModule` flags). So enabling a service type is the mechanism that flips the owning advanced bundle on, which is what the module gate checks. `getNavigationGroups` still accepts `enabledServiceTypes`/`enabledSubcategories`/`enableHajjUmrahModule`/`enableBdOperationsModule`, but the config comment (lines 76-78) and the deprecated options (lines 64-67) confirm these are effectively ignored for sidebar shaping — the module gate is now the single source. Route-level pages (ServiceOperations, `HajjModuleGate`, `BdModuleGate`) apply the real service-type checks when a URL is opened directly.

### Where the enabled-module set comes from
`tenant.enabledModules` (`api.ts` line 635) is a string array of bundle ids. It is edited by tenant owners in `src/components/ModuleSettings.tsx` (saved via `tenantApi.update({ enabledModules })`, line 77) and sanitized by `sanitizeEnabledModules` (`moduleAccess.ts` lines 97-100), which keeps only known bundle ids the current plan is allowed to activate (`planCanUseModule`).

### Active-state matching — `navActive.ts`
`navItemIsActive(url, pathname)` provides section-aware highlighting: `/bookings` and `/packages/all` match their sub-routes; `/accounts` also highlights on `/expenses`; `/hajj-umrah`, `/operations/bd` match their subtrees; `/dashboard` matches exactly; default is exact-or-prefix (`pathname === url || pathname.startsWith(url + "/")`). On route change, `AppSidebar` scrolls the active menu button into view (lines 37-47).

**Net decision order for one item:** RBAC `canAccess(module)` → owning-bundle plan floor → autoOn-or-tenant-opted-in (`enabledModules`) → if it passes, `minPlan` floor decides *active link* vs *locked "upgrade" row*; if the whole group ends up empty, the section header disappears.


---

# APPENDIX C — CUSTOMER PORTAL, WEBSITE CMS & MARKETING SITE

# Customer / Agent Portal, Website CMS & Marketing Site

The portals-cms area spans three distinct surfaces, each served by different hosts/subdomains and different auth namespaces:

1. **Self-service Portal** (`portal.` subdomain) — magic-link, read-only customer / supplier / agent access. Separate JWT audience.
2. **Website Builder / CMS** (agency dashboard, `/website/*`) — per-tenant public marketing site builder (themes, sections, blog, SEO, custom domains). Gated to Pro+.
3. **Public tenant sites** (`{slug}.domain` / custom domains, `/site/*`) + **SaaS marketing site** (root domain: `/`, `/features`, `/pricing`, etc.) and a super-admin **Landing CMS**.

Host routing is decided at bootstrap: `src/main.tsx` mounts `PortalApp` when `isPortalHost()` is true (host `portal.<APP_DOMAIN>`), otherwise the main `App`. Within `App`, `resolveHostname()` (`src/lib/domainResolver.ts`) classifies the host as `slug` / `custom-domain` / `main-app` / `portal` and switches the root route between the SaaS landing page (`Index`) and a tenant public site.

---

## 1. Customer / Supplier / Agent Portal

### 1.1 Auth model (`backend/src/routes/portal.js`, `backend/src/middleware/portalAuth.js`)

- **Email-based identity, no passwords.** A single email may hold multiple roles simultaneously.
- **Roles** are derived by `classifyEmail(emailLower)` which looks the email up across three tables:
  | Role | Source table | Match rule |
  |------|--------------|-----------|
  | `customer` | `Client.email` | exact match on lowercased email |
  | `supplier` | `Vendor.email` | exact match |
  | `agent` | `Agent.email` | case-insensitive (`mode: "insensitive"`), excludes empty string |
- **Separate JWT audience.** Portal session tokens use audience `"portal"` (`PORTAL_AUDIENCE`); magic-link tokens use `"portal-magic"` (`MAGIC_AUDIENCE`). This means portal tokens cannot be used against agency (main-app) endpoints and vice versa. `SECRET` comes from the shared `getJwtSecret()`.
- **Magic-link lifetime:** magic token = 15 min; exchanged session token = 7 days.
- **Frontend token store** (`src/lib/portalApi.ts`): stored in `localStorage` under key `portal_token`, sent as `Authorization: Bearer`.

### 1.2 Portal endpoints (all under `/api/portal`)

| Method | Path | Auth | Role gate | Purpose |
|--------|------|------|-----------|---------|
| POST | `/auth/request-link` | rate-limited (`portalAuthLimiter`) | — | Classify email, email a magic sign-in link. **Always returns `{ ok: true }`** even for unknown emails (anti-enumeration). |
| POST | `/auth/verify` | rate-limited | — | Verify magic token, **re-classify roles** (in case they changed), issue 7-day session JWT. Returns `{ token, email, roles }`. |
| GET | `/auth/me` | `portalAuthenticate` | — | Returns `{ token: "", email, roles }` for the current session. |
| GET | `/bookings` | `portalAuthenticate` | customer (else `[]`) | List of the customer's bookings. |
| GET | `/bookings/:id` | `portalAuthenticate` | customer (403 if not) | Booking detail (sanitized). 404 if booking not owned by this email. |
| GET | `/agent/bookings` | `portalAuthenticate` | agent (else `[]`) | B2B sub-agent's bookings (with commission fields). |
| GET | `/agent/commissions` | `portalAuthenticate` | agent (else zeroed summary) | Commission wallet: pending/paid totals + booking list. |
| GET | `/purchase-orders` | `portalAuthenticate` | supplier (else `[]`) | Vendor bills issued to this supplier email, presented as "purchase orders". |

**Magic-link email** (`sendEmail`): subject "Your sign-in link", HTML button linking to `${PORTAL_URL}/verify?token=...`. `PORTAL_URL` defaults to the `FRONTEND_URL` with the host rewritten to `portal.` (fallback `https://portal.travelagencyweb.com`).

### 1.3 Data shapes returned (customer)

- **`GET /bookings`** selects: `id, title, destination, travelDateFrom, travelDateTo, status, paymentStatus, amount, paidAmount, dueAmount`, plus `tenantName` (flattened from `tenant.name`). Ordered `createdAt desc`.
- **`GET /bookings/:id`** includes `tenant`, `travelers` (id/name/nationality), `invoices` (with nested `installments` sorted by `sortOrder`), and last-20 `timeline` events, then runs through `sanitizePortalBookingDetail`.

### 1.4 Portal response sanitization (`backend/src/lib/portalBooking.js`)

Customer-facing responses are deliberately stripped of internal cost/profit data:

- `sanitizePortalBookingDetail(booking)` — returns only customer-safe booking fields (`id, type, title, destination, travelDate*, travelerCount, status, paymentStatus, amount, paidAmount, dueAmount, serviceType, tenantName`), maps travelers/invoices/installments through their own sanitizers, and **filters the timeline to only `status_change` and `system` event types** (`CUSTOMER_TIMELINE_TYPES`) — internal notes are hidden.
- `sanitizeInvoice` / `sanitizeInstallment` — expose only number/status/date fields.
- `sanitizeAgentBooking(row)` — flattens `clientName`, `tenantName`, and `commissionAmount`/`commissionStatus` from `agentCommission`.
- `summarizeAgentCommissions(bookings)` — sums `agentCommissionAmount` into `paidTotal` (status `"paid"`) vs `pendingTotal` (any positive non-paid), rounds to 2 dp, returns `bookingCount`.

### 1.5 Portal frontend (`src/portal/`)

Standalone SPA (`PortalApp.tsx`) with its own `BrowserRouter`, React-Query client, and toasters — completely separate from the main app shell.

**Routes** (`PortalApp.tsx`):
| Path | Component | Guard |
|------|-----------|-------|
| `/login` | `PortalLogin` | public |
| `/verify` | `PortalVerify` | public |
| `/` (index) | → redirect to `/bookings` | `RequireAuth` |
| `/bookings` | `MyBookings` | `RequireAuth` |
| `/bookings/:id` | `BookingDetail` | `RequireAuth` |
| `/agent` | `AgentCommissions` | `RequireAuth` |
| `/purchase-orders` | `MyPurchaseOrders` | `RequireAuth` |
| `*` | → redirect to `/` | — |

`RequireAuth` = presence of `getPortalToken()` else `<Navigate to="/login">`.

**Pages:**
- **`PortalLogin.tsx`** — single email input → `portalApi.requestMagicLink`; on success shows "check your email" state with a "different email" reset. i18n keys under `portal.*`.
- **`PortalVerify.tsx`** — reads `?token=`, calls `portalApi.verify`, stores token, then redirects by role priority: **customer → `/bookings`, else agent → `/agent`, else → `/purchase-orders`**. Shows error + "request new link" link on failure.
- **`PortalLayout.tsx`** — shared header with role-conditional nav (customer → My Bookings, agent → agent nav, supplier → purchase orders), an EN/বাং language toggle, and logout (clears token → `/login`). Fetches session via `portalApi.me` (React-Query key `portal-me`).
- **`MyBookings.tsx`** — card list; each card shows title/destination/dates/agency, status + paymentStatus badges, `paid / total` in Taka (`৳`), and due amount in red. Links to detail.
- **`BookingDetail.tsx`** — back link; header with status badges; **Payment summary** (total/paid/due); **Travelers** card; **Invoices** card (each invoice with paid/total/due + nested installments showing label, due date, paid/amount, status); **Updates** (timeline) card.
- **`AgentCommissions.tsx`** — three summary cards (Pending total, Paid total in green, Booking count) + per-booking list showing client name, amount, and commission amount/status badge.
- **`MyPurchaseOrders.tsx`** — card list of vendor bills: description, from-agency, created date, due date, status badge, paid/total, due in red.

All portal money is formatted `৳{n.toLocaleString()}`.

---

## 2. Website Builder / CMS (agency dashboard)

### 2.1 Feature gate & routes

- Backend `website.js` and `blogs.js` routers both apply `router.use(authenticate)` then `router.use(requireFeature("hasWebsiteTemplates"))` — the whole builder + blog CMS is a **paid feature (Pro+)**. Per-endpoint `requirePermission("website", <action>)` further gates by RBAC.
- Frontend routes (`src/App.tsx`), all wrapped in `<PM module="website">` (ProtectedRoute + PermissionRoute):
  | Path | Component |
  |------|-----------|
  | `/website` | `WebsiteBuilderHome` (overview hub) |
  | `/website/blog` | `WebsiteBlog` |
  | `/website/builder` | `WebsiteCustomizer` (Theme Builder) |
  | `/website/publish` | `WebsitePublishGuide` |
  | `/website/domains` | `WebsitePublishGuide` (alias) |
  | `/website/seo` | `WebsiteSeo` (lazy) |

### 2.2 Config storage model (`backend/src/routes/website.js`)

There is **no dedicated website table** — the entire website config is JSON persisted in `Tenant.notes` under key `websiteConfig` (and `socialLinks` mirrored to `notes.socialLinks`). `parseNotes()` tolerates legacy plain-text notes.

**Dual-shape model with bidirectional sync:**
- Legacy `content.*` object (the surface the `WebsiteCustomizer` UI actually edits) is authoritative.
- A newer `cms.sections[]` array (versioned, `version: 1`) drives ordering/enable/reorder.
- `buildSectionsFromLegacy(config)` seeds 10 canonical sections in order: **hero, about, services, featured-packages, why-choose-us, testimonials, faq, team, cta, contact**.
- `syncLegacyToSections()` pushes fresh `content.*` edits into matching section fields (prevents a stale server section snapshot from clobbering new edits like an uploaded hero image).
- `applyCmsToLegacy()` is the inverse (sections → legacy content), and re-numbers `order`.
- `normalizeWebsiteConfig()` runs the full round-trip on both GET and POST; `validateWebsiteConfig()` requires `template` (string), `colors` (object), `content` (object).
- `ensureSectionShape()` coerces each section to `{ id, type, label, enabled, order, fields, items }`.

### 2.3 Website builder endpoints (`/api/website`)

| Method | Path | Permission | Purpose |
|--------|------|------------|---------|
| POST | `/upload-asset` | website:edit | Upload logo/hero/about/gallery image (base64/dataUrl). Validates asset type ∈ {logo,hero,about,gallery} and MIME ∈ {png,jpeg,jpg,webp}. **SVG explicitly excluded** (stored-XSS risk from embedded JS). Max **8 MB**. Writes to `uploads/website-assets/{tenantId}/{assetType}-{ts}-{name}.{ext}`; returns `{ assetUrl, fileName }`. |
| GET | `/config` | website:view | Return normalized config; `null` if not configured yet (not an error — clients fall back to template defaults). |
| POST | `/config` | website:edit | Normalize + validate + save. |
| GET | `/sections` | website:view | Return `cms.sections[]`. |
| POST | `/sections` | website:edit | Append a new section (`type` required); re-orders all. |
| PATCH | `/sections/:sectionId` | website:edit | Merge fields/items into a section. 404 if not found. |
| POST | `/sections/reorder` | website:edit | Reorder by `sectionIds[]` (unlisted sections appended after). |
| DELETE | `/sections/:sectionId` | website:edit | Remove a section, re-number order. |

**MIME allow-list** (`ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES`): `image/png→.png`, `image/jpeg→.jpg`, `image/jpg→.jpg`, `image/webp→.webp`. `sanitizeBaseName` strips unsafe chars and caps at 50 chars. `buildAssetResponse` derives an absolute origin from `API_BASE_URL` (or request host).

### 2.4 Frontend website config model (`src/lib/websiteApi.ts`)

- **3 templates:** `travel-agency` ✈️, `hajj-umrah` 🕌, `tour-packages` 🌴 — each with full `templateDefaults[...]` (colors + rich sample content: hero/about, services, stats, testimonials, faq, team, package labels, footer). Hajj/Umrah defaults include Bangla content.
- **12 theme presets** (`themePresets[]`), 4 per template:
  | Template | Presets |
  |----------|---------|
  | travel-agency | Skyline Blue, Luxury Gold, Ocean Teal, Midnight Elite |
  | hajj-umrah | Haramain Green, Royal Maroon, Desert Amber, Emerald Deen |
  | tour-packages | Adventure Purple, Sunset Coral, Tropical Cyan, Forest Explorer |
  Each preset has `id, name, description, template, colors, palettePreview, sampleContent`.
- **`WebsiteConfig`** shape: `template`, `logo`, `colors{primary,secondary,accent,background,text}`, `content{...}` (hero, about, services[], stats[], testimonials[], faq[], team[], whyChooseUs[], package labels/buttons, ctaTitle/Subtitle, footerText), `socialLinks{facebook,instagram,twitter,whatsapp,youtube,linkedin}`, `contactInfo{phone,email,address,mapEmbed}`, `buttons{...}`.
- **`resolveButtons(config)`** resolves 8 button colors (primaryBg/Text, secondaryBg/Text, outline, contact, whatsapp, backToTop) each falling back to a theme palette color.
- **`websiteApi`** methods: `getConfig` (falls back to `travel-agency` defaults on empty/error), `saveConfig`, `uploadAsset` (client-side optimizes non-SVG images: resizes to max 900px logo / 1600px others, re-encodes to WebP, iteratively drops quality until < 900 KB), `getPublicConfig(slug)`, `getPublicConfigByDomain(domain)`.

### 2.5 `WebsiteCustomizer.tsx` (Theme Builder) — the main builder UI

Loads config + domains on mount; `ensureConfig()` deep-merges incoming config with template defaults and back-fills empty arrays. Auto-seeds contact email from the logged-in user's email if blank.

**Top cards:** Quick setup (agency name [read-only], template [read-only], hero badge/title/subtitle, phone, email); Builder status (theme preset, logo/hero/packages readiness badges); Live URL (primary domain or default URL + copy).

**6 tabs:**
1. **Themes** — grouped by template; each preset rendered with a `ThemeMiniPreview` (a live mini mock website with navbar/hero/stats/services/packages/CTA/footer rendered from the preset colors) + color swatches. Click = `applyPreset` (preserves logo + hero/about images + contact + social).
2. **Branding** — 3 image uploaders (logo/hero/about) each supporting upload **or** paste image URL; upload auto-saves. Color controls: 5 HSL colors (primary/secondary/accent/background/text) with native color picker (hex↔HSL conversion helpers `hslToHex`/`hexToHsl`) + manual HSL input. Button colors: 8 individually settable button colors (blank = inherit theme).
3. **Agency (content)** — hero section, about & CTA (about title/text, CTA title/subtitle, footer text), contact info (phone/email/address/Google Map embed URL), social links (facebook/instagram/twitter/whatsapp/youtube/linkedin).
4. **Packages** — homepage packages section labels (badge/title/subtitle), all-packages page labels + button texts, "Reset to template" button; note that actual package data is managed in the ERP `/packages`.
5. **Sections** — repeatable-item editors: Service cards (icon from 25-icon list + title + desc), Stats/counters, Why-choose-us, Testimonials (name/date/star rating 1-5/text), FAQ (question/answer), Team (name/role/photo URL/bio).
6. **Publish** — live URL banner (copy/open); Publishing summary (account, plan, domain allowance, active domain); **Connect a custom domain** form + connected-domains list with DNS TXT instructions (see §2.7). A floating "Save website" bar is always visible.

Super-admin sees a warning that the builder edits the currently-logged-in tenant only.

### 2.6 Blog CMS

**Backend (`/api/blogs`, `blogs.js`)** — model `WebsitePost` (tenant-scoped). Slugs auto-generated + de-duplicated via `uniqueSlug` (appends `-1`, `-2`, …). `publishedAt` set when status flips to `published`, cleared on `draft`.
| Method | Path | Permission |
|--------|------|-----------|
| GET | `/` (optional `?status=`) | website:view |
| GET | `/:id` | website:view |
| POST | `/` | website:create |
| PATCH | `/:id` | website:edit |
| DELETE | `/:id` | website:delete |

**`WebsitePost` fields** (`BlogPost` interface): `id, tenantId, slug, title, excerpt, body, coverImage?, status(draft|published), publishedAt?, authorName?, createdAt, updatedAt`.

**Frontend (`WebsiteBlog.tsx`)** — searchable table (title/slug, status badge, published date, edit/delete actions gated per-permission via `PermissionGate`). Create/edit dialog with title, slug (auto if blank), status select, excerpt, **Markdown body editor with Write/Preview toggle** (in-house `renderMarkdown` supporting h1-h3, `-`/`*` lists, `**bold**`, `*italic*`, `` `code` ``, `[link](url)`), cover image URL, author name. Uses `blogApi` (`src/lib/api.ts`).

### 2.7 Custom domains & publishing

**Backend (`/api/tenant-domains`, `tenantDomains.js`)** — requires `authenticate` + `requireRole("tenant_owner")`. Model `TenantDomain`.
- **Per-plan domain limits** (`DOMAIN_PLAN_LIMITS`): `free:0, basic:0, pro:1, business:2, enterprise:-1, unlimited:-1` (`-1` = unlimited). Also requires active/trial subscription.
| Method | Path | Purpose |
|--------|------|---------|
| GET | `/summary` | Plan, limits, used/allowed domain counts, `canAddDomain`, `defaultWebsiteUrl` (= `${FRONTEND_URL}/site/{slug}`). |
| GET | `/` | List domains (primary first). |
| POST | `/` | Add domain: normalizes (strips protocol/www/path), validates plan+status+limit, rejects dupes (409 with `sameTenant` / `existingTenantName` hints), generates `tas-verify-…` token, marks first domain primary. Writes an **audit log** (`module:"domains"`, action `tenant_create`). |
| POST | `/:id/verify` | Sets status `verifying`, then queries **Google DNS-over-HTTPS** (`https://dns.google/resolve?name=_verify.{domain}&type=TXT`) and compares the TXT value to the token. Sets `verified`/`unverified`, updates `lastDnsCheck`, audit-logs `tenant_verify`/`tenant_verify_failed`. |
| PATCH | `/:id/primary` | Requires `verified`; unsets other primaries, sets this one. Audit `tenant_set_primary`. |
| DELETE | `/:id` | Deletes; if it was primary, promotes the oldest remaining. Audit `tenant_delete`. |

**Domain record fields** (`TenantDomainRecord`): `id, tenantId, domain, wwwRedirect(www-to-root|root-to-www), verificationToken, verificationStatus(unverified|verifying|verified), status(pending|active|error), sslStatus(none|pending|active), isPrimary, lastDnsCheck, createdAt, updatedAt`. SSL/activation are completed separately by super-admin (verify is tenant-self-service, activation is not).

**Frontend (`tenantDomainApi.ts`)** — `getSummary, list, add, verify, setPrimary, remove`.

**`WebsitePublishGuide.tsx`** (`/website/publish`, `/website/domains`) — bilingual (EN/Bangla) publish hub: default URL card, primary live-domain card, domain-limit card, quick publish steps, add-domain form (name + redirect rule), connected-domains list with verify/set-primary/remove + status badges (Verified/Needs verify, Active/Pending, SSL Ready/Pending). Same domain CRUD is also embedded in the `WebsiteCustomizer` Publish tab, which additionally shows the DNS TXT setup instructions (Name/Host `_verify`, Value = token, TTL 300) and A-record guidance.

**`WebsiteBuilderHome.tsx`** (`/website`) — bilingual overview hub with cards linking to Theme Builder, Publish & Domain, Packages, Blog, and Public site; shows default URL, primary domain (with status), and domain usage `used/limit`.

### 2.8 SEO (`WebsiteSeo.tsx`, `/website/seo`)

SEO settings are stored as JSON in `Tenant.notes` under key `seo` (via `tenantApi.get`/`update`). Three tabs:
- **Meta Tags** — `siteTitle, siteDescription, siteKeywords, googleVerification, bingVerification`.
- **Social / OG** — `ogTitle, ogDescription, ogImage` (with live preview).
- **Sitemap** — displays the auto-generated sitemap URL `${VITE_API_URL}/public/sitemap/{slug}` with Google Search Console submit instructions.

Sitemap/robots are generated server-side (see §3.3).

---

## 3. Public tenant sites (`/site/*`, tenant subdomains, custom domains)

### 3.1 Tenant context (`src/contexts/WebsiteContext.tsx`)

`WebsiteProvider` resolves the host, then for tenant sites fetches `tenant`, `packages`, and `websiteConfig` in parallel (`publicApi` + `websiteApi`), by **slug** (`identifier`) or **custom domain**. It **holds the first paint** (spinner) until real data loads so visitors never see demo content flash; on failure it renders `DomainErrorPage`. Falls back to hard-coded `demoTenant` + `demoPackages` (a Bengali travel agency demo) + `travel-agency` template defaults for the main app. Exposes `{ tenant, packages, websiteConfig, loading, domainResolution }` via `useWebsite()`.

### 3.2 Public backend API (`/api/public`, `public.js`) — no auth

| Method | Path | Purpose |
|--------|------|---------|
| GET | `/:slug` | Tenant public info (`publicTenant`: id, name, slug, logo, description=hero subtitle, phone, address, website, socialLinks). Email deliberately `undefined`. |
| GET | `/:slug/packages` | Published packages (`?featured=true` filter). Merges **TravelPackage** (status `published`) + **HajjPackage** (status ≠ archived), mapped to a unified `PackagePublic` shape (`publicTravelPackage`/`publicHajjPackage`), highlights derived from inclusions + itinerary day titles. |
| GET | `/:slug/blog-posts` | Published `WebsitePost` list. |
| GET | `/:slug/blog-posts/:postSlug` | Single published post. |
| GET | `/:slug/website` | Saved website config (normalized). |
| GET | `/domain/:domain` | Tenant by custom domain — only if `findReadyDomain` returns a domain that is `active`+`verified` **and** the tenant is on a domain-enabled plan (`pro/business/enterprise/unlimited`) with active/trial status (`tenantCanUseCustomDomain`). |
| GET | `/domain/:domain/packages` | Packages by custom domain. |
| GET | `/domain/:domain/website` | Config by custom domain. |
| GET | `/sitemap/:slug` | XML sitemap (home/packages/about/contact + one `<url>` per published package and blog post), base `https://{slug}.travelagencyweb.com`. |
| GET | `/robots/:slug` | `robots.txt` allowing all + sitemap link. |
| POST | `/book` | **Public booking inquiry** — resolves tenant by slug or ready custom domain, rejects if subscription not active/trial (403), creates a **`Lead`** (`source:"website"`, tag `web-inquiry`) + a `LeadActivity` note, then fire-and-forget notifies the agency owner via **WhatsApp + email** (owner contact from `resolveTenantOwnerContact`). Requires name + phone. |

`publicApi` (`src/lib/publicApi.ts`) client also exposes `submitContact` (POST `/contact`) and `submitDemo` (POST `/demo-requests`).

### 3.3 Public site pages (`src/pages/site/`)

All wrapped in `PublicLayout` and driven by `useWebsite()`.

| Page | File | Content |
|------|------|---------|
| Home | `SiteHome.tsx` | Renders one of 3 template components (`TravelAgencyTemplate` / `HajjUmrahTemplate` / `TourPackagesTemplate` in `src/components/templates/`) chosen by `websiteConfig.template`. |
| About | `SiteAbout.tsx` | Static story/stats/values sections, personalized with `tenant.name`. |
| Packages | `SitePackages.tsx` | Search + type-filter (tour/ticket/hotel/visa/hajj/umrah/etc.) grid of published packages; Featured + Visa-required badges; price in `৳`; **`BookingModal`** (`BookingPackage`) on "Book Now". Logged-in users see "Manage packages"/"Edit package section" shortcuts. Bilingual labels. |
| Pricing | `SitePricing.tsx` | Standalone (own nav/footer, not `PublicLayout`) SaaS pricing — plan cards + feature-comparison table from `src/lib/plans` (`PLANS`, `FEATURE_COMPARISON`); subscribe links to `/register?plan=` or `/site/contact`. |
| Contact | `SiteContact.tsx` | Contact form → `publicApi.submitContact({...form, tenantSlug})`; contact info + social links from `tenant`. |
| Blog | `SiteBlog.tsx` | Blog list + single-post view (`:postSlug`), Markdown-rendered body (in-house `renderMarkdown`). Blog base path switches between `/blog` (tenant host) and `/site/blog` based on `domainResolution.type`. |

**Routing wrinkle:** On a tenant public host (`slug` or `custom-domain`), the bare paths `/`, `/about`, `/packages`, `/blog`, `/contact` serve the tenant site (`PublicSiteRoute`/`TenantBlogPostRoute`). On the main app root they serve the SaaS `Index` landing (or redirect `app.` subdomain to `/login`). Explicit `/site/*` and `/site/:slug/*` routes exist for previewing any tenant by slug.

---

## 4. SaaS Marketing Site (root domain)

### 4.1 Marketing pages (`src/pages/marketing/`)

All use `MarketingLayout` (dark theme, amber/orange accent) and are fully i18n-driven (`marketing.*` keys, EN/Bangla).

| Route | File | Content |
|-------|------|---------|
| `/features` | `Features.tsx` | 6-step workflow strip + 8 feature groups (leads, quotes, bookings, invoices, vendors, team, reports, hajj) with bullet points; CTAs to `/pricing` and `/demo`. |
| `/pricing` | `Pricing.tsx` | Full SaaS pricing page (18 KB — plan cards, comparison, billing). |
| `/demo` | `Demo.tsx` | Demo-request form (name, company, email, phone, team size, message) → `publicApi.submitDemo`; "what to expect" + testimonials. |
| `/contact-us` | `ContactUs.tsx` | Contact form (name, email, phone, subject, message) → `publicApi.submitContact`; contact info cards. |
| `/faq` | `FAQ.tsx` | Accordion FAQ in 5 categories (general, pricing, features, security, support), all i18n. |
| `/privacy` | `Privacy.tsx` | Legal doc via `<LegalRenderer ns="privacy">`. |
| `/terms` | `Terms.tsx` | Legal doc via `<LegalRenderer ns="terms">`. |

### 4.2 SaaS landing page (`src/pages/Index.tsx`)

The root `/` on the main app. Renders hero / stats / workflow / features / testimonials / faq / cta sections. Content is fetched from the **Landing CMS** (`GET /landing-cms`); if the CMS returns data it overrides the i18n defaults (`defaultCms(t)`), otherwise localized defaults are used.

### 4.3 Landing CMS (super-admin) (`backend/src/routes/landingCms.js`, `/api/landing-cms`)

File-backed (not DB): reads/writes `backend/data/landing-cms.json`.
| Method | Path | Auth | Purpose |
|--------|------|------|---------|
| GET | `/` | **public** | Serve current landing content (consumed by `Index.tsx` and admin editor). |
| POST | `/` | `authenticate` + `requireSuperAdmin` | Overwrite the whole CMS JSON. |
| POST | `/upload` | super-admin | Upload hero/about image (base64) → `uploads/landing/{ts}-{safeName}`, returns `{ url }`. |

**Admin editor** `src/pages/admin/AdminLandingCMS.tsx` (`/admin/landing-cms`, super-admin only via `<A>` AdminRoute; linked from `AdminSidebar` "Landing Page CMS"). Edits the `LandingCms` shape: `hero{badge,title1,title2,subtitle,ctaText,heroImage}`, `stats[]`, `workflow{badge,title,subtitle,steps[]}`, `features{badge,title,subtitle,items[]}`, `testimonials{badge,title,subtitle,items[]}`, `faq{badge,title,items[]}`, `cta{title,subtitle}`. `mergeCms` fills gaps from defaults.

---

## 5. Cross-cutting notes

- **Three separate auth namespaces:** portal JWT (`aud: "portal"`), main-app JWT (`localStorage.token`), and public/unauthenticated endpoints. The portal is intentionally sandboxed from agency endpoints.
- **Domain resolution reserved subdomains** (`domainResolver.ts`): `app`, `portal`, `api`, `www`, `admin` are never treated as tenant slugs. `portal.` → PortalApp; `app.`/`admin.` → main app; other subdomains of `APP_DOMAIN` → tenant slug; anything else → custom-domain lookup.
- **Backend route mounts** (`backend/src/app.js`): `/api/tenant-domains`, `/api/blogs`, `/api/website`, `/api/public`, `/api/portal`, `/api/landing-cms`, and `/api/admin/domains` (+ legacy `/api/domains` alias). `portal.travelagencyweb.com` is in the CORS allow-list.
- **Security specifics:** SVG uploads blocked in the tenant website uploader (stored-XSS); portal `request-link` never reveals whether an email exists; portal booking detail hides internal timeline/cost data; custom-domain public serving requires verified+active domain AND a domain-enabled plan even after the tenant self-verifies DNS.

**Key files:** portal — `backend/src/routes/portal.js`, `backend/src/middleware/portalAuth.js`, `backend/src/lib/portalBooking.js`, `src/lib/portalApi.ts`, `src/portal/**`. Website CMS — `backend/src/routes/website.js`, `blogs.js`, `tenantDomains.js`, `public.js`, `landingCms.js`, `src/lib/websiteApi.ts`, `src/lib/tenantDomainApi.ts`, `src/lib/publicApi.ts`, `src/lib/domainResolver.ts`, `src/contexts/WebsiteContext.tsx`, `src/pages/WebsiteBuilderHome.tsx`, `WebsiteCustomizer.tsx`, `WebsiteBlog.tsx`, `WebsiteSeo.tsx`, `WebsitePublishGuide.tsx`. Public/marketing — `src/pages/site/**`, `src/pages/marketing/**`, `src/pages/Index.tsx`, `src/pages/admin/AdminLandingCMS.tsx`, `src/components/templates/**`.


---

# APPENDIX D — INTEGRATIONS & FRONTEND ARCHITECTURE (contexts, hooks, providers, api client)

# Integrations & Frontend Architecture

## Part 1 — External Integrations

All third-party integrations live in `backend/src/services/` and `backend/src/routes/`. Integration **credentials are environment-variable–driven** (server `.env`); DB tables (`SmsSettings`, `PaymentMethodConfig`, `WhatsAppLog`, `NotificationAutomation`) hold only per-tenant *toggles / receiver metadata / logs / quotas* — never provider secrets. Config-management endpoints explicitly refuse to store secrets (`sms.js PUT /config` returns 400 for `apiKey`/`apiSecret`).

### 1.1 Integration Inventory

| Integration | Provider(s) | Service / Route file | Configured via |
|---|---|---|---|
| Online payment | **SSLCommerz** | `routes/sslcommerz.js` + `services/paymentGateway.js` | env: `SSLCOMMERZ_STORE_ID`, `SSLCOMMERZ_STORE_PASSWORD`, `SSLCOMMERZ_SANDBOX` |
| Online payment | **bKash** (tokenized checkout) | `routes/bkash.js` + `services/paymentGateway.js` | env: `BKASH_APP_KEY`, `BKASH_APP_SECRET`, `BKASH_USERNAME`, `BKASH_PASSWORD`, `BKASH_SANDBOX` |
| Manual MFS / bank (proof-based) | bKash / Nagad / Rocket / Bank Transfer | `lib/paymentGatewayConfig.js` | env: `*_ACCOUNT_NAME`, `*_ACCOUNT_NUMBER`, `*_INSTRUCTIONS`, `BANK_NAME`, `BANK_BRANCH`; tenant overrides in `PaymentMethodConfig` table |
| SMS | **BulkSMSBD**, **Twilio**, console fallback | `services/smsService.js` | env: `SMS_PROVIDER`, `SMS_API_KEY`, `SMS_SENDER_ID`, `SMS_BASE_URL`, `TWILIO_*` |
| WhatsApp | **WasenderAPI**, **Meta WhatsApp Cloud API**, **Twilio WhatsApp**, console fallback | `services/whatsappService.js` | env: `WHATSAPP_PROVIDER`, `WASENDER_API_KEY`, `META_WHATSAPP_TOKEN`, `META_WHATSAPP_PHONE_ID`, `WHATSAPP_FROM_NUMBER`, `TWILIO_*` |
| Email / SMTP | **Nodemailer SMTP** (any host), console fallback | `services/emailService.js` | env: `SMTP_HOST`, `SMTP_PORT`, `SMTP_SECURE`, `SMTP_USER`, `SMTP_PASS`, `SMTP_FROM`, `SMTP_FROM_NAME` |
| Telegram | **Telegram Bot API** (admin alerts) | `services/telegramService.js` | env: `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`, `TELEGRAM_ADMIN_CHAT_ID` |

Every messaging channel **fails soft**: if the provider isn't configured, the message is `console.log`ged instead of sent (no throw).

### 1.2 Payment Gateways

**Shared service — `services/paymentGateway.js`** (used by both bKash + SSLCommerz):
- `handlePaymentSuccess({ transactionId, invoiceId, paymentRequestId, amount, method, gateway, tenantId, metadata })` — the single idempotent success handler. Two branches:
  1. **Invoice branch** (`invoiceId` set): creates a `Payment` (idempotent on `transactionRef`), recomputes invoice `paidAmount`/`dueAmount`/`status` (`paid`/`partial`/`unpaid`), rolls the totals up to the parent `Booking` (`paymentStatus`), writes a ledger `Transaction` (type `income`, category `online_payment`, via `ensureLedgerTransaction`, idempotent on `referenceType:"payment"`+`referenceId`), then fires `notifyEvent("payment_received", …)`.
  2. **Subscription branch** (`paymentRequestId` set): `activateSubscriptionFromPaymentRequest()` — approves the `PaymentRequest`, sets `tenant.subscriptionPlan/Status/Expiry` (adds 12 mo for yearly / 1 mo for monthly; for a `renew` on a still-valid plan it extends from current expiry), inserts `Subscription` + `SubscriptionHistory` rows (`source: "payment_gateway"`), and fires `notifyEvent("subscription_activated", …)` to the tenant owner.
- `auditPaymentEvent({ action, gateway, … })` — writes an `AuditLog` row (`module: "payment_gateway"`, actor `system`) for every gateway event.
- `getCallbackUrls(gateway)` — builds `{success, fail, cancel, ipn, frontendCallback}` from `API_BASE_URL` (default `http://localhost:${PORT|4000}/api`) and `FRONTEND_URL` (default `https://travelagencyweb.com`).

**bKash — `routes/bkash.js`** (mounted at `/api/payments/bkash`):
- Tokenized Checkout v1.2.0-beta. Sandbox URL `tokenized.sandbox.bka.sh`, live `tokenized.pay.bka.sh` (switch on `BKASH_SANDBOX !== "false"`).
- **In-memory token cache** (`tokenCache`, refreshed 60s before `expires_in`; comment notes "production should use Redis").
- `POST /create` (authenticated) — grants token, `POST /create` to bKash with `mode:"0011"`, stores the `{invoiceId, paymentRequestId, tenantId, amount}` context in an `AuditLog` row (`action:"bkash_created"`, keyed by `paymentID`), returns `bkashURL` redirect.
- `GET /callback` — on `status=success`, executes the payment (`/execute`), and if `transactionStatus==="Completed"` re-reads the stored context from `AuditLog`, calls `handlePaymentSuccess`, then redirects to `${FRONTEND}/payment/callback?status=…&gateway=bkash`.
- Returns **HTTP 503** if any of the four bKash env vars are missing.

**SSLCommerz — `routes/sslcommerz.js`** (mounted at `/api/payments/sslcommerz`):
- Sandbox `sandbox.sslcommerz.com`, live `securepay.sslcommerz.com` (switch on `SSLCOMMERZ_SANDBOX !== "false"`).
- `POST /initiate` (authenticated) — form-POST to `/gwprocess/v4`; carries `invoiceId`/`paymentRequestId`/`tenantId`/`amount` in the SSLCommerz `value_a…value_d` passthrough fields; returns `GatewayPageURL`.
- `POST /success` — **validates** `val_id` against `/validator/api/validationserverAPI.php` (must be `VALID`/`VALIDATED`) before calling `handlePaymentSuccess`; redirects to frontend callback.
- `POST /fail`, `POST /cancel` — audit + redirect.
- `POST /ipn` — server-to-server; on `status VALID|VALIDATED` calls `handlePaymentSuccess` (source `ipn`) and returns `{status:"ok"}`. Provides at-least-once delivery guarantee (idempotency handled downstream).
- Returns **HTTP 503** if store creds missing.

**Gateway status config — `lib/paymentGatewayConfig.js`** (env only, no DB):
- `isBkashOnlineConfigured()` (needs all 4 bKash vars), `isSslcommerzConfigured()` (needs store id+pass).
- `getGatewayStatus()` → `{bkash, sslcommerz, manual}` each with `configured/sandbox/mode` (`sandbox`|`live`|`disabled`).
- `getGatewayStatusDetailed()` adds `apiBaseUrl`, all callback URLs, and manual method summary.
- `getDefaultManualPaymentMethods()` — returns 4 receivers (`bkash`, `nagad`, `rocket`, `bank_transfer`); each `enabled` iff an account number env var exists. `isOnlinePaymentMethod(code)` = `code.startsWith("online_")`.
- **Payments router** `routes/payments.js` mounts the two sub-routers and exposes `GET /gateway-status` (auth), `POST /initiate`, `GET /status/:transactionId`, `POST /callback/:gateway`.

### 1.3 SMS — `services/smsService.js`

- `sendSms({ to, message })` dispatches on `SMS_PROVIDER` (`twilio` | `bulksmsbd` | `console` default).
- **BulkSMSBD** (`sendViaBulkSmsBD`): GET `${SMS_BASE_URL||http://bulksmsbd.net/api}/smsapi` with `api_key`, `type:"text"`, `number`, `senderid`, `message`. Numbers normalized to `88017XXXXXXXX` (no `+`, via `toBulkSmsNumber`). Response code `202` = success; a full `BULKSMSBD_ERRORS` map (1001–1032) translates failures (invalid number, sender disabled, insufficient balance, IP not whitelisted, etc.).
- **Twilio** (`sendViaTwilio`): POST to `api.twilio.com/2010-04-01/Accounts/{SID}/Messages.json` with Basic auth; needs `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID`+`TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN`+`TWILIO_FROM_NUMBER`.
- `getSmsBalance()` — BulkSMSBD-only diagnostic (`/getBalanceApi`), super-admin use.
- **Templates**: `getSmsTemplate(name, data)` renders from a large built-in `templates` map — bookingConfirmation, paymentReceived, agencySignupOwner/AdminAlert, subscriptionActivated/Expiring(+Admin)/Expired(+Bn), trialExpired(+Bn/+Admin), subscriptionOrderAlert/Confirmation, passwordResetOtp, invoiceReminder, trialDripDay1/Day2/Last (each with `*Bn` Bengali variant), passportExpiryAlert(+Bn), travelDepartureReminder(+Bn). Currency rendered as `৳`.
- **Per-tenant gating** via `SmsSettings` table (`smsEnabled`, `notifyOnBooking`, `notifyOnPayment`, `notifyOnLead`) — checked in `tenantAutomationService.js` / `notificationAutomation.js`, not in the send function itself.
- Routes `routes/sms.js`: `GET/PUT /config` (super-admin, env-derived — **rejects secret writes**), `GET /balance`, `POST /send` (tenant_owner/manager/super_admin), `POST /send-bulk`, `POST /test`, `GET /logs` (+`/stats`,`/:id`), template CRUD (`/templates` …).

### 1.4 WhatsApp — `services/whatsappService.js`

- `sendWhatsApp({ to, message, templateName, templateParams, tenantId, templateType, createdByUserId })` dispatches on `WHATSAPP_PROVIDER` (`twilio` | `meta` | `wasender` | `console` default).
- **Per-tenant monthly quota** enforced *before* every send via `_withinWhatsAppQuota(tenantId)`: reads `getPlanLimit(plan,"whatsapp")` from `planFeatures.js` (`basic:0`, `pro:200`, `business:-1` unlimited, `enterprise:-1`), counts month-to-date `WhatsAppLog` rows with `status:"sent"`. `-1`=unlimited, `0`/undefined=blocked. **Fail-open** on counter error.
- Every send is persisted to **`WhatsAppLog`** (`_saveLog`): phone, message, status (`sent`/`failed`), provider, error, providerMessageId, templateType, createdByUserId.
- **WasenderAPI** (`sendViaWasender`): POST `wasenderapi.com/api/send-message` (Bearer `WASENDER_API_KEY`); auto-normalizes to `8801XXXXXXXXX`; treats `retry_after` rate-limit as non-fatal.
- **Meta Cloud API** (`sendViaMetaWhatsApp`): POST `graph.facebook.com/v18.0/{PHONE_ID}/messages` (Bearer `META_WHATSAPP_TOKEN`); supports both free-text and named-template messages (`language:{code:"en"}`, body params).
- **Twilio WhatsApp** (`sendViaTwilioWhatsApp`): same Twilio Messages API, `From` defaults `whatsapp:+14155238886`.
- `getWhatsAppConfig()` / `isWhatsAppConfigured(provider)` return provider + `configured` flag with no secrets.
- **Templates** — `services/whatsappTemplateService.js`: `getWhatsAppTemplate(type, data, {useBn})` prefers a DB `WhatsAppTemplate` (global `tenantId:null`, active) and falls back to a `FALLBACK_TEMPLATES` map (subscriptionRenewal(+Bn), subscriptionExpiring, paymentReminder, trialDripLast, passportExpiryAlert(+Bn), travelDepartureReminder(+Bn)). `renderMessage` interpolates `{{var}}` placeholders; `metaTemplateName` passed through for Meta-approved templates. Bengali selected by `*Bn` key.
- Routes `routes/whatsapp.js` (all gated by `requireFeature("hasWhatsApp")`): `GET /config` (adds live `{quota:{limit,used,unlimited}}`), `POST /test`, template CRUD, `GET /logs`, `POST /send`.

### 1.5 Email / SMTP — `services/emailService.js`

- Singleton **Nodemailer** transporter created lazily from `SMTP_HOST/PORT/SECURE/USER/PASS`. `sendEmail({to,subject,html,text})` returns `true` if sent, `false` if only console-logged (no SMTP).
- Pre-built templates: `sendPasswordReset`, `sendEmailVerification` (24h link, references configured trial days), `sendDemoRequestConfirmation`/`Notification`, `sendContactConfirmation`/`Notification`, `sendBookingConfirmation`, `sendInvoiceEmail`. Admin-notification emails go to `ADMIN_NOTIFICATION_EMAIL`. `from` = `SMTP_FROM` (default `noreply@travelagencyweb.com`).
- `testSmtpConnection(config)` verifies a connection (`transporter.verify()`) for the settings UI.
- Routes `routes/email.js`: `GET /config` (env-derived, **never returns password**), `POST /test`, `POST /send/booking-confirmation`, `POST /send/invoice`.

### 1.6 Telegram — `services/telegramService.js`

- Admin-alert channel. `sendTelegramMessage(text, {chatId})` POSTs to `api.telegram.org/bot{TOKEN}/sendMessage` (`parse_mode: "HTML"`, previews disabled); skips silently if `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`/`TELEGRAM_ADMIN_CHAT_ID` unset. HTML-escapes user data via `esc()`.
- `notifyNewSignup({name,email,phone,tenantName,userId,plan})` — sends a formatted new-agency-signup alert to the admin chat **and bridges** into the multi-channel `notifyEvent("agency_signup", …)`.
- `notifyUserApproved({name,email})` — user-approved alert.

### 1.7 Notification Orchestration — `services/notificationService.js`

Central fan-out: `notifyEvent(event, data)` renders per-event content and dispatches across **email + SMS + WhatsApp + in-app**, composing `emailService`, `smsService`, `whatsappService`, and `whatsappTemplateService`. Events include `payment_received`, `subscription_activated`, `agency_signup`, subscription expiry/renewal (with Bengali variants), trial-drip, passport-expiry, travel-departure, plus super-admin alert emails/SMS (`SUPER_ADMIN_ALERT_EMAIL`/`SUPER_ADMIN_ALERT_PHONE`). Owner contact resolved via `lib/tenantOwnerContact.js`. This is the layer that reads `SmsSettings` / `NotificationAutomation` toggles to decide which channels fire.

---

## Part 2 — Frontend Architecture

Stack: **React 18 + Vite + TypeScript**, React Router v6, TanStack Query, react-i18next, shadcn/ui (Radix) + Sonner/Toaster. Path alias `@/` → `src/`.

### 2.1 Provider Tree — `src/App.tsx`

Nesting (outer → inner), assembled in `AppContent`:

1. **`BrowserRouter`** (`AppWithRouter`) — only added if not already inside a router context (`useInRouterContext` guard, so `App` is embeddable).
2. **`QueryClientProvider`** — one module-level `new QueryClient()` (default options).
3. **`TooltipProvider`** (Radix).
4. **`AuthProvider`** — the app-wide auth/tenant/plan/role context.
5. **`<Toaster/>` + `<Sonner/>`** — dual toast systems mounted as siblings of `<Routes>`.
6. **`WebsiteProvider`** — *not* global; wrapped only around public tenant-site routes (`/site*`, tenant-host `/`, `/about`, `/packages`, `/blog`, `/contact`).

**Route guards** (composable wrappers):
- `P` = `<ProtectedRoute>` (authenticated).
- `PM` = `<ProtectedRoute><PermissionRoute module=…>` (authenticated + RBAC module check).
- `A` = `<AdminRoute>` (super-admin only).
- Plus `SubscriptionRoute`, `HajjModuleGate`, `BdModuleGate`, `BookingSegmentRoute` for finer gating.

**Route surface**: ~90 routes across four zones — public marketing (`/`, `/features`, `/pricing`, `/demo`, `/faq`, `/privacy`, `/terms`), auth (`/login`, `/register`, `/forgot-password`, `/reset-password`, `/verify-email`, `/onboarding`), public tenant sites (`/site*`, `/site/:slug*`), the authenticated ERP app (dashboard, CRM, clients, agents, vendors, leads, quotations, bookings, invoices, accounts, reports, hajj-umrah, operations, website builder, HRM/payroll, settings, subscription, loyalty/referrals, MICE, visa-tracker, inventory, recruitment, ticket-transactions, flight-reminders), and `/admin/*` (super-admin). Heavier pages (`SettingsTax`, `FinancialStatements`, `Payroll`, `WebsiteSeo`, `Loyalty`, `Referrals`, `SalesAnalytics`, `GroupTours`, `MiceEvents`, `TravelApprovals`, `VisaTracker`, `Inventory`, `Recruitment`, `TicketTransactions`, `FlightReminders`) are **`lazy()`-loaded** behind `<Suspense>` with a `Skeleton` fallback.

### 2.2 Contexts — `src/contexts/`

**`AuthContext.tsx`** (`AuthProvider`, `useAuth`) — single source of truth for identity/plan/subscription. Exposes:

| Value | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `user`, `tenant` | current `User` / `Tenant` objects |
| `currentPlan` | `tenant.subscriptionPlan` or `"free"` |
| `appRole` | RBAC role via `mapLegacyRole(user.role)` (`sales_agent` default) |
| `isTrialActive`, `trialDaysLeft` | derived from `subscriptionStatus==="trial"` + expiry |
| `isSubscriptionBlocked` / `isSubscriptionExpired` | from `getSubscriptionBlockState`; **super_admin never blocked** |
| `subscriptionBlockReason` | typed reason |
| `loading` | bootstrap flag |
| `login(email,password,totpCode?)` | throws `2FA_REQUIRED` if `requires2FA`; stores JWT in `localStorage`, fetches tenant |
| `register(data)` | instant-token Pro-trial flow (falls back to pending-approval on legacy backend) |
| `logout()` | fire-and-forget audit log, clears token + state |
| `refreshTenant()` | re-fetch `tenantApi.get()` |

Bootstrap `useEffect`: reads `localStorage.token`; runs `authApi.me()` + `tenantApi.get()` in parallel with a **12s safety timeout** to release `loading`; clears token on `me()` failure.

**`WebsiteContext.tsx`** (`WebsiteProvider`, `useWebsite`) — powers the public agency websites. Resolves the hostname synchronously (`resolveHostname()`), then loads `{tenant, packages, websiteConfig}` in parallel via `publicApi` + `websiteApi` (by slug or by custom domain). **Gates first paint** for real tenant sites (spinner until loaded, so no demo-flash); renders `<DomainErrorPage>` on resolution failure. Ships built-in `demoTenant` + `demoPackages` fallback for the marketing app. Provides `domainResolution`.

### 2.3 Custom Hooks — `src/hooks/`

| Hook | File | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `useAuth` | (contexts/AuthContext) | consume AuthContext (throws if outside provider) |
| `useWebsite` | (contexts/WebsiteContext) | consume public-site context |
| `usePermissions` | `usePermissions.ts` | RBAC helper off `useAuth`: `can(module,action)`, `canAccess`, `modulePerms`, `accessibleModules`, plus role booleans (`isSuperAdmin`, `isTenantOwner`, `isManager`, `isSalesAgent`, `isAccountant`, `isOperations`) and `canManageTeam/Subscription/AccessAdmin`. Delegates to `lib/permissions.ts` |
| `usePlanAccess` | `usePlanAccess.ts` | plan-feature gate off `getPlan(plan)`: exposes limits (`maxClients/Bookings/Users/Domains/SmsPerMonth/StorageMB/Reports`) and `canUse*` feature flags (payment gateways, SSLCommerz, bKash, custom domain, website templates, SMS, WhatsApp, email, agent commission, analytics, marketing, API, refund, Hajj/Umrah); helpers `hasFeature`, `requiresUpgrade`, `getUpgradePlan` |
| `useGatewayStatus` | `useGatewayStatus.ts` | fetches `paymentGatewayApi.gatewayStatus()`; returns `{status, loading, hasOnlineGateway}` (bKash/SSLCommerz configured) |
| `useConfiguredTrialDays` | `useConfiguredTrialDays.ts` | fetches signup trial length from API health (`fetchConfiguredTrialDays`, `DEFAULT_TRIAL_DAYS` fallback) |
| `useSubscriptionPayment` | `useSubscriptionPayment.ts` | drives the whole plan-purchase flow: loads payment methods + prior requests (`tenantSubscriptionWorkflowApi`), manages the request dialog/form, coupon validation (`subscriptionCouponApi`), payment-proof upload, create/resubmit/cancel; exports `nextSuggestedPlan()` |
| `useHajjData` | `useHajjData.ts` | loads/manages Hajj packages+groups+pilgrims (`hajjApi`); computes `summary` + `pkgProfitability`; save/delete mutations with optimistic state |
| `useHajjPilgrim` | `useHajjPilgrim.ts` | Hajj pilgrim table: filters, pagination, pilgrim + payment forms, CRUD |
| `useHumanError` | `useHumanError.ts` | i18n-aware error formatting (`formatError`, `errorTitle` via `lib/humanErrors` + `react-i18next`) |
| `usePermissions`… | | (listed above) |
| `useToast` / `toast` | `use-toast.ts` | shadcn toast store: external reducer + listener pattern, `TOAST_LIMIT=1`, `ADD/UPDATE/DISMISS/REMOVE_TOAST` actions |
| `useIsMobile` | `use-mobile.tsx` | `matchMedia` breakpoint hook (`< 768px`) |

### 2.4 API Layer — `src/lib/api.ts` (~1500 lines) + companion `lib/*Api.ts` modules

**Core client** (top of `api.ts`):
- `BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "http://localhost:4000/api"`.
- `request<T>(path, options)` — the single fetch wrapper: injects `Content-Type: application/json` + `Authorization: Bearer <localStorage.token>`, parses JSON, and on non-2xx throws `new Error(parsed.message || parsed.error || statusText)` (guards against HTML bodies). All auth is stateless JWT in `localStorage`.
- `createCrudApi<T>(resource)` — factory yielding `{list, get, create, update(PATCH), delete}` for a REST resource. Used directly for `taskApi`, `paymentApi`, `transactionApi`, `subscriptionApi`, `paymentRequestApi`.

**Domain API objects** exported from `api.ts` (each a namespaced object of typed `request()` calls): `authApi` (login/2FA-TOTP/register/checkEmail/WhatsApp-OTP/me/forgot+reset password/verify+resend email/logout), `tenantApi` (me + members CRUD), `dashboardApi`, `clientApi`, `agentApi`, `visaStockApi`, `vendorApi`, `complaintApi`, `campaignApi`, `crmAnalyticsApi`, `crmSettingsApi`, `leadApi`, `documentHubApi`, `taskApi`, `supportTicketApi`, `blogApi`, `hrmApi`, `bookingApi`, `invoiceApi`, `financeApi`, `taxRuleApi`, `payrollApi`, `paymentApi`, `accountApi`, `transactionApi`, `expenseApi`, `subscriptionApi`, `paymentRequestApi`, `quotationApi`, `adminApi`, `domainApi`, `loyaltyApi`, `referralApi`, `groupTourApi`, `analyticsApi`, `miceApi`, `travelPolicyApi`, `visaApi`, `inventoryApi`, `recruitmentApi`. It also exports the full **domain type layer** (`User`, `Tenant`, `Client`, `Booking`, `Invoice`, `Payment`, `Quotation`, `Lead`, `Vendor`, `Agent`, plus stats/report/enum types).

**Companion API modules** (same `request`-style pattern, split by domain) in `src/lib/`: `adminNotificationApi`, `auditLogApi`, `automationApi`, `domainApi`, `emailApi`, `exportApi`, `hajjApi`, `masterDataApi`, `notificationApi`, `paymentGatewayApi`, `platformAdminApi`, `platformHealthApi`, `portalApi`, `publicApi`, `smsApi`, `smsTemplateApi`, `subscriptionWorkflowApi`, `tenantDomainApi`, `travelPackageApi`, `websiteApi`, `websiteCmsApi`, `whatsappLogApi`. Non-API support libs of note: `permissions.ts` (RBAC matrix), `plans.ts` (plan configs/limits/pricing), `moduleAccess.ts`/`features.ts` (module gating), `domainResolver.ts` (tenant-from-hostname), `serviceCatalog.ts`, `notificationEngine.ts`.

**`domainResolver.ts`** — multi-tenant hostname routing used by `WebsiteContext`/`App`. `resolveHostname()` classifies the host into `main-app` | `slug` (`{slug}.APP_DOMAIN` wildcard) | `custom-domain` | `portal`; `RESERVED_SUBDOMAINS = [app, portal, api, www, admin]`; localhost/`127.0.0.1`/no `VITE_APP_DOMAIN` → main-app. Helpers: `getReservedSubdomain`, `isPortalHost`, `getSubdomainUrl`, `extractSlugFromHostname`, `fetchTenantByDomain`, `resolveTenant`.

**Notable API-layer conventions**: state is JWT-in-localStorage (no cookies); TanStack Query wraps these `request()` calls in components; all mutating calls use `PATCH` for updates (per the CRUD factory); errors surface as `Error.message` for `useHumanError`/toasts to render.

Relevant files: `backend/src/services/{paymentGateway,emailService,smsService,whatsappService,telegramService,whatsappTemplateService,notificationService}.js`, `backend/src/lib/paymentGatewayConfig.js`, `backend/src/lib/planFeatures.js`, `backend/src/routes/{bkash,sslcommerz,payments,sms,whatsapp,email}.js`, `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` (models `PaymentMethodConfig`, `SmsSettings`, `WhatsAppLog`, `NotificationAutomation`); `src/contexts/{AuthContext,WebsiteContext}.tsx`, `src/hooks/*`, `src/lib/api.ts`, `src/lib/domainResolver.ts`, `src/App.tsx`.
